' ============================================
' ДИАЛОГИ ПОВЕРХ ВСЕХ ОКОН
' ============================================

Private Function TopMsg(text As String, title As String, _
    Optional icon As Long = MB_ICONINFORMATION) As Long
    TopMsg = MessageBoxW(0, StrPtr(text), StrPtr(title), _
        MB_OK Or icon Or MB_TOPMOST)
End Function

Private Function TopYesNo(text As String, title As String) As Boolean
    Dim result As Long
    result = MessageBoxW(0, StrPtr(text), StrPtr(title), _
        MB_YESNO Or MB_ICONQUESTION Or MB_TOPMOST)
    TopYesNo = (result = IDYES)
End Function

Private Function TopYesNoCancel(text As String, title As String) As Long
    TopYesNoCancel = MessageBoxW(0, StrPtr(text), StrPtr(title), _
        MB_YESNOCANCEL Or MB_ICONQUESTION Or MB_TOPMOST)
End Function

' ============================================
' ПРОВЕРКА КОРОТКИХ ПРЕДЛОГОВ И СОЮЗОВ
' ============================================

Private Function IsPreposition(ByVal w As String) As Boolean
    w = LCase(Trim(w))
    Select Case w
        Case "в", "во", "и", "с", "со", "к", "ко", "о", "об", "у", "на", "по", "за", "из", "под", "над", "при", "от", "до", "для"
            IsPreposition = True
        Case Else
            IsPreposition = False
    End Select
End Function

' ============================================
' РАЗБИЕНИЕ ТЕКСТА ПО СЛОВАМ И ПЕРЕНОСАМ
' ============================================

Private Function WrapTextByWords(ByVal text As String, ByVal maxLen As Long) As Variant
    text = Trim(text)
    If Len(text) = 0 Then
        WrapTextByWords = Array("")
        Exit Function
    End If

    ' Заменяем неразрывные пробелы
    text = Replace(text, Chr(160), " ")

    ' 1. Связываем числа с единицами измерений (например "75 мм")
    Dim units As Variant
    units = Array("мм", "см", "м", "шт", "шт.", "кг", "г", "°", "град", "град.")
    Dim u As Variant, pos As Long
    For Each u In units
        pos = 1
        Do
            pos = InStr(pos, text, " " & CStr(u), vbTextCompare)
            If pos > 1 Then
                Dim prevChar As String
                prevChar = Mid(text, pos - 1, 1)
                If IsNumeric(prevChar) Then
                    text = Left(text, pos - 1) & Chr(1) & Mid(text, pos + 1)
                End If
                pos = pos + Len(CStr(u)) + 1
            Else
                Exit Do
            End If
        Loop
    Next u

    ' 2. Связываем технологические термины (например "Разрез П-П", "Эскиз 1", "Вид А")
    Dim techTerms As Variant
    techTerms = Array("разрез", "сечение", "вид", "эскиз", "дет.", "поз.")
    Dim tTerm As Variant
    For Each tTerm In techTerms
        pos = 1
        Do
            pos = InStr(pos, text, CStr(tTerm) & " ", vbTextCompare)
            If pos > 0 Then
                Dim termEnd As Long
                termEnd = pos + Len(CStr(tTerm))
                ' Заменяем пробел сразу после термина на Chr(1)
                text = Left(text, termEnd - 1) & Chr(1) & Mid(text, termEnd + 1)
                pos = termEnd + 1
            Else
                Exit Do
            End If
        Loop
    Next tTerm

    ' Приводим все типы переводов строк к единому символу vbLf
    text = Replace(text, vbCrLf, vbLf)
    text = Replace(text, vbCr, vbLf)

    Dim rawParagraphs() As String
    rawParagraphs = Split(text, vbLf)

    Dim lines() As String
    Dim lineCount As Long
    lineCount = 0

    Dim pIdx As Long
    For pIdx = LBound(rawParagraphs) To UBound(rawParagraphs)
        Dim pText As String
        pText = Trim(rawParagraphs(pIdx))

        Do While InStr(1, pText, "  ") > 0
            pText = Replace(pText, "  ", " ")
        Loop

        If Len(pText) > 0 Then
            Dim words() As String
            words = Split(pText, " ")

            Dim curLine As String
            curLine = ""

            Dim i As Long
            For i = LBound(words) To UBound(words)
                Dim w As String
                w = words(i)

                If Len(w) > 0 Then
                    If Len(curLine) = 0 Then
                        curLine = w
                    Else
                        If Len(curLine & " " & w) <= maxLen Then
                            curLine = curLine & " " & w
                        Else
                            ' Проверяем висячие короткие предлоги
                            Dim curParts() As String
                            curParts = Split(curLine, " ")
                            Dim lastW As String
                            lastW = curParts(UBound(curParts))

                            If UBound(curParts) > 0 And IsPreposition(lastW) Then
                                Dim reassembled As String
                                reassembled = ""
                                Dim k As Long
                                For k = 0 To UBound(curParts) - 1
                                    If Len(reassembled) = 0 Then
                                        reassembled = curParts(k)
                                    Else
                                        reassembled = reassembled & " " & curParts(k)
                                    End If
                                Next k
                                lineCount = lineCount + 1
                                ReDim Preserve lines(1 To lineCount)
                                lines(lineCount) = Replace(reassembled, Chr(1), " ")
                                curLine = lastW & " " & w
                            Else
                                lineCount = lineCount + 1
                                ReDim Preserve lines(1 To lineCount)
                                lines(lineCount) = Replace(curLine, Chr(1), " ")
                                curLine = w
                            End If
                        End If
                    End If
                End If
            Next i

            If Len(curLine) > 0 Then
                lineCount = lineCount + 1
                ReDim Preserve lines(1 To lineCount)
                lines(lineCount) = Replace(curLine, Chr(1), " ")
            End If
        End If
    Next pIdx

    If lineCount = 0 Then
        WrapTextByWords = Array("")
    Else
        WrapTextByWords = lines
    End If
End Function

' ============================================
' ВЫБОР ФАЙЛА
' ============================================

Private Function ParentFolderOfFile(ByVal filePath As String) As String
    Dim p As Long
    p = InStrRev(filePath, "\")
    If p > 0 Then ParentFolderOfFile = Left$(filePath, p)
End Function

Private Function BrowseForFile(swApp As Object, _
    fileFilter As String, dialogTitle As String, _
    Optional initialFolder As String = "") As String

    Dim tempXL As Object, dlg As Object, filters As Variant, i As Long
    Dim result As Variant, errorText As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    Set tempXL = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
    tempXL.Visible = False
    If Len(initialFolder) = 0 Then
        result = tempXL.GetOpenFileName(fileFilter, 1, dialogTitle, , False)
        If VarType(result) = vbString Then BrowseForFile = CStr(result)
    Else
        ' msoFileDialogFilePicker = 3. InitialFileName задаёт папку именно этого окна.
        Set dlg = tempXL.FileDialog(3)
        dlg.title = dialogTitle
        dlg.AllowMultiSelect = False
        dlg.filters.Clear
        filters = Split(fileFilter, ",")
        For i = LBound(filters) To UBound(filters) - 1 Step 2
            dlg.filters.Add CStr(filters(i)), CStr(filters(i + 1))
        Next i
        If Right$(initialFolder, 1) <> "\" Then initialFolder = initialFolder & "\"
        dlg.InitialFileName = initialFolder
        If dlg.Show = -1 Then BrowseForFile = CStr(dlg.SelectedItems(1))
    End If
CleanExit:
    On Error Resume Next
    Set dlg = Nothing
    If Not tempXL Is Nothing Then tempXL.Quit
    Set tempXL = Nothing
    On Error GoTo 0
    If Len(errorText) > 0 Then TopMsg errorText, "Выбор файла", MB_ICONEXCLAMATION
    Exit Function
Failed:
    errorText = "Не удалось открыть диалог выбора файла: " & CStr(Err.number) & ": " & Err.description
    Resume CleanExit
End Function

' =========================================================
' ПЕРЕМЕЩЕНИЕ ЛИСТОВ В НУЖНУЮ ПОЗИЦИЮ ЧЕРТЕЖА
' =========================================================

Private Function MoveSheetsAfterTarget(swDraw As Object, _
    ByVal targetSheetName As String, _
    createdSheets() As String) As Boolean

    MoveSheetsAfterTarget = False
    targetSheetName = Trim(targetSheetName)
    If Len(targetSheetName) = 0 Then Exit Function

    Dim allSheets As Variant
    allSheets = swDraw.GetSheetNames
    If IsEmpty(allSheets) Then Exit Function

    Dim totalCount As Long
    totalCount = UBound(allSheets) - LBound(allSheets) + 1

    Dim targetFound As Boolean: targetFound = False
    Dim exactTargetName As String: exactTargetName = ""
    Dim i As Long

    For i = LBound(allSheets) To UBound(allSheets)
        If StrComp(Trim(CStr(allSheets(i))), targetSheetName, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
            targetFound = True
            exactTargetName = CStr(allSheets(i))
            Exit For
        End If
    Next i

    If Not targetFound Then
        TopMsg "Лист '" & targetSheetName & "' не найден в чертеже! Листы останутся в конце.", "Внимание", MB_ICONEXCLAMATION
        Exit Function
    End If

    ' Собираем полный список всех листов в новом порядке
    Dim newSheetOrder() As String
    ReDim newSheetOrder(0 To totalCount - 1)
    Dim ordIdx As Long: ordIdx = 0

    Dim sName As String
    Dim isNew As Boolean
    Dim c As Long

    For i = LBound(allSheets) To UBound(allSheets)
        sName = CStr(allSheets(i))

        isNew = False
        For c = LBound(createdSheets) To UBound(createdSheets)
            If StrComp(sName, createdSheets(c), vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
                isNew = True
                Exit For
            End If
        Next c

        If Not isNew Then
            newSheetOrder(ordIdx) = sName
            ordIdx = ordIdx + 1

            ' Сразу после целевого листа подставляем наши созданные листы
            If StrComp(sName, exactTargetName, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
                For c = LBound(createdSheets) To UBound(createdSheets)
                    newSheetOrder(ordIdx) = createdSheets(c)
                    ordIdx = ordIdx + 1
                Next c
            End If
        End If
    Next i

    Dim vOrder As Variant
    vOrder = newSheetOrder

    Dim ok As Boolean
    On Error Resume Next
    ok = swDraw.ReorderSheets(vOrder)
    On Error GoTo 0

    MoveSheetsAfterTarget = ok
End Function


' ============================================
' РАЗБОР ИМЕНИ ФАЙЛА-ПРИЕМНИКА
' ============================================

Private Function CleanFileStem(ByVal filePath As String) As String
    Dim f As String, p As Long
    f = Mid$(filePath, InStrRev(filePath, "\") + 1)
    p = InStrRev(f, ".")
    If p > 1 Then f = Left$(f, p - 1)
    CleanFileStem = f
End Function



Private Function BuildShiftedCode(ByVal sourceNumber As String, ByVal code As String) As String
    Dim p1 As Long, p2 As Long, prefix As String, digits As String, prefixLength As Long
    sourceNumber = NormalizePartDesignation(sourceNumber)
    If Len(sourceNumber) = 0 Then Exit Function
    p1 = InStr(1, sourceNumber, ".")
    If p1 > 0 Then
        p2 = InStr(p1 + 1, sourceNumber, ".")
        prefix = Left$(sourceNumber, p1 - 1)
        digits = Mid$(sourceNumber, p1 + 1, p2 - p1 - 1) & Replace(Mid$(sourceNumber, p2 + 1), "-", "")
    Else
        prefixLength = CLng(KC28_N("Codes.CompactPrefixLength"))
        p2 = InStr(1, sourceNumber, "-")
        If p2 = 0 Then p2 = Len(sourceNumber) + 1
        If p2 - 1 <= prefixLength Then Exit Function
        prefix = Left$(sourceNumber, prefixLength)
        digits = Replace(Mid$(sourceNumber, prefixLength + 1), "-", "")
    End If
    If Len(digits) < 5 Then Exit Function
    BuildShiftedCode = prefix & "." & code & "." & Right$(digits, 5)
End Function

Private Function BuildPrefixedCode(ByVal sourceNumber As String, ByVal code As String) As String
    Dim p As Long
    p = InStr(1, sourceNumber, ".")
    If p <= 1 Then Exit Function
    BuildPrefixedCode = Left$(sourceNumber, p - 1) & "." & code & "." & _
                        Mid$(sourceNumber, p + 1)
End Function

Private Function UniqueFrameDisplayName(ByVal swModel As Object, ByVal requested As String, _
    ByVal feat As Object) As String
    Dim candidate As String, n As Long
    EnsureFeatureNameCache swModel
    If StrComp(feat.name, requested, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
        UniqueFrameDisplayName = feat.name
        Exit Function
    End If
    candidate = requested
    n = 2
    Do While frameAttributeNames.Exists(candidate)
        candidate = requested & "_" & CStr(n)
        n = n + 1
        If n > 1000000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2353, , "Не найдено свободное имя таблицы рамки."
    Loop
    frameAttributeNames.Add candidate, True
    UniqueFrameDisplayName = candidate
End Function

Private Sub SetTableName(ByVal tbl As Object, ByVal tableName As String)
    Dim feat As Object, actualName As String, model As Object, app As Object
    If Len(tableName) = 0 Then Exit Sub
    On Error GoTo Failed
    Set feat = GetFrameTableFeature(tbl)
    actualName = tableName
    If Left$(tableName, 3) = "MF_" Then
        Set app = Application.SldWorks
        Set model = app.ActiveDoc
        actualName = UniqueFrameDisplayName(model, tableName, feat)
    End If
    ' «Таблица данныхN» и «Время операций» остаются строгими именами.
    feat.name = actualName
    If StrComp(feat.name, actualName, vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2352, , "Запрошенное имя не присвоено (возможно, занято)."
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    AddMacroWarning "Имя таблицы '" & tableName & "': " & CStr(Err.number) & ": " & Err.description
End Sub

Private Sub AddMacroWarning(ByVal message As String)
    Debug.Print message
    ' Ограничиваем длину окна, полный список остаётся в Immediate Window.
    If Len(macroWarnings) < 2500 Then
        macroWarnings = macroWarnings & message & vbCrLf
    End If
End Sub

Private Function ActivateSheetForMacro(ByVal swDraw As Object, _
    ByVal sheetName As String) As Boolean
    Dim actualSheet As Object
    On Error GoTo Failed
    If Not swDraw.ActivateSheet(sheetName) Then GoTo Failed
    Set actualSheet = swDraw.GetCurrentSheet
    If actualSheet Is Nothing Then GoTo Failed
    If StrComp(actualSheet.GetName, sheetName, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then GoTo Failed
    ActivateSheetForMacro = True
    Exit Function
Failed:
    AddMacroWarning "Не удалось активировать лист: " & sheetName
End Function

Private Function ApplyMacroTableFont(ByVal tbl As Object) As Boolean
    Dim fmt As Object, cellFmt As Object
    Dim r As Long, c As Long, ok As Boolean
    On Error GoTo Failed
    Set fmt = tbl.GetTextFormat
    If fmt Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2101, , "Нет формата таблицы."
    ' CharHeight в API задаётся в метрах: 3,5 мм = 0,0035 м.
    ' Название шрифта, наклон и начертание сохраняем.
    fmt.CharHeight = MACRO_TEXT_HEIGHT_MM / 1000#
    ok = tbl.SetTextFormat(False, fmt)
    If Not ok Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2102, , "Не задан формат таблицы."

    ' Ячейки тоже переводим на независимый формат, сохраняя их начертание.
    For r = 0 To tbl.rowCount - 1
        For c = 0 To tbl.ColumnCount - 1
            Set cellFmt = tbl.GetCellTextFormat(r, c)
            If cellFmt Is Nothing Then Set cellFmt = tbl.GetTextFormat
            cellFmt.CharHeight = MACRO_TEXT_HEIGHT_MM / 1000#
            ok = tbl.SetCellTextFormat(r, c, False, cellFmt)
            If Not ok Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2103, , _
                "Не задан формат ячейки " & CStr(r) & ", " & CStr(c)
        Next c
    Next r
    ApplyMacroTableFont = True
    Exit Function
Failed:
    AddMacroWarning "Шрифт таблицы: " & Err.description
End Function

Private Function FrameCellName(ByVal swModel As Object, _
    ByVal xMm As Double, ByVal yMm As Double) As String
    Dim sh As Object
    Set sh = swModel.GetCurrentSheet
    FrameCellName = "MF_" & sh.GetName & "_" & _
        CStr(CLng(xMm * 1000#)) & "_" & CStr(CLng(yMm * 1000#))
End Function

Private Function FrameTableFeatureName(ByVal tbl As Object) As String
    Dim gt As Object, ft As Object
    Set gt = tbl.GeneralTableFeature
    If gt Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    Set ft = gt.GetFeature
    If Not ft Is Nothing Then FrameTableFeatureName = ft.name
End Function

Private Function GetFrameAttributeDef() As Object
    Dim app As Object, added As Boolean
    If frameAttributeDef Is Nothing Then
        Set app = Application.SldWorks
        Set frameAttributeDef = app.DefineAttribute(FRAME_ATTR_DEF)
        If frameAttributeDef Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2200, , _
            "Не получено определение служебной маркировки."

        ' DefineAttribute подключается и к уже существующему определению.
        ' У зарегистрированного определения параметры менять нельзя.
        On Error Resume Next
        added = frameAttributeDef.AddParameter(FRAME_ATTR_KEY, 1, 0#, 0)
        Err.Clear
        On Error GoTo 0
        ' 1 = swParamTypeString. Регистрируем только вновь добавленную схему.
        If added Then
            If Not frameAttributeDef.Register Then
                Set frameAttributeDef = Nothing
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2201, , "Не зарегистрирована служебная маркировка."
            End If
        End If
    End If
    Set GetFrameAttributeDef = frameAttributeDef
End Function

Private Function FrameFieldKey(ByVal xMm As Double, ByVal yMm As Double, Optional ByVal fieldName As String = "") As String
    ' Ключ хранится в атрибуте, а не вычисляется из текущей позиции таблицы.
    ' Поиск выполняется только на текущем листе, поэтому имя листа в ключе не нужно.
    If Left$(fieldName, 9) = "MF_FIELD_" Then
        FrameFieldKey = "Field_" & Mid$(fieldName, 10)
        Exit Function
    End If
    FrameFieldKey = "Cell_" & CStr(CLng(xMm * 1000#)) & "_" & CStr(CLng(yMm * 1000#))
End Function

Private Function GetFrameTableFeature(ByVal tbl As Object) As Object
    Dim gt As Object
    Set gt = tbl.GeneralTableFeature
    If gt Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2202, , "Это не общая таблица."
    Set GetFrameTableFeature = gt.GetFeature
    If GetFrameTableFeature Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2203, , _
        "Не получен Feature общей таблицы."
End Function

Private Function GetFrameTableAttribute(ByVal tbl As Object) As Object
    ' Entity нужен для QueryInterface: FindAttribute нет у интерфейса Feature.
    ' SldWorks — стандартная библиотека типов макроса SOLIDWORKS.
    Dim entity As SldWorks.entity
    Dim feat As Object, attDef As Object, att As Object
    Dim stage As String, number As Long, description As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    stage = "GeneralTableFeature.GetFeature"
    Set feat = GetFrameTableFeature(tbl)
    stage = "Feature -> Entity"
    Set entity = feat
    stage = "DefineAttribute"
    Set attDef = GetFrameAttributeDef()
    stage = "Entity.FindAttribute"
    Set att = entity.FindAttribute(attDef, 0)
    stage = "Attribute.GetEntityState"
    If Not att Is Nothing Then
        ' 0 = swIsEntityInvalid, 2 = swIsEntityAmbiguous.
        If att.GetEntityState(0) Or att.GetEntityState(2) Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2204, , "Повреждённая или неоднозначная маркировка таблицы."
    End If
    Set GetFrameTableAttribute = att
    Exit Function
Failed:
    number = Err.number
    description = Err.description
    Err.Raise number, "GetFrameTableAttribute", "[" & stage & "] " & description
End Function

Private Function ReadFrameTableKey(ByVal tbl As Object) As String
    Dim att As Object, param As Object
    Set att = GetFrameTableAttribute(tbl)
    If att Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    Set param = att.GetParameter(FRAME_ATTR_KEY)
    If param Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2205, , _
        "У маркировки отсутствует параметр FieldKey."
    ReadFrameTableKey = CStr(param.GetStringValue())
End Function

Private Sub AddFeatureTreeNames(ByVal feat As Object, ByVal names As Object, ByVal depth As Long)
    Dim child As Object, key As String
    If depth > 100 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2211, , "Слишком большая глубина дерева Feature."
    key = CStr(feat.name)
    If names.Exists(key) Then Exit Sub
    names.Add key, True
    Set child = feat.GetFirstSubFeature
    Do While Not child Is Nothing
        AddFeatureTreeNames child, names, depth + 1
        Set child = child.GetNextSubFeature
    Loop
End Sub

Private Sub EnsureFeatureNameCache(ByVal swModel As Object)
    Dim feat As Object
    If Not frameAttributeNames Is Nothing Then Exit Sub
    Set frameAttributeNames = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    frameAttributeNames.CompareMode = vbTextCompare
    Set feat = swModel.FirstFeature
    Do While Not feat Is Nothing
        AddFeatureTreeNames feat, frameAttributeNames, 0
        Set feat = feat.GetNextFeature
    Loop
End Sub

Private Function UniqueFrameAttributeName(ByVal swModel As Object) As String
    Dim n As Long, candidate As String
    EnsureFeatureNameCache swModel
    n = 1
    Do
        candidate = "MF_CellTag_" & CStr(n)
        If Not frameAttributeNames.Exists(candidate) Then Exit Do
        n = n + 1
        If n > 1000000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2206, , "Не найдено свободное имя атрибута."
    Loop
    frameAttributeNames.Add candidate, True
    UniqueFrameAttributeName = candidate
End Function

Private Function TryMarkFrameTable(ByVal swModel As Object, ByVal tbl As Object, _
    ByVal fieldKey As String) As Boolean
    Dim errorNumber As Long, errorDescription As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    MarkFrameTable swModel, tbl, fieldKey
    TryMarkFrameTable = True
    Exit Function
Failed:
    errorNumber = Err.number
    errorDescription = Err.description
    AddMacroWarning "Поле " & fieldKey & ": текст заполнен, но маркировка не записана. Ошибка " & _
        CStr(errorNumber) & ": " & errorDescription & ". Дубли этого поля не удалялись."
End Function

Private Sub MarkFrameTable(ByVal swModel As Object, ByVal tbl As Object, ByVal fieldKey As String)
    Dim att As Object, attDef As Object, param As Object, feat As Object, currentKey As String
    Set att = GetFrameTableAttribute(tbl)
    If att Is Nothing Then
        Set attDef = GetFrameAttributeDef()
        Set feat = GetFrameTableFeature(tbl)
        ' 1 = скрытый атрибут в дереве, 2 = swAllConfiguration (для чертежа).
        Set att = attDef.CreateInstance5(swModel, feat, UniqueFrameAttributeName(swModel), 1, 2)
        If att Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2207, , _
            "Не создана постоянная маркировка таблицы."
    End If
    Set param = att.GetParameter(FRAME_ATTR_KEY)
    If param Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2205, , "Нет параметра FieldKey."
    currentKey = CStr(param.GetStringValue())
    If Len(currentKey) > 0 And StrComp(currentKey, fieldKey, vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then
        ' Переход с координатного ключа разрешён только для найденного поля КЭ.
        If Not KC34_IsKEAlias(currentKey, fieldKey) Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2208, , "Таблица уже помечена как другое поле."
    End If
    If Not param.SetStringValue2(fieldKey, 2, "") Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2209, , "Не записана маркировка таблицы."
    If StrComp(ReadFrameTableKey(tbl), fieldKey, vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2210, , "Не подтверждено чтение записанной маркировки."
End Sub

Private Function CollectFrameCells(ByVal swModel As Object, _
    ByVal xMm As Double, ByVal yMm As Double, ByVal wMm As Double, _
    ByVal legacyName As String, ByRef preferred As Object, _
    ByRef hasLegacy As Boolean) As Collection

    Dim result As New Collection
    Dim v As Object, tables As Variant, i As Long, firstTable As Long, lastTable As Long
    Dim t As Object, gt As Object, ann As Object, pos As Variant
    Dim nm As String, marker As String, wanted As String, oldName As String
    Dim rank As Long, bestRank As Long, byPosition As Boolean
    Dim existing As Variant, alreadyAdded As Boolean

    wanted = FrameFieldKey(xMm, yMm, legacyName)
    oldName = FrameCellName(swModel, xMm, yMm)
    Set preferred = Nothing
    hasLegacy = False
    Set v = swModel.GetFirstView
    Do While Not v Is Nothing
        tables = v.GetTableAnnotations
        If KC15_TryArrayBounds(tables, firstTable, lastTable) Then
            For i = firstTable To lastTable
                Set t = tables(i)
                If t.rowCount = 1 And t.ColumnCount = 1 Then
                    Set gt = t.GeneralTableFeature
                    If Not gt Is Nothing Then
                        marker = ReadFrameTableKey(t)
                        rank = 0
                        If Len(marker) > 0 Then
                            ' Чужую маркировку не переопределяем по координатам.
                            If KC34_FrameKeyMatches(marker, wanted, xMm, yMm) Then rank = 3
                        Else
                            nm = FrameTableFeatureName(t)
                            If StrComp(nm, oldName, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then rank = 2
                            If KC34_LegacyFrameName(nm, xMm, yMm) Then rank = 2
                            If KC34_StableFrameName(nm, legacyName) Then rank = 2
                            If Len(legacyName) > 0 Then
                                If StrComp(nm, legacyName, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then rank = 2
                            End If
                            If rank = 0 Then
                                Set ann = t.GetAnnotation
                                If Not ann Is Nothing Then
                                    pos = ann.GetPosition
                                    If IsArray(pos) Then
                                        If UBound(pos) - LBound(pos) >= 1 Then
                                            byPosition = _
                                                Abs(CDbl(pos(LBound(pos))) - xMm / 1000#) <= FRAME_MATCH_TOL_M And _
                                                Abs(CDbl(pos(LBound(pos) + 1)) - yMm / 1000#) <= FRAME_MATCH_TOL_M And _
                                                Abs(t.GetColumnWidth(0) - wMm / 1000#) <= FRAME_MATCH_TOL_M
                                            If byPosition Then rank = 1
                                        End If
                                    End If
                                End If
                            End If
                        End If
                        If rank > 0 Then
                            ' Один Feature не должен попасть в список дважды.
                            nm = FrameTableFeatureName(t)
                            alreadyAdded = False
                            For Each existing In result
                                If StrComp(FrameTableFeatureName(existing), nm, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
                                    alreadyAdded = True
                                    Exit For
                                End If
                            Next existing
                            If Not alreadyAdded Then
                                result.Add t
                                If rank < 3 Then hasLegacy = True
                                If rank > bestRank Then
                                    Set preferred = t
                                    bestRank = rank
                                End If
                            End If
                        End If
                    End If
                End If
            Next i
        End If
        Set v = v.GetNextView
    Loop
    Set CollectFrameCells = result
End Function

Private Function AllowLegacyFrameMigration() As Boolean
    If frameLegacyDecision = 0 Then
        If TopYesNo( _
            "Найдены таблицы заполнения без постоянной служебной маркировки." & vbCrLf & _
            "Для перехода со старой версии они определяются по прежним именам " & _
            "или позиции и ширине поля (допуск 0,2 мм)." & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
            "Разрешить принять такие таблицы, обновить одну и удалить совпадающие дубли?" & vbCrLf & _
            "Внимание: вручную созданная таблица с теми же параметрами также может совпасть." & vbCrLf & _
            "При отказе неоднозначные поля останутся без изменений.", _
            "Переход на служебную маркировку") Then
            frameLegacyDecision = 1
        Else
            frameLegacyDecision = -1
        End If
    End If
    AllowLegacyFrameMigration = (frameLegacyDecision = 1)
End Function

Private Sub DeleteFrameDuplicate(ByVal swModel As Object, ByVal tbl As Object)
    Dim feat As Object, selMgr As Object
    Set feat = GetFrameTableFeature(tbl)
    swModel.ClearSelection2 True
    If Not feat.Select2(False, 0) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2111, , _
        "Не удалось выбрать дубликат: " & feat.name
    Set selMgr = swModel.SelectionManager
    If selMgr.GetSelectedObjectCount2(-1) <> 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2111, , _
        "Для удаления дубликата выделен не один объект."
    If Not swModel.Extension.DeleteSelection2(0) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2112, , _
        "Не удалось удалить дубликат: " & feat.name
    swModel.ClearSelection2 True
End Sub

Private Sub RemoveFrameDuplicates(ByVal swModel As Object, ByVal keeper As Object, _
    ByVal xMm As Double, ByVal yMm As Double, ByVal wMm As Double, ByVal legacyName As String)
    Dim cells As Collection, preferred As Object, t As Variant, duplicate As Object
    Dim hasLegacy As Boolean, keeperName As String, deleted As Long
    keeperName = FrameTableFeatureName(keeper)
    Do
        ' После удаления перечитываем список; сохраняем обновлённую таблицу.
        Set cells = CollectFrameCells(swModel, xMm, yMm, wMm, legacyName, preferred, hasLegacy)
        Set duplicate = Nothing
        For Each t In cells
            If StrComp(FrameTableFeatureName(t), keeperName, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then
                ' Без согласия старые немаркированные таблицы не трогаем.
                If Len(ReadFrameTableKey(t)) > 0 Or frameLegacyDecision = 1 Then
                    Set duplicate = t
                    Exit For
                End If
            End If
        Next t
        If duplicate Is Nothing Then Exit Do
        DeleteFrameDuplicate swModel, duplicate
        Set duplicate = Nothing
        deleted = deleted + 1
        If deleted > 10000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2113, , "Превышен лимит очистки дублей."
    Loop
End Sub

Private Function FrameTextComparable(ByVal value As String) As String
    FrameTextComparable = Replace(Replace(value, vbCrLf, vbLf), vbCr, vbLf)
End Function

Private Function InsertBorderlessCell(ByVal swModel As Object, _
    ByVal xMm As Double, ByVal yMm As Double, _
    ByVal wMm As Double, ByVal hMm As Double, _
    ByVal cellText As String, Optional ByVal tableName As String = "") As Boolean

    Dim tbl As Object, ann As Object, cells As Collection
    Dim hasLegacy As Boolean, isNew As Boolean, oldText As String, haveOldText As Boolean
    Dim fieldKey As String, updateComplete As Boolean, errorText As String
    Dim stage As String, errorNumber As Long
    On Error GoTo Failed
    fieldKey = FrameFieldKey(xMm, yMm, tableName)
    stage = "поиск существующей таблицы"
    Set cells = CollectFrameCells(swModel, xMm, yMm, wMm, tableName, tbl, hasLegacy)

    If hasLegacy Then
        If Not AllowLegacyFrameMigration() Then
            ' Не вставляем новую таблицу поверх не принятой старой.
            If tbl Is Nothing Then Exit Function
            If Len(ReadFrameTableKey(tbl)) = 0 Then
                AddMacroWarning "Поле " & FrameCellName(swModel, xMm, yMm) & _
                    ": переход со старой версии не разрешён; поле не изменено."
                Exit Function
            End If
        End If
    End If

    stage = "получение или создание таблицы"
    If tbl Is Nothing Then
        swModel.ClearSelection2 True
        Set tbl = swModel.Extension.InsertGeneralTableAnnotation(False, _
            xMm / 1000#, yMm / 1000#, 1, "", 1, 1)
        If tbl Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2120, , "Таблица не создана."
        isNew = True
    Else
        oldText = CStr(tbl.Text2(0, 0, False))
        haveOldText = True
    End If

    ' Сначала заполняем саму таблицу. Неудача маркировки не должна оставлять поле пустым.
    stage = "ширина таблицы"
    tbl.SetColumnWidth 0, wMm / 1000#, 0
    stage = "запись Text2"
    tbl.Text2(0, 0, False) = cellText
    stage = "независимый шрифт"
    If Not ApplyMacroTableFont(tbl) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2121, , _
        "Не применён независимый формат текста."
    stage = "высота строки и оформление"
    tbl.SetRowHeight 0, hMm / 1000#, 0
    tbl.CellTextHorizontalJustification(0, 0) = 2
    tbl.BorderLineWeight = -1
    tbl.GridLineWeight = -1
    stage = "позиция аннотации"
    tbl.Anchored = False
    Set ann = tbl.GetAnnotation
    If ann Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2122, , "Нет аннотации обновляемой таблицы."
    If Not ann.SetPosition2(xMm / 1000#, yMm / 1000#, 0) Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2123, , "Не задана позиция таблицы."
    stage = "проверка записанного текста"
    If FrameTextComparable(CStr(tbl.Text2(0, 0, False))) <> FrameTextComparable(cellText) Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2124, , "Не подтверждено новое содержимое ячейки."

    If isNew Then
        If Len(tableName) > 0 Then
            SetTableName tbl, tableName
        Else
            SetTableName tbl, FrameCellName(swModel, xMm, yMm)
        End If
    End If
    ' Имя существующей таблицы сохраняем: маркировка от него не зависит.
    updateComplete = True
    stage = "постоянная маркировка"
    If Not TryMarkFrameTable(swModel, tbl, fieldKey) Then
        InsertBorderlessCell = True
        Exit Function
    End If
    stage = "удаление только дублей"
    RemoveFrameDuplicates swModel, tbl, xMm, yMm, wMm, tableName
    If hasLegacy And frameLegacyDecision = -1 Then _
        AddMacroWarning "Поле " & FrameCellName(swModel, xMm, yMm) & _
            ": маркированная таблица обновлена; старые немаркированные совпадения оставлены."
    InsertBorderlessCell = True
    Exit Function
Failed:
    errorNumber = Err.number
    errorText = "этап '" & stage & "', ошибка " & CStr(errorNumber) & ": " & Err.description
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not updateComplete Then
        If isNew Then
            If Not tbl Is Nothing Then DeleteFrameDuplicate swModel, tbl
        ElseIf haveOldText Then
            tbl.Text2(0, 0, False) = oldText
        End If
    End If
    swModel.ClearSelection2 True
    On Error GoTo 0
    If updateComplete Then
        AddMacroWarning "Поле " & FrameCellName(swModel, xMm, yMm) & _
            ": таблица обновлена, но очистка дублей не завершена: " & errorText
    Else
        AddMacroWarning "Поле " & FrameCellName(swModel, xMm, yMm) & _
            ": обновление не завершено: " & errorText & ". Проверьте содержимое поля."
    End If
End Function

Private Function FormatFrameCode(ByVal codeValue As String) As String
    Dim p As Long
    p = InStr(1, codeValue, ".")
    If p > 0 And p < Len(codeValue) Then
        FormatFrameCode = Left$(codeValue, p) & vbCrLf & Mid$(codeValue, p + 1)
    Else
        FormatFrameCode = codeValue
    End If
End Function

Private Function IsNumberedSheet(ByVal sheetName As String, ByVal prefix As String) As Boolean
    Dim tail As String
    sheetName = Trim$(sheetName)
    If StrComp(Left$(sheetName, Len(prefix)), prefix, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then Exit Function
    tail = Mid$(sheetName, Len(prefix) + 1)
    IsNumberedSheet = (Len(tail) > 0 And IsNumeric(tail))
End Function

Private Function SheetExistsExact(ByVal swDraw As Object, ByVal sheetName As String) As Boolean
    Dim names As Variant, i As Long
    names = swDraw.GetSheetNames
    For i = LBound(names) To UBound(names)
        If StrComp(CStr(names(i)), sheetName, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
            SheetExistsExact = True
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next i
End Function

Private Function IsReplaceableOKSheet(ByVal sheetName As String) As Boolean
    Dim re As Object, matches As Object
    Set re = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")
    re.IgnoreCase = True
    ' Только имена, применяемые этим макросом, включая старый суффикс времени.
    re.Pattern = "^ОК([0-9]+)(_[0-9]{6})?$"
    Set matches = re.Execute(sheetName)
    If matches.count = 0 Then Exit Function
    IsReplaceableOKSheet = (CDbl(matches(0).SubMatches(0)) >= START_SHEET_NUM)
End Function

Private Function IsActiveKEWeldSheet(ByVal sheetName As String) As Boolean
    Dim re As Object
    Set re = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")
    re.IgnoreCase = True
    re.Pattern = "^КЭ \(Швы Лист [0-9]+\)(_[0-9]{6})?$"
    IsActiveKEWeldSheet = re.Test(sheetName)
End Function

Private Function IsArchivedKEWeldSheet(ByVal sheetName As String) As Boolean
    Dim re As Object
    Set re = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")
    re.IgnoreCase = True
    re.Pattern = "^КЭ\s*\(Швы\s+Лист\s+[0-9]+\)(_[0-9]{6})?_old(_?[0-9]+)?$"
    IsArchivedKEWeldSheet = re.Test(Trim$(sheetName))
End Function

Private Function MoveArchivedKEToEnd(ByVal swDraw As Object) As Boolean
    Dim names As Variant, actual As Variant, order() As String, vOrder As Variant
    Dim i As Long, idx As Long, total As Long, archived As Long, changed As Boolean
    On Error GoTo Failed
    names = swDraw.GetSheetNames
    total = UBound(names) - LBound(names) + 1
    ReDim order(0 To total - 1)
    For i = LBound(names) To UBound(names)
        If Not IsArchivedKEWeldSheet(CStr(names(i))) Then
            order(idx) = CStr(names(i))
            idx = idx + 1
        Else
            archived = archived + 1
        End If
    Next i
    For i = LBound(names) To UBound(names)
        If IsArchivedKEWeldSheet(CStr(names(i))) Then
            order(idx) = CStr(names(i))
            idx = idx + 1
        End If
    Next i
    If archived = 0 Then
        MoveArchivedKEToEnd = True
        Exit Function
    End If
    For i = 0 To total - 1
        If StrComp(order(i), CStr(names(LBound(names) + i)), vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then changed = True
    Next i
    If changed Then
        vOrder = order
        If Not swDraw.ReorderSheets(vOrder) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2330, , _
            "ReorderSheets вернул False."
        actual = swDraw.GetSheetNames
        If UBound(actual) - LBound(actual) + 1 <> total Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2331, , _
            "После перестановки изменилось число листов."
        For i = 0 To total - 1
            If StrComp(order(i), CStr(actual(LBound(actual) + i)), vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then _
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2332, , "Не подтверждён порядок листов после перестановки."
        Next i
    End If
    MoveArchivedKEToEnd = True
    Exit Function
Failed:
    AddMacroWarning "Архивы КЭ не удалось переместить в конец: " & CStr(Err.number) & ": " & Err.description
End Function

Private Function PrepareOKSheetsForReplacement(ByVal swDraw As Object, _
    ByVal swModel As Object, ByRef insertAfterSheet As String) As Boolean

    Dim names As Variant, i As Long, n As String, toDelete As New Collection
    Dim retained As New Collection, item As Variant, anchor As String
    Dim preview As String, originalAnchor As String, deletedName As String
    Dim selMgr As Object
    names = swDraw.GetSheetNames
    For i = LBound(names) To UBound(names)
        n = CStr(names(i))
        If IsReplaceableOKSheet(n) Then
            toDelete.Add n
        Else
            retained.Add n
        End If
    Next i
    If toDelete.count = 0 Then
        PrepareOKSheetsForReplacement = True
        Exit Function
    End If
    If retained.count = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2300, , _
        "Нельзя удалить все листы. Нужен сохраняемый исходный лист, например ОК1."

    originalAnchor = insertAfterSheet
    For Each item In retained
        If StrComp(CStr(item), insertAfterSheet, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then anchor = CStr(item)
    Next item
    If Len(anchor) = 0 Then
        ' Если выбранный ориентир удаляется, по возможности используем ОК1.
        For Each item In retained
            If StrComp(CStr(item), "ОК1", vbTextCompare) = 0 Then anchor = CStr(item)
        Next item
        If Len(anchor) = 0 Then anchor = CStr(retained(1))
    End If

    For Each item In toDelete
        preview = preview & CStr(item) & vbCrLf
    Next item
    If Len(preview) > 2200 Then
        ' Не удаляем без полного предварительного списка в окне подтверждения.
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2301, , _
            "Слишком много листов для безопасного подтверждения удаления. Очистите часть ОК вручную на копии."
    End If
    If Not replacementApproved Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3058, , "Удаление ОК не подтверждено."

    ' Сначала активируем лист, который точно не удаляется.
    If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(swDraw, anchor) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2302, , _
        "Не удалось активировать сохраняемый лист перед удалением ОК."
    For Each item In toDelete
        deletedName = CStr(item)
        If Not IsReplaceableOKSheet(deletedName) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2303, , _
            "Попытка удалить лист вне разрешённого шаблона имени."
        swModel.ClearSelection2 True
        If Not swModel.Extension.SelectByID2(deletedName, "SHEET", 0#, 0#, 0#, False, 0, Nothing, 0) Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2304, , "Не удалось выбрать лист для удаления: " & deletedName
        Set selMgr = swModel.SelectionManager
        If selMgr.GetSelectedObjectCount2(-1) <> 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2305, , _
            "Для удаления листа выделен не один объект: " & deletedName
        If Not swModel.Extension.DeleteSelection2(0) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2306, , _
            "Не удалось удалить лист: " & deletedName
        If SheetExistsExact(swDraw, deletedName) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2307, , _
            "Лист всё ещё существует после удаления: " & deletedName
        deletedOKCount = deletedOKCount + 1
        Debug.Print "Удалён лист ОК: " & deletedName
    Next item
    swModel.ClearSelection2 True
    insertAfterSheet = anchor
    If StrComp(originalAnchor, anchor, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then _
        Debug.Print "Ориентир новых ОК заменён: " & originalAnchor & " -> " & anchor
    PrepareOKSheetsForReplacement = True
End Function

Private Sub RenameSheetChecked(ByVal swDraw As Object, ByVal oldName As String, ByVal newName As String)
    Dim sheet As Object
    If SheetExistsExact(swDraw, newName) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2310, , _
        "Имя листа уже занято: " & newName
    If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(swDraw, oldName) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2311, , _
        "Не удалось активировать лист для переименования: " & oldName
    Set sheet = swDraw.GetCurrentSheet
    sheet.SetName newName
    If StrComp(sheet.GetName, newName, vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2312, , "Не подтверждено новое имя листа: " & newName
    If Not SheetExistsExact(swDraw, newName) Or SheetExistsExact(swDraw, oldName) Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2313, , "Не подтверждено переименование листа: " & oldName
End Sub

Private Sub ArchiveExistingKEWeldSheets(ByVal swDraw As Object, ByRef insertAfterSheet As String)
    Dim names As Variant, i As Long, n As String, archivedName As String, generation As Long
    Dim changedFrom As New Collection, changedTo As New Collection
    Dim oldAnchor As String, failure As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    oldAnchor = insertAfterSheet
    names = swDraw.GetSheetNames
    generation = KC18_NextArchiveGeneration(swDraw)
    ' Проверяем все будущие имена до первого переименования.
    For i = LBound(names) To UBound(names)
        n = CStr(names(i))
        If IsActiveKEWeldSheet(n) Then
            archivedName = n & "_old" & CStr(generation)
            If SheetExistsExact(swDraw, archivedName) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3191, , "Имя архива занято: " & archivedName
        End If
    Next i
    For i = LBound(names) To UBound(names)
        n = CStr(names(i))
        If IsActiveKEWeldSheet(n) Then
            archivedName = n & "_old" & CStr(generation)
            RenameSheetChecked swDraw, n, archivedName
            changedFrom.Add n
            changedTo.Add archivedName
            If StrComp(insertAfterSheet, n, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then insertAfterSheet = archivedName
            Debug.Print "Архив КЭ: " & n & " -> " & archivedName
        End If
    Next i
    archivedKECount = archivedKECount + changedFrom.count
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    failure = Err.description
    ' Если архивирование оборвалось, пытаемся вернуть уже изменённые имена.
    On Error Resume Next
    For i = changedFrom.count To 1 Step -1
        RenameSheetChecked swDraw, CStr(changedTo(i)), CStr(changedFrom(i))
        If Err.number <> 0 Then
            Debug.Print "Не удалось вернуть имя: " & CStr(changedTo(i)) & "; " & Err.description
            Err.Clear
        End If
    Next i
    insertAfterSheet = oldAnchor
    On Error GoTo 0
    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2314, , "Архивирование КЭ остановлено: " & failure & _
        ". Новые КЭ не создавались. Проверьте имена сохранённых листов."
End Sub

Private Function TryCreateSheetVersion(ByVal swDraw As Object, ByVal sheetName As String, _
    ByVal templatePath As String, ByVal paperW As Double, ByVal paperH As Double, _
    ByVal apiVersion As Long) As Boolean
    On Error Resume Next
    Select Case apiVersion
        Case 4
            swDraw.NewSheet4 sheetName, 0, 12, 1, 1, True, templatePath, paperW, paperH, "", True
        Case 3
            swDraw.NewSheet3 sheetName, 0, 12, 1, 1, True, templatePath, paperW, paperH, ""
        Case 2
            swDraw.NewSheet2 sheetName, 0, 12, 1, 1, True, "", paperW, paperH
        Case Else
            swDraw.NewSheet sheetName
    End Select
    Err.Clear
    On Error GoTo 0
    ' Проверяем фактическое существование, а не только возвращаемое значение API.
    TryCreateSheetVersion = SheetExistsExact(swDraw, sheetName)
End Function

Private Sub CreateMacroSheetChecked(ByVal swDraw As Object, ByVal sheetName As String, _
    ByVal templatePath As String, ByVal useTemplate As Boolean, _
    ByVal paperW As Double, ByVal paperH As Double)
    Dim tplArg As String, created As Boolean, sheet As Object
    If SheetExistsExact(swDraw, sheetName) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2320, , _
        "Лист уже существует: " & sheetName & ". Создание дубля с суффиксом запрещено."
    tplArg = IIf(useTemplate, templatePath, "")
    created = TryCreateSheetVersion(swDraw, sheetName, tplArg, paperW, paperH, 4)
    If Not created Then created = TryCreateSheetVersion(swDraw, sheetName, tplArg, paperW, paperH, 3)
    If Not created Then created = TryCreateSheetVersion(swDraw, sheetName, tplArg, paperW, paperH, 2)
    If Not created Then created = TryCreateSheetVersion(swDraw, sheetName, tplArg, paperW, paperH, 1)
    If Not created Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2321, , "Не создан лист: " & sheetName
    If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(swDraw, sheetName) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2322, , _
        "Новый лист не активирован: " & sheetName
    If useTemplate And Len(templatePath) > 0 Then
        Set sheet = swDraw.GetCurrentSheet
        EnsureMacroSheetFormat sheet, templatePath
    End If
End Sub

Private Function RegularKENumber(ByVal sheetName As String) As Long
    Dim re As Object, matches As Object, value As Double
    Set re = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")
    re.IgnoreCase = True
    re.Pattern = "^КЭ([0-9]+)$"
    Set matches = re.Execute(Trim$(sheetName))
    If matches.count = 0 Then Exit Function
    value = CDbl(matches(0).SubMatches(0))
    If value < 1 Or value > 2147483647# Then Exit Function
    RegularKENumber = CLng(value)
End Function

Private Function IsKESheet(ByVal sheetName As String) As Boolean
    sheetName = Trim$(sheetName)
    If IsArchivedKEWeldSheet(sheetName) Then Exit Function
    IsKESheet = (RegularKENumber(sheetName) > 0) Or IsActiveKEWeldSheet(sheetName)
End Function

Private Function MaxRegularKENumber(ByVal swDraw As Object) As Long
    Dim names As Variant, i As Long, n As Long
    names = swDraw.GetSheetNames
    For i = LBound(names) To UBound(names)
        n = RegularKENumber(CStr(names(i)))
        If n > MaxRegularKENumber Then MaxRegularKENumber = n
    Next i
End Function

Private Function KEPrintedNumber(ByVal sheetName As String, ByVal maxRegular As Long, _
    ByRef weldOrdinal As Long) As Long
    Dim n As Long
    n = RegularKENumber(sheetName)
    If n > 0 Then
        KEPrintedNumber = n
    ElseIf IsActiveKEWeldSheet(sheetName) Then
        weldOrdinal = weldOrdinal + 1
        KEPrintedNumber = maxRegular + weldOrdinal
    End If
End Function

Private Function CountRelevantSheets(ByVal swDraw As Object) As Long
    Dim names As Variant, i As Long, n As String, total As Long
    names = swDraw.GetSheetNames
    For i = LBound(names) To UBound(names)
        n = Trim$(CStr(names(i)))
        If Not IsArchivedKEWeldSheet(n) Then
            If StrComp(n, "Титульный лист", vbTextCompare) = 0 _
                Or IsNumberedSheet(n, "ОК") _
                Or IsKESheet(n) Then total = total + 1
        End If
    Next i
    CountRelevantSheets = total
End Function

Private Function CountOKSheets(ByVal swDraw As Object) As Long
    Dim names As Variant, i As Long, total As Long
    names = swDraw.GetSheetNames
    For i = LBound(names) To UBound(names)
        If IsNumberedSheet(CStr(names(i)), "ОК") Then total = total + 1
    Next i
    CountOKSheets = total
End Function

Private Function CountKESheets(ByVal swDraw As Object) As Long
    Dim names As Variant, i As Long, total As Long
    names = swDraw.GetSheetNames
    For i = LBound(names) To UBound(names)
        If IsKESheet(CStr(names(i))) Then total = total + 1
    Next i
    CountKESheets = total
End Function

Private Function GetTableByName(ByVal swDraw As Object, ByVal tableName As String) As Object
    Dim allViews As Variant, sheetViews As Variant, i As Long
    Dim sheetView As Object, viewTables As Variant, t As Long
    Dim tbl As Object, genTable As Object, feat As Object, ann As Object
    Dim activeSheet As Object, activeName As String, tableNameInTree As String

    ' GetViews возвращает массив массивов: первый View каждого массива — сам лист.
    On Error Resume Next
    Set activeSheet = swDraw.GetCurrentSheet
    activeName = activeSheet.GetName
    allViews = swDraw.GetViews
    If IsEmpty(allViews) Then
        On Error GoTo 0
        Exit Function
    End If

    For i = LBound(allViews) To UBound(allViews)
        sheetViews = allViews(i)
        If IsEmpty(sheetViews) Then GoTo NextSheetViewGroup
        Set sheetView = sheetViews(LBound(sheetViews))
        If StrComp(sheetView.GetName2, activeName, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then GoTo NextSheetViewGroup

        viewTables = sheetView.GetTableAnnotations
        If IsEmpty(viewTables) Then GoTo NextSheetViewGroup

        For t = LBound(viewTables) To UBound(viewTables)
            Set tbl = viewTables(t)
            tableNameInTree = ""
            Set genTable = tbl.GeneralTableFeature
            If Not genTable Is Nothing Then
                Set feat = genTable.GetFeature
                If Not feat Is Nothing Then tableNameInTree = feat.name
            End If
            If Len(tableNameInTree) = 0 Then
                Set ann = tbl.GetAnnotation
                If Not ann Is Nothing Then tableNameInTree = ann.GetName
            End If
            If StrComp(tableNameInTree, tableName, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
                Set GetTableByName = tbl
                On Error GoTo 0
                Exit Function
            End If
        Next t
NextSheetViewGroup:
    Next i
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

Private Function NumericCellValue(ByVal valueText As String, ByRef numberValue As Double) As Boolean
    Dim s As String, decSep As String
    Dim posComma As Long, posDot As Long

    s = Trim$(valueText)
    If Len(s) = 0 Then Exit Function
    s = Replace(s, Chr(160), "")
    s = Replace(s, " ", "")
    s = Replace(s, "'", "")

    ' Учитываем как русскую запись 0,5, так и запись 1.5.
    ' Приводим десятичный разделитель к разделителю Windows/VBA.
    decSep = Mid$(CStr(1.1), 2, 1)
    posComma = InStrRev(s, ",")
    posDot = InStrRev(s, ".")

    If posComma > 0 And posDot > 0 Then
        ' Последний разделитель считаем десятичным, другой — разделителем тысяч.
        If posComma > posDot Then
            s = Replace(s, ".", "")
            s = Replace(s, ",", decSep)
        Else
            s = Replace(s, ",", "")
            s = Replace(s, ".", decSep)
        End If
    ElseIf posComma > 0 Then
        s = Replace(s, ",", decSep)
    ElseIf posDot > 0 Then
        s = Replace(s, ".", decSep)
    End If

    If Not IsNumeric(s) Then Exit Function
    numberValue = CDbl(s)
    NumericCellValue = True
End Function

Private Function FormatSumValue(ByVal total As Double) As String
    Dim roundedTotal As Double
    ' Итоговое время записываем с точностью до 0,1 часа.
    roundedTotal = Round(total, 1)
    If Abs(roundedTotal - Fix(roundedTotal)) < 0.000001 Then
        FormatSumValue = CStr(CLng(roundedTotal))
    Else
        ' Format$ использует локальный десятичный разделитель Windows.
        FormatSumValue = Format$(roundedTotal, "0.0")
    End If
End Function

Private Function ExtractFirstInteger(ByVal valueText As String) As String
    Dim re As Object, matches As Object
    Set re = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")
    re.Global = False
    re.IgnoreCase = True
    re.Pattern = "(^|[^0-9])([0-9]+)"
    Set matches = re.Execute(valueText)
    If matches.count > 0 Then ExtractFirstInteger = matches(0).SubMatches(1)
End Function

Private Function FindSeamNumberRange(ByRef col0() As String, _
    ByRef col1() As String, ByVal rowCount As Long) As String
    Dim r As Long, n As String, firstNo As String, lastNo As String
    For r = 1 To rowCount
        If InStr(1, col0(r), "шва", vbTextCompare) > 0 _
            Or InStr(1, col1(r), "шва", vbTextCompare) > 0 Then
            ' Номер берём из столбца B Excel (в коде это col0).
            n = ExtractFirstInteger(col0(r))
            If Len(n) > 0 Then
                If Len(firstNo) = 0 Then firstNo = n
                lastNo = n
            End If
        End If
    Next r

    If Len(firstNo) = 0 Then Exit Function
    If firstNo = lastNo Then
        FindSeamNumberRange = "№ " & firstNo
    Else
        FindSeamNumberRange = "№ " & firstNo & "-" & lastNo
    End If
End Function

Private Function ReplaceSeamRangeTail(ByVal tail As String, ByVal seamRange As String) As String
    Dim re As Object, matches As Object, suffix As String, firstChar As String
    Set re = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")
    re.IgnoreCase = True
    re.Pattern = "^\s*№\s*[0-9]+(\s*[-–—]\s*[0-9]+)?(\s*,\s*[0-9]+(\s*[-–—]\s*[0-9]+)?)*"
    Set matches = re.Execute(tail)
    suffix = tail
    If matches.count > 0 Then
        suffix = Mid$(tail, matches(0).Length + 1)
    Else
        re.Pattern = "^\s*№"
        If re.Test(tail) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2340, , _
            "После фразы найден номер нестандартного вида. Автоматическая замена отменена."
    End If
    If Len(seamRange) > 0 Then
        If Len(suffix) > 0 Then
            firstChar = Left$(suffix, 1)
            If InStr(" " & vbTab & vbCr & vbLf & ",.;:!?)]}", firstChar) = 0 Then suffix = " " & suffix
        End If
        ReplaceSeamRangeTail = " " & seamRange & suffix
    Else
        ReplaceSeamRangeTail = suffix
    End If
End Function



Private Sub FillSumTableOnOK1(ByVal swDraw As Object, ByVal swModel As Object, ByVal total As Double)
    If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(swDraw, "ОК1") Then Exit Sub
    If Not InsertBorderlessCell(swModel, 109.5, 131.75, 20.8, 12.75, _
        FormatSumValue(total), "Время операций") Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2427, , "Не обновлено итоговое время на ОК1."
End Sub

Private Sub FillCountTables(ByVal swDraw As Object, ByVal swModel As Object)
    Dim totalSheets As Long, totalOKSheets As Long
    Dim names As Variant, i As Long, n As String
    totalSheets = CountRelevantSheets(swDraw)
    totalOKSheets = CountOKSheets(swDraw)
    names = swDraw.GetSheetNames
    For i = LBound(names) To UBound(names)
        n = CStr(names(i))
        If StrComp(n, "Титульный лист", vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
            If ActivateSheetForMacro(swDraw, n) Then
                InsertBorderlessCell swModel, TL_X5, TL_Y5, TL_W5, TL_H5, CStr(totalSheets)
            End If
        ElseIf StrComp(n, "ОК1", vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
            If ActivateSheetForMacro(swDraw, n) Then
                InsertBorderlessCell swModel, OK1_X5, OK1_Y5, OK1_W5, OK1_H5, CStr(totalOKSheets)
                InsertBorderlessCell swModel, OK1_X6, OK1_Y6, OK1_W6, OK1_H6, "1"
            End If
        End If
    Next i
End Sub

Private Function FillSpecialSheet(ByVal swDraw As Object, ByVal swModel As Object, _
    ByVal sheetName As String, ByVal detailName As String, ByVal kdNumber As String, _
    ByVal primary02190 As String, ByVal kd02102 As String, ByVal primary60102 As String, _
    ByVal isTitle As Boolean) As Boolean

    Dim names As Variant, i As Long, exactName As String
    names = swDraw.GetSheetNames
    For i = LBound(names) To UBound(names)
        If StrComp(CStr(names(i)), sheetName, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then exactName = CStr(names(i)): Exit For
    Next i
    If Len(exactName) = 0 Then Exit Function

    If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(swDraw, exactName) Then Exit Function
    swModel.ClearSelection2 True

    If isTitle Then
        InsertBorderlessCell swModel, TL_X1, TL_Y1, TL_W1, TL_H1, detailName
        InsertBorderlessCell swModel, TL_X2, TL_Y2, TL_W2, TL_H2, kdNumber
        InsertBorderlessCell swModel, TL_X3, TL_Y3, TL_W3, TL_H3, primary02190
        InsertBorderlessCell swModel, TL_X4, TL_Y4, TL_W4, TL_H4, FormatFrameCode(kd02102)
    Else
        InsertBorderlessCell swModel, OK1_X1, OK1_Y1, OK1_W1, OK1_H1, detailName
        InsertBorderlessCell swModel, OK1_X2, OK1_Y2, OK1_W2, OK1_H2, kdNumber
        InsertBorderlessCell swModel, OK1_X3, OK1_Y3, OK1_W3, OK1_H3, primary02190
        InsertBorderlessCell swModel, OK1_X4, OK1_Y4, OK1_W4, OK1_H4, FormatFrameCode(primary60102)
        KC23_UpdateSignHeader swModel, KC28_N("Sign.OK1Y")
        ' Только ОК1. Таблица 1 x 1: здесь высота 12,75 мм, а не 4,25 мм.
        If Not InsertBorderlessCell(swModel, KC28_N("Header.EquipmentX"), KC28_N("Header.EquipmentY"), KC28_N("Header.EquipmentW"), KC28_N("Header.EquipmentH"), gKC29Equipment) Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3356, , "Не удалось обновить ячейку оборудования ОК1."
    End If

    FillSpecialSheet = True
End Function

' ============================================
' ТОЧКА ВХОДА
' ============================================

Public Sub Main()
    replacementApproved = False
    macroWarnings = ""
    gKC23HeaderReady = False
    gKC25TemplatePath = ""
    frameLegacyDecision = 0
    Set frameAttributeNames = Nothing
    Set operationTableFormat = Nothing
    archivedKECount = 0
    deletedOKCount = 0
    Dim swApp As Object
    Dim swModel As Object
    Dim swDraw As Object
    Dim wrapProbe As Object
    Dim fillOwnExcel As Boolean, mainError As String, logDrawing As String
    Dim variantEnabled As Boolean, variantText As String
    Dim fillStage As String
    Dim introSyncReason As String
    Dim keLayoutBefore As String
    Dim variantSheets As Variant, variantSheetIndex As Long, variantFirst As Long, variantLast As Long
    Dim variantSheetName As String
    fillStage = "Подключение к чертежу"
    On Error GoTo ErrHandler

    Set swApp = Application.SldWorks
    Set swModel = swApp.ActiveDoc

    If swModel Is Nothing Then
        TopMsg "Откройте чертёж!", "Ошибка", MB_ICONCRITICAL
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If swModel.GetType <> 3 Then
        TopMsg "Это не чертёж!", "Ошибка", MB_ICONCRITICAL
        Exit Sub
    End If
    Set swDraw = swModel
    fillStage = "Загрузка общих настроек"
    KC28_Load swApp

    ' === ВЫБОР ФАЙЛА EXCEL (открывается сразу) ===
    Dim xlFilePath As String
    fillStage = "Поиск привязанной книги Excel"
    xlFilePath = KC14_Resolve(swModel.GetPathName)

    ' === ФИКСИРОВАННЫЙ ШАБЛОН ===
    Dim templatePath As String
    Dim useTemplate As Boolean
    templatePath = DEFAULT_TEMPLATE_PATH
    If Len(Dir$(templatePath)) = 0 Then
        TopMsg "Не найден шаблон листа:" & vbCrLf & templatePath, "Ошибка", MB_ICONCRITICAL
        Exit Sub
    End If
    useTemplate = True
    If Len(Dir$(OK1_TEMPLATE_PATH)) = 0 Then
        TopMsg "Не найден шаблон ОК1:" & vbCrLf & OK1_TEMPLATE_PATH, "Ошибка", MB_ICONCRITICAL
        Exit Sub
    End If

    Dim kdNumber As String, detailName As String, primaryNumber As String

    ' === ФИКСИРОВАННЫЕ КООРДИНАТЫ ОСНОВНОЙ ТАБЛИЦЫ ===
    Dim tableX As Double, tableY As Double
    Dim anchorType As Long
    tableX = DEFAULT_X_MM / 1000#
    tableY = DEFAULT_Y_MM / 1000#
    anchorType = 1

    ' === ВЫБОР МЕСТА ВСТАВКИ ЛИСТОВ В ЧЕРТЕЖЕ ===
    Dim initialSheets As Variant
    fillStage = "Чтение списка листов чертежа"
    initialSheets = swDraw.GetSheetNames
    Dim defaultInsertAfter As String
    defaultInsertAfter = ""

    Dim sIdx As Long
    For sIdx = 0 To UBound(initialSheets)
        If StrComp(CStr(initialSheets(sIdx)), "Титульный лист", vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
            defaultInsertAfter = CStr(initialSheets(sIdx))
            Exit For
        End If
    Next sIdx
    If Len(defaultInsertAfter) = 0 Then
        For sIdx = 0 To UBound(initialSheets)
            If StrComp(CStr(initialSheets(sIdx)), "ОК1", vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
                defaultInsertAfter = CStr(initialSheets(sIdx))
                Exit For
            End If
        Next sIdx
    End If

    If Len(defaultInsertAfter) = 0 Then
        Dim curShObj As Object
        Set curShObj = swDraw.GetCurrentSheet
        If Not curShObj Is Nothing Then
            defaultInsertAfter = curShObj.GetName
        Else
            defaultInsertAfter = CStr(initialSheets(0))
        End If
    End If

    Dim insertAfterSheet As String
    insertAfterSheet = KC14_Setting(swModel.GetPathName, "Anchor", defaultInsertAfter)
    If Len(insertAfterSheet) = 0 Then insertAfterSheet = defaultInsertAfter
    insertAfterSheet = Trim(insertAfterSheet)
    If Len(insertAfterSheet) = 0 Then Exit Sub
    If Not SheetExistsExact(swDraw, insertAfterSheet) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3710, , _
        "Нет ориентира вставки ОК. Сначала выполните режим 1 первого макроса или исправьте настройки."
    Dim requestedInsertAfter As String
    requestedInsertAfter = insertAfterSheet

    ' === ЧТЕНИЕ EXCEL ===
    Dim xlApp As Object
    Dim xlWB As Object
    Dim wsCard As Object

    On Error GoTo ErrHandler
    fillStage = "Открытие рабочей книги Excel"
    KC14_FillBook xlFilePath, xlApp, xlWB, fillOwnExcel
    fillStage = "Проверка принадлежности и готовности книги"
    KC14_CheckReady xlWB, swModel.GetPathName
    keLayoutBefore = KC37_ValidateForFill(swDraw, swModel, xlWB)

    Dim wsFound As Boolean
    wsFound = False
    Dim ws As Object
    fillStage = "Поиск листа Карта контроля"
    For Each ws In xlWB.sheets
        If ws.name = "Карта контроля" Then
            Set wsCard = ws
            wsFound = True
            Exit For
        End If
    Next

    If Not wsFound Then
        TopMsg "Лист 'Карта контроля' не найден!", "Внимание", _
            MB_ICONEXCLAMATION
        If fillOwnExcel Then xlWB.Close False: xlApp.Quit
        Exit Sub
    End If

    Dim ok1Data() As String, partMassText As String, totalTimeExcel As Double
    fillStage = "Общая проверка реквизитов, массы и фамилии разработчика"
    xlApp.Calculate
    If Not EnsureUnifiedExcel(xlApp, xlWB, wsCard, xlFilePath, primaryNumber, kdNumber, detailName) Then
        ' Книга остаётся открытой; все найденные ошибки перечислены одним сообщением.
        Exit Sub
    End If

    Dim hdrDeciNum As String, hdrPartNum1 As String, hdrPartNum2 As String
    hdrDeciNum = BuildShiftedCode(primaryNumber, KC28_S("Codes.02190"))
    hdrPartNum1 = kdNumber
    ' Для ОК2 и последующих листов используем ту же комбинацию,
    ' что и в ОК1: номер КД с шифром 60102 и преобразованным окончанием.
    hdrPartNum2 = BuildShiftedCode(kdNumber, KC28_S("Codes.60102"))
    If Len(hdrDeciNum) = 0 Or Len(hdrPartNum2) = 0 Then
        TopMsg "Номер КД или первичного применения имеет неверный формат.", "Ошибка", MB_ICONCRITICAL
        Exit Sub
    End If
    Dim dotPos As Long
    dotPos = InStr(1, hdrPartNum2, ".")
    If dotPos > 0 And dotPos < Len(hdrPartNum2) Then
        hdrPartNum2 = Left$(hdrPartNum2, dotPos) & vbCrLf & Mid$(hdrPartNum2, dotPos + 1)
    End If
    Dim primary60102 As String
    primary60102 = BuildShiftedCode(kdNumber, KC28_S("Codes.60102"))
    Dim kd02102 As String
    kd02102 = BuildShiftedCode(kdNumber, KC28_S("Codes.02102"))
    fillStage = "Проверка нумерации вводных и параметров"
    If Not KC20_CheckIntroSync(swModel, wsCard, introSyncReason) Then
        TopMsg introSyncReason, "Проверка вводных пунктов", MB_ICONEXCLAMATION
        ' Закрываем только экземпляр Excel, открытый этим запуском.
        On Error Resume Next
        If fillOwnExcel Then
            xlWB.Close False
            xlApp.Quit
        End If
        Set xlWB = Nothing: Set xlApp = Nothing
        On Error GoTo 0
        Exit Sub
    End If
    KC_ValidateCardNumbering xlWB, wsCard
    fillStage = "Чтение трёх фиксированных строк ОК1"
    ReadOK1ExcelData wsCard, ok1Data, partMassText, totalTimeExcel
    fillStage = "Чтение таблицы исполнений"
    KC15_ReadVariants wsCard, variantEnabled, variantText
    fillStage = "Чтение должностей и фамилий шапки"
    gKC23HeaderReady = KC23_ReadHeader(wsCard, gKC23Roles, gKC23Surnames)
    If Not gKC23HeaderReady Then AddMacroWarning "В книге нет панели шапки v16; прежние подписи ОК1/КЭ1 оставлены без изменения."

    Dim lastRow As Long
    fillStage = "Поиск последней строки карты"
    lastRow = LastOKContinuationRow(wsCard)

    Dim startDataRow As Long
    startDataRow = 7

    If lastRow < startDataRow Then
        TopMsg "Нет данных!", "Внимание", MB_ICONEXCLAMATION
        If fillOwnExcel Then xlWB.Close False: xlApp.Quit
        Exit Sub
    End If

    Dim totalExcelRows As Long
    totalExcelRows = lastRow - startDataRow + 1

    Dim rawCol0() As String
    Dim rawCol1() As String
    Dim rawCol2() As String
    Dim rawCol3() As String
    Dim rawNum() As String

    ReDim rawCol0(1 To totalExcelRows)
    ReDim rawCol1(1 To totalExcelRows)
    ReDim rawCol2(1 To totalExcelRows)
    ReDim rawCol3(1 To totalExcelRows)
    ReDim rawNum(1 To totalExcelRows)

    Dim r As Long
    For r = 1 To totalExcelRows
        Dim xlR As Long
        xlR = startDataRow + r - 1
        fillStage = "Чтение Excel A:E, строка " & CStr(xlR)
        rawNum(r) = OK1ExcelText(wsCard, xlR, 1)
        rawCol0(r) = OK1ExcelText(wsCard, xlR, 2)
        rawCol1(r) = OK1ExcelText(wsCard, xlR, 3)
        rawCol2(r) = OK1ExcelText(wsCard, xlR, 4)
        If Len(rawCol2(r)) > 0 Then rawCol2(r) = FormatPercentValue(wsCard.cells(xlR, 4).Value2)
        rawCol3(r) = OK1ExcelText(wsCard, xlR, 5)
        If Not IsOKCardFooter(rawCol0(r), rawCol1(r), rawNum(r)) Then
            totalTimeExcel = totalTimeExcel + ExcelTimeValue(wsCard, xlR, 5)
        End If
    Next r

    If fillOwnExcel Then xlWB.Close False: xlApp.Quit
    Set xlWB = Nothing
    Set xlApp = Nothing

    fillStage = "Исключение итоговых строк"
    ' === ФИЛЬТРАЦИЯ ===
    Dim filtCol0() As String
    Dim filtCol1() As String
    Dim filtCol2() As String
    Dim filtCol3() As String
    Dim filtNum() As String
    Dim filtCount As Long
    filtCount = 0

    ReDim filtCol0(1 To totalExcelRows)
    ReDim filtCol1(1 To totalExcelRows)
    ReDim filtCol2(1 To totalExcelRows)
    ReDim filtCol3(1 To totalExcelRows)
    ReDim filtNum(1 To totalExcelRows)

    For r = 1 To totalExcelRows
        If IsOKCardFooter(rawCol0(r), rawCol1(r), rawNum(r)) Then GoTo SkipFilterRow

        filtCount = filtCount + 1
        filtCol0(filtCount) = rawCol0(r)
        filtCol1(filtCount) = rawCol1(r)
        filtCol2(filtCount) = rawCol2(r)
        filtCol3(filtCount) = rawCol3(r)
        filtNum(filtCount) = rawNum(r)
SkipFilterRow:
    Next r

    totalExcelRows = filtCount

    If totalExcelRows = 0 Then
        TopMsg "Нет данных после фильтрации!", "Внимание", _
            MB_ICONEXCLAMATION
        Exit Sub
    End If

    Dim seamNumberRange As String

    ReDim rawCol0(1 To totalExcelRows)
    ReDim rawCol1(1 To totalExcelRows)
    ReDim rawCol2(1 To totalExcelRows)
    ReDim rawCol3(1 To totalExcelRows)
    ReDim rawNum(1 To totalExcelRows)

    For r = 1 To totalExcelRows
        rawCol0(r) = filtCol0(r)
        rawCol1(r) = filtCol1(r)
        rawCol2(r) = filtCol2(r)
        rawCol3(r) = filtCol3(r)
        rawNum(r) = filtNum(r)
    Next r

    fillStage = "Определение диапазона сварных швов"
    seamNumberRange = FindSeamNumberRange(rawCol0, rawCol1, totalExcelRows)
    fillStage = "Подстановка диапазона сварных швов"
    UpdateSeamPhraseInRaw rawCol0, totalExcelRows, seamNumberRange

    fillStage = "Определение границ пунктов"
    ' === ГРУППЫ ===
    Dim grpAssign() As Long
    ReDim grpAssign(1 To totalExcelRows)
    Dim curGrp As Long
    curGrp = 0

    For r = 1 To totalExcelRows
        If Len(rawNum(r)) > 0 And rawNum(r) <> "0" _
            And rawNum(r) <> "ОК" Then
            curGrp = curGrp + 1
        End If
        If curGrp = 0 Then
            If Len(rawCol0(r) & rawCol1(r) & rawCol2(r) & rawCol3(r)) > 0 Then _
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2515, , _
                "Перед первым пунктом есть строка без номера группы в A. Укажите номер начала пункта."
        End If
        grpAssign(r) = curGrp
    Next r

    Dim totalGroups As Long
    totalGroups = curGrp

    If totalGroups = 0 Then
        TopMsg "Не найдено позиций!", "Внимание", MB_ICONEXCLAMATION
        Exit Sub
    End If

    ' Измерение в SolidWorks до удаления прежних ОК и изменения рабочих таблиц.
    fillStage = "Создание временной таблицы для измерения текста"
    Set wrapProbe = CreateWrapProbe(swModel)

    ' === ВИЗУАЛЬНЫЕ СТРОКИ С ПЕРЕНОСОМ ДЛИННОГО ТЕКСТА ===
    Dim visRows() As VisRow
    Dim visCount As Long
    visCount = 0
    ReDim visRows(1 To (totalExcelRows * 6) + totalGroups)

    Dim g As Long
    For g = 1 To totalGroups
        Dim grpRowStart As Long, grpRowEnd As Long
        grpRowStart = 0: grpRowEnd = 0

        For r = 1 To totalExcelRows
            If grpAssign(r) = g Then
                If grpRowStart = 0 Then grpRowStart = r
                grpRowEnd = r
            End If
        Next r

        If grpRowStart = 0 Then GoTo NextGroup

        fillStage = "Перенос пункта " & CStr(g) & "; строки Excel " & CStr(grpRowStart + 6) & "–" & CStr(grpRowEnd + 6)
        KC14_RenderGroup rawCol0, rawCol1, rawCol2, rawCol3, grpRowStart, grpRowEnd, g, wrapProbe, visRows, visCount
        If g < totalGroups Then KC14_AddVisual visRows, visCount, g, "", "", "", "", True
NextGroup:
    Next g

    DropWrapProbe swModel, wrapProbe
    fillStage = "Проверка вместимости фиксированных строк ОК1"
    ValidateFixedOK1Text swModel, ok1Data
    fillStage = "Проверка вместимости текста исполнений"
    If variantEnabled Then KC15_PreflightVariant swModel, variantText
    If gKC23HeaderReady Then
        fillStage = "Проверка шаблона шапки ОК1/КЭ1 (14 пт, 4,25 мм)"
        KC23_PreflightHeader swModel
    End If

    ' === ЕДИНАЯ ПАГИНАЦИЯ: ОК1 — 8 строк операций; ОК2+ — 16 ===
    Dim visPage() As Long, totalPages As Long, splitGroups As Long
    Dim vi As Long
    fillStage = "Распределение пунктов по листам ОК"
    PaginateUnifiedRows visRows, visCount, visPage, totalPages, splitGroups
    fillStage = "Подготовка строк первого листа ОК1"
    PutFirstPageRows ok1Data, visRows, visPage, visCount
    Dim continuationPages As Long, continuationNames As String
    continuationPages = totalPages - 1
    If continuationPages > 0 Then
        continuationNames = "ОК2 - ОК" & CStr(totalPages)
    Else
        continuationNames = "не требуются, все операции помещаются на ОК1"
    End If

    fillStage = "Повторная проверка состава КЭ перед обновлением ОК"
    If KC37_RegularSnapshot(swDraw) <> keLayoutBefore Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3710, , _
        "Состав КЭ изменился после чтения карты. Выполните режим 1 первого макроса. ОК не обновлены."

    fillStage = "Подтверждение обновления комплекта ОК"
    ' === ПОДТВЕРЖДЕНИЕ ===
    If Not TopYesNo("Обновить комплект ОК в '" & swModel.GetTitle & "'?" & vbCrLf & _
        "Листов ОК: " & totalPages & "; То/Тв: " & FormatSumValue(totalTimeExcel) & "." & vbCrLf & _
        "Удаляются прежние листы: " & KC14_DeletePreview(swDraw) & "." & vbCrLf & _
        "ОК1 обновляется; КЭ швов пересоздаются при изменении номеров/обозначений или по запросу. Общего отката нет.", "Обновление ОК") Then Exit Sub
    replacementApproved = True

    ' После подтверждения ещё раз проверяем имена; самостоятельной перенумерации КЭ здесь нет.
    If KC37_RegularSnapshot(swDraw) <> keLayoutBefore Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3710, , _
        "Состав КЭ изменился во время подтверждения. Сначала выполните режим 1 первого макроса."

    ' До любых изменений рамок подтверждаем и удаляем прежние ОК2 и далее.
    fillStage = "Подготовка замены прежних листов ОК"
    If Not PrepareOKSheetsForReplacement(swDraw, swModel, insertAfterSheet) Then Exit Sub

    ' Сначала создаём/обновляем ОК1 и его данные, затем реквизиты и номера швов.
    fillStage = "Создание или обновление листа ОК1"
    EnsureOK1SheetFromTemplate swDraw, swModel, insertAfterSheet
    fillStage = "Заполнение таблицы ОК1"
    BuildOK1DataTable swDraw, swModel, ok1Data
    If Not InsertBorderlessCell(swModel, OK1_MASS_X, OK1_MASS_Y, OK1_MASS_W, OK1_MASS_H, _
        partMassText, "Масса детали") Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2428, , "Не обновлена масса детали на ОК1."

    fillStage = "Заполнение рамок и реквизитов"
    ' === АВТОЗАПОЛНЕНИЕ ТИТУЛЬНОГО ЛИСТА И ОК1 ===
    FillSpecialSheet swDraw, swModel, "Титульный лист", detailName, kdNumber, _
        hdrDeciNum, kd02102, primary60102, True
    FillSpecialSheet swDraw, swModel, "ОК1", detailName, kdNumber, _
        hdrDeciNum, kd02102, primary60102, False
    CheckOK1LayoutAfterSeamUpdate swDraw

    ' =============================================
    ' СОЗДАНИЕ ЛИСТОВ
    ' =============================================

    Dim refSheet As Object
    Set refSheet = swDraw.GetCurrentSheet
    Dim refProps As Variant
    refProps = refSheet.GetProperties()
    Dim paperW As Double, paperH As Double
    paperW = refProps(5)
    paperH = refProps(6)

    Dim pageNum As Long
    Dim createdSheets As Long: createdSheets = 0
    Dim createdSheetNames() As String
    If continuationPages > 0 Then ReDim createdSheetNames(1 To continuationPages)

    For pageNum = 2 To totalPages
        fillStage = "Создание и заполнение листа ОК" & CStr(pageNum)
        Dim rowsThisPage As Long: rowsThisPage = 0
        For vi = 1 To visCount
            If visPage(vi) = pageNum Then rowsThisPage = rowsThisPage + 1
        Next vi
        If rowsThisPage = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2516, , "Пустой лист продолжения в плане."
        If rowsThisPage > MAX_ROWS_PER_PAGE Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2517, , "Переполнение листа продолжения в плане."

        Dim tableRowCount As Long
        tableRowCount = MAX_ROWS_PER_PAGE

        Dim sheetNum As Long
        sheetNum = pageNum

        Dim sheetName As String
        sheetName = "ОК" & sheetNum

        CreateMacroSheetChecked swDraw, sheetName, templatePath, useTemplate, paperW, paperH

        createdSheets = createdSheets + 1
        createdSheetNames(createdSheets) = sheetName

        ' === ТАБЛИЦА 1 ===
        Dim swHdr1 As Object
        On Error Resume Next
        Set swHdr1 = swModel.Extension.InsertGeneralTableAnnotation( _
            True, HDR1_X, HDR1_Y, 1, "", 1, 2)
        If Not swHdr1 Is Nothing Then
            swHdr1.SetColumnWidth 0, HDR1_COL1_W, 0
            swHdr1.SetColumnWidth 1, HDR1_COL2_W, 0
            swHdr1.SetRowHeight 0, HDR1_ROW_H, 0
            swHdr1.Text2(0, 0, False) = hdrDeciNum
            swHdr1.Text2(0, 1, False) = CStr(sheetNum)
            ApplyMacroTableFont swHdr1
            swHdr1.SetRowHeight 0, HDR1_ROW_H, 0
            swHdr1.CellTextHorizontalJustification(0, 0) = 2
            swHdr1.CellTextHorizontalJustification(0, 1) = 2
            swHdr1.BorderLineWeight = -1
            swHdr1.GridLineWeight = -1
            Dim swHdr1Ann As Object
            Set swHdr1Ann = swHdr1.GetAnnotation
            If Not swHdr1Ann Is Nothing Then
                swHdr1Ann.SetPosition2 HDR1_X, HDR1_Y, 0
            End If
        End If
        On Error GoTo ErrHandler

        ' === ТАБЛИЦА 2 ===
        Dim swHdr2 As Object
        On Error Resume Next
        Set swHdr2 = swModel.Extension.InsertGeneralTableAnnotation( _
            True, HDR2_X, HDR2_Y, 1, "", 1, 2)
        If Not swHdr2 Is Nothing Then
            swHdr2.SetColumnWidth 0, HDR2_COL1_W, 0
            swHdr2.SetColumnWidth 1, HDR2_COL2_W, 0
            swHdr2.SetRowHeight 0, HDR2_ROW_H, 0
            swHdr2.Text2(0, 0, False) = hdrPartNum1
            swHdr2.Text2(0, 1, False) = hdrPartNum2
            ApplyMacroTableFont swHdr2
            swHdr2.SetRowHeight 0, HDR2_ROW_H, 0
            swHdr2.CellTextHorizontalJustification(0, 0) = 2
