            swHdr2.CellTextHorizontalJustification(0, 1) = 2
            swHdr2.BorderLineWeight = -1
            swHdr2.GridLineWeight = -1
            Dim swHdr2Ann As Object
            Set swHdr2Ann = swHdr2.GetAnnotation
            If Not swHdr2Ann Is Nothing Then
                swHdr2Ann.SetPosition2 HDR2_X, HDR2_Y, 0
            End If
        End If
        On Error GoTo ErrHandler

        ' === ОСНОВНАЯ ТАБЛИЦА: без пропуска ошибок и обрезания лишних строк ===
        Dim swTableAnn As Object, swTblAnnotation As Object, ri As Long, aRow As Long
        Dim tblRow As Long
        swModel.ClearSelection2 True
        Set swTableAnn = swModel.Extension.InsertGeneralTableAnnotation( _
            False, tableX, tableY, anchorType, "", tableRowCount, NUM_COLS)
        If swTableAnn Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2518, , "Не создана таблица " & sheetName
        swTableAnn.Anchored = False
        swTableAnn.SetColumnWidth 0, COL2_W, 0
        swTableAnn.SetColumnWidth 1, COL3_W, 0
        swTableAnn.SetColumnWidth 2, COL4_W, 0
        swTableAnn.SetColumnWidth 3, COL5_W, 0
        If Not ApplyOperationTableFont(swTableAnn) Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2519, , "Не задан шрифт " & sheetName
        tblRow = 0
        For vi = 1 To visCount
            If visPage(vi) = pageNum Then
                If tblRow >= tableRowCount Then _
                    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2520, , "Попытка обрезать строку на " & sheetName
                swTableAnn.Text2(tblRow, 0, False) = visRows(vi).col0
                swTableAnn.Text2(tblRow, 1, False) = visRows(vi).col1
                swTableAnn.Text2(tblRow, 2, False) = visRows(vi).Col2
                swTableAnn.Text2(tblRow, 3, False) = visRows(vi).Col3
                If FrameTextComparable(CStr(swTableAnn.Text2(tblRow, 0, False))) <> FrameTextComparable(visRows(vi).col0) Or _
                    FrameTextComparable(CStr(swTableAnn.Text2(tblRow, 1, False))) <> FrameTextComparable(visRows(vi).col1) Or _
                    FrameTextComparable(CStr(swTableAnn.Text2(tblRow, 2, False))) <> FrameTextComparable(visRows(vi).Col2) Or _
                    FrameTextComparable(CStr(swTableAnn.Text2(tblRow, 3, False))) <> FrameTextComparable(visRows(vi).Col3) Then _
                    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2529, , "Не подтверждено содержимое строки на " & sheetName
                tblRow = tblRow + 1
            End If
        Next vi
        If tblRow <> rowsThisPage Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2521, , "Не все строки записаны на " & sheetName
        For aRow = 0 To tableRowCount - 1
            swTableAnn.CellTextHorizontalJustification(aRow, 0) = 1
            swTableAnn.CellTextHorizontalJustification(aRow, 1) = 1
            swTableAnn.CellTextHorizontalJustification(aRow, 2) = 2
            swTableAnn.CellTextHorizontalJustification(aRow, 3) = 2
            swTableAnn.SetRowHeight aRow, ROW_H, 0
            If Abs(swTableAnn.GetRowHeight(aRow) - ROW_H) > 0.00005 Then _
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2522, , _
                "Текст не помещается в строку " & CStr(aRow + 1) & " на " & sheetName & "."
        Next aRow
        swTableAnn.BorderLineWeight = -1
        swTableAnn.GridLineWeight = -1
        Set swTblAnnotation = swTableAnn.GetAnnotation
        If Not swTblAnnotation.SetPosition2(tableX, tableY, 0) Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2523, , "Не задано положение таблицы " & sheetName
        SetTableName swTableAnn, "Таблица данных" & CStr(sheetNum)
        swModel.ClearSelection2 True

NextPage:
    Next pageNum

    ' =========================================================
    ' ПЕРЕМЕЩЕНИЕ ЛИСТОВ В НУЖНУЮ ПОЗИЦИЮ ЧЕРТЕЖА
    ' =========================================================
    If Len(insertAfterSheet) > 0 And createdSheets > 0 Then
        If createdSheets < continuationPages Then
            ReDim Preserve createdSheetNames(1 To createdSheets)
        End If
        
        MoveSheetsAfterTarget swDraw, insertAfterSheet, createdSheetNames
    End If

    ' === СОЗДАНИЕ КАРТ КЭ ШВОВ И ЗАПОЛНЕНИЕ ВСЕХ ЛИСТОВ КЭ ===
    RunIntegratedKE xlFilePath, swApp, swModel, swDraw, detailName, kdNumber, hdrDeciNum, insertAfterSheet
    MoveArchivedKEToEnd swDraw
    ' Получаем окончательный список только после создания и расстановки ОК/КЭ.
    ' Без фильтра имён: титульный, исходные, ОК, КЭ и архивные листы.
    fillStage = "Получение всех листов для таблиц исполнений"
    variantSheets = swDraw.GetSheetNames
    If Not KC15_TryArrayBounds(variantSheets, variantFirst, variantLast) Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3172, , "Не получен список листов для таблиц исполнений."
    For variantSheetIndex = variantFirst To variantLast
        variantSheetName = CStr(variantSheets(variantSheetIndex))
        fillStage = "Таблица исполнений на листе: " & variantSheetName
        If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(swDraw, variantSheetName) Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3160, , "Не активирован лист для таблицы исполнений: " & variantSheetName
        ' enabled=False также проходит все листы и удаляет лишь собственные таблицы.
        KC15_UpdateVariant swModel, variantEnabled, variantText, variantSheetName
    Next variantSheetIndex

    fillStage = "Обновление количества листов и суммарного времени"
    ' === КОЛИЧЕСТВО ЛИСТОВ В ТИТУЛЬНОМ ЛИСТЕ И ОК1 ===
    FillCountTables swDraw, swModel
    FillSumTableOnOK1 swDraw, swModel, totalTimeExcel

    ' Номера страниц не создаём и не изменяем.

    If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(swDraw, "ОК1") Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2524, , "Не активирован ОК1 после заполнения."

    swModel.ForceRebuild3 True
    swModel.GraphicsRedraw2

    KC14_Log swModel.GetPathName, "ОК: листов " & totalPages & "; время " & FormatSumValue(totalTimeExcel) & vbCrLf & macroWarnings
    TopMsg "Готово. Листов ОК: " & totalPages & "; время: " & FormatSumValue(totalTimeExcel) & "." & _
        IIf(Len(macroWarnings) > 0, vbCrLf & "Есть замечания: " & macroWarnings, ""), "Карта контроля", MB_ICONINFORMATION

    Exit Sub

ErrHandler:
    mainError = "Ошибка " & Err.number & ": " & Err.description & vbCrLf & _
        "Этап: " & fillStage & vbCrLf & "Источник: " & Err.source
    On Error Resume Next
    logDrawing = swModel.GetPathName
    KC14_Log logDrawing, mainError
    On Error GoTo 0
    TopMsg mainError & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
        "Уже удалено прежних ОК: " & deletedOKCount & vbCrLf & _
        "Архивировано КЭ: " & archivedKECount & vbCrLf & _
        "Операция не имеет общего отката. Проверьте чертёж; не сохраняйте его поверх резервной копии.", _
        "Ошибка", MB_ICONCRITICAL
    On Error Resume Next
    DropWrapProbe swModel, wrapProbe
    KC14_Log swModel.GetPathName, "Ошибка заполнения ОК; книгу не закрываем. " & macroWarnings
    Set xlWB = Nothing: Set xlApp = Nothing
    On Error GoTo 0
End Sub





' ============================================
' ФОРМАТИРОВАНИЕ ЧИСЛА
' ============================================
Private Function FormatNum(val As Double) As String
    Dim s As String
    s = CStr(val)
    If InStr(s, ",") > 0 Or InStr(s, ".") > 0 Then
        Do While Right(s, 1) = "0"
            s = Left(s, Len(s) - 1)
        Loop
        If Right(s, 1) = "," Or Right(s, 1) = "." Then
            s = Left(s, Len(s) - 1)
        End If
    End If
    FormatNum = s
End Function

' Единое правило для обозначения, размеров, рисунка и группировки.
Private Function KE_IsStandardWeld(w As UnifiedWeld) As Boolean
    If InStr(1, w.WeldStandard, "нестандарт", vbTextCompare) > 0 Then Exit Function
    If InStr(1, w.weldType, "нестандарт", vbTextCompare) > 0 Then Exit Function
    If Len(Trim$(w.WeldStandard)) = 0 Or Len(Trim$(w.weldType)) = 0 Then Exit Function
    KE_IsStandardWeld = (InStr(1, w.WeldStandard, "14771", vbTextCompare) > 0 Or _
        InStr(1, w.WeldStandard, "23518", vbTextCompare) > 0)
End Function

Private Function KE_BuildWeldPictureText(w As UnifiedWeld) As String
    If Not KE_IsStandardWeld(w) Then KE_BuildWeldPictureText = Trim$(w.ViewReference)
End Function

' Ищем фактические заголовки, а не угадываем значение по другим столбцам.
Private Function KE_ExcelHeaderColumn(ByVal ws As Object, ByVal headerText As String) As Long
    Dim lastCol As Long, col As Long, actual As String, expected As String, value As Variant
    lastCol = ws.cells(1, ws.Columns.count).End(-4159).Column
    expected = Replace(UCase$(Trim$(headerText)), "Ё", "Е")
    For col = 1 To lastCol
        value = ws.cells(1, col).Value2
        If Not IsError(value) Then
            actual = Replace(UCase$(Trim$(Replace(CStr(value), ChrW(160), " "))), "Ё", "Е")
            If actual = expected Then
                KE_ExcelHeaderColumn = col
                Exit Function
            End If
        End If
    Next col
End Function

Private Function KE_ExcelViewReference(ByVal ws As Object, ByVal row As Long, _
    ByVal viewCol As Long, ByVal sketchCol As Long) As String
    Dim value As Variant, result As String
    If viewCol > 0 Then
        value = ws.cells(row, viewCol).Value2
        If Not IsError(value) Then result = Trim$(Replace(CStr(value), ChrW(160), " "))
    End If
    If Len(result) = 0 And sketchCol > 0 Then
        value = ws.cells(row, sketchCol).Value2
        If Not IsError(value) Then result = Trim$(Replace(CStr(value), ChrW(160), " "))
    End If
    KE_ExcelViewReference = result
End Function

' Возвращает одну подтверждённую таблицу швов текущего листа.
Private Function KE_OneWeldTableOnSheet(ByVal drawing As Object) As Object
    Dim view As Object, tables As Variant, table As Object, found As Object, seen As Object
    Dim first As Long, last As Long, i As Long, matches As Long, featureName As String
    Set seen = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Set view = drawing.GetFirstView
    Do While Not view Is Nothing
        tables = view.GetTableAnnotations
        If KC15_TryArrayBounds(tables, first, last) Then
            For i = first To last
                Set table = tables(i)
                If KC22_WeldHeaders(table) Then
                    featureName = FrameTableFeatureName(table)
                    If Len(featureName) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2740, , "Не определён Feature таблицы швов."
                    If Not seen.Exists(featureName) Then
                        seen.Add featureName, True
                        matches = matches + 1
                        Set found = table
                    End If
                End If
            Next i
        End If
        Set view = view.GetNextView
    Loop
    If matches <> 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2741, , "Найдено таблиц швов: " & CStr(matches) & ". Ожидается одна."
    Set KE_OneWeldTableOnSheet = found
End Function

' Вызывается ТОЛЬКО после совпадения номеров/обозначений существующих листов.
' Меняет текст двух ячеек нестандартных швов. Картинки/варианты/обычные размеры не трогает.
Private Sub KE_RefreshNonstandardCells(ByVal drawing As Object, _
    ByRef welds() As UnifiedWeld, ByVal count As Long)
    Dim i As Long, anyNonstandard As Boolean, pageHasNonstandard As Boolean
    Dim originalSheet As String, sheetName As String, table As Object
    Dim perPage As Long, pages As Long, page As Long, firstIdx As Long, lastIdx As Long
    Dim rowsData As Long, localIdx As Long, row As Long, col As Long, changed As Boolean
    Dim pictureText As String, sizeText As String, failure As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    For i = 1 To count
        If Not KE_IsStandardWeld(welds(i)) Then
            anyNonstandard = True
            Exit For
        End If
    Next i
    If Not anyNonstandard Then Exit Sub
    originalSheet = drawing.GetCurrentSheet.GetName
    perPage = KE_ROWS_PER_PAGE * KE_GROUPS_COUNT
    pages = (count + perPage - 1) \ perPage
    For page = 1 To pages
        firstIdx = (page - 1) * perPage + 1
        lastIdx = firstIdx + perPage - 1
        If lastIdx > count Then lastIdx = count
        pageHasNonstandard = False
        For i = firstIdx To lastIdx
            If Not KE_IsStandardWeld(welds(i)) Then pageHasNonstandard = True
        Next i
        If pageHasNonstandard Then
            sheetName = "КЭ (Швы Лист " & CStr(page) & ")"
            If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(drawing, sheetName) Then _
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2742, , "Не активирован " & sheetName & "."
            Set table = KE_OneWeldTableOnSheet(drawing)
            rowsData = (lastIdx - firstIdx + 1 + KE_GROUPS_COUNT - 1) \ KE_GROUPS_COUNT
            If table.rowCount <> rowsData + 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2743, , "Не совпало число строк на " & sheetName & "."
            changed = False
            For i = firstIdx To lastIdx
                If Not KE_IsStandardWeld(welds(i)) Then
                    localIdx = i - firstIdx
                    row = (localIdx Mod rowsData) + 1
                    col = (localIdx \ rowsData) * 4
                    ' Повторная проверка защищает от записи в чужую строку.
                    If KC22_WeldText(CStr(table.Text2(row, col, False))) <> KC22_WeldText(welds(i).NumberLabel) Then _
                        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2744, , "Изменился номер строки на " & sheetName & "."
                    If KC22_WeldText(CStr(table.Text2(row, col + 1, False))) <> KC22_WeldText(BuildWeldDesignation(welds(i))) Then _
                        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2745, , "Изменилось обозначение строки на " & sheetName & "."
                    pictureText = KE_BuildWeldPictureText(welds(i))
                    sizeText = BuildSizeText(welds(i))
                    ' Если ссылка в Excel пуста, существующий ручной текст не стираем.
                    If Len(pictureText) > 0 Then
                        If KC22_WeldText(CStr(table.Text2(row, col + 2, False))) <> KC22_WeldText(pictureText) Then
                            table.Text2(row, col + 2, False) = pictureText
                            table.CellTextHorizontalJustification(row, col + 2) = 2
                            table.CellTextVerticalJustification(row, col + 2) = swTextAlignmentMiddle
                            changed = True
                        End If
                    End If
                    If KC22_WeldText(CStr(table.Text2(row, col + 3, False))) <> KC22_WeldText(sizeText) Then
                        table.Text2(row, col + 3, False) = sizeText
                        table.CellTextHorizontalJustification(row, col + 3) = 2
                        table.CellTextVerticalJustification(row, col + 3) = swTextAlignmentMiddle
                        changed = True
                    End If
                End If
            Next i
            If changed Then
                keSwModel.SetSaveFlag
                keSwModel.GraphicsRedraw2
            End If
        End If
    Next page
    If Len(originalSheet) > 0 Then
        If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(drawing, originalSheet) Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2746, , "Не восстановлен активный лист после обновления текста."
    End If
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    failure = "Текст нестандартных швов обновлён не полностью: " & Err.description
    AddMacroWarning failure
    On Error Resume Next
    If Len(originalSheet) > 0 Then drawing.ActivateSheet originalSheet
    On Error GoTo 0
End Sub

' ============================================
' ТЕКСТ РАЗМЕРОВ
' ============================================
Private Function BuildSizeText(w As UnifiedWeld) As String
    BuildSizeText = ""
    If Not KE_IsStandardWeld(w) Then
        BuildSizeText = "Согласно рис."
        Exit Function
    End If
    If w.weldLeg <= 0 Then
        BuildSizeText = KC26_SizeTemplate(w)
        Exit Function
    End If

    Dim result As String
    result = "К=" & FormatNum(w.weldLeg)

    If w.tolPlus <> 0 Or w.tolMinus <> 0 Then
        result = result & "("
        If w.tolPlus > 0 Then
            result = result & "+" & FormatNum(w.tolPlus)
        ElseIf w.tolPlus < 0 Then
            result = result & FormatNum(w.tolPlus)
        End If
        If w.tolMinus <> 0 Then
            result = result & "/"
            If w.tolMinus > 0 Then
                result = result & "+" & FormatNum(w.tolMinus)
            Else
                result = result & FormatNum(w.tolMinus)
            End If
        End If
        result = result & ")"
    End If

    BuildSizeText = result
End Function

' ============================================
' ОБОЗНАЧЕНИЕ ШВА
' ============================================
Private Function BuildWeldDesignation(w As UnifiedWeld) As String
    Dim result As String
    result = ""

    If KE_IsStandardWeld(w) Then
        result = w.WeldStandard & Chr(10) & w.weldType
        If InStr(1, w.WeldStandard, "14771") > 0 And w.weldLeg > 0 Then
            result = result & "-к" & FormatNum(w.weldLeg)
        End If
    Else
        result = "Нестандартный"
    End If

    BuildWeldDesignation = result
End Function

' ============================================
' ГРУППИРОВКА ШВОВ ПО ТИПАМ
' ============================================
Private Sub GroupWeldsByType(ByRef welds() As UnifiedWeld, _
                              cnt As Long, _
                              ByRef grouped() As UnifiedWeld, _
                              ByRef grpCnt As Long)
    grpCnt = 0
    ReDim grouped(1 To cnt)

    Dim used() As Boolean
    ReDim used(1 To cnt)
    Dim i As Long
    Dim j As Long

    For i = 1 To cnt
        If used(i) Then GoTo NextWeld

        If Not KE_IsStandardWeld(welds(i)) Or Not ShouldGroupWeld(welds(i).weldType) Then
            grpCnt = grpCnt + 1
            grouped(grpCnt) = welds(i)
            used(i) = True
            GoTo NextWeld
        End If

        Dim numbers() As Long
        Dim numCnt As Long
        numCnt = 0
        ReDim numbers(1 To cnt)

        For j = i To cnt
            If used(j) Then GoTo NextJ
            If WeldsAreIdentical(welds(i), welds(j)) Then
                numCnt = numCnt + 1
                numbers(numCnt) = welds(j).number
                used(j) = True
            End If
NextJ:
        Next j

        grpCnt = grpCnt + 1
        grouped(grpCnt) = welds(i)
        ReDim Preserve numbers(1 To numCnt)
        grouped(grpCnt).NumberLabel = BuildNumberRange(numbers, numCnt)

NextWeld:
    Next i

    If grpCnt > 0 Then ReDim Preserve grouped(1 To grpCnt)
End Sub

' ============================================
' ПРОВЕРКА: НУЖНО ЛИ ГРУППИРОВАТЬ
' ============================================
Private Function ShouldGroupWeld(weldType As String) As Boolean
    ShouldGroupWeld = (weldType = "Т1" Or weldType = "Т3" Or weldType = "Н1")
End Function

' ============================================
' ПРОВЕРКА ИДЕНТИЧНОСТИ ШВОВ
' ============================================
Private Function WeldsAreIdentical(w1 As UnifiedWeld, w2 As UnifiedWeld) As Boolean
    WeldsAreIdentical = False
    If w1.WeldStandard <> w2.WeldStandard Then Exit Function
    If w1.weldType <> w2.weldType Then Exit Function
    If Abs(w1.weldLeg - w2.weldLeg) > 0.01 Then Exit Function
    If Abs(w1.tolPlus - w2.tolPlus) > 0.01 Then Exit Function
    If Abs(w1.tolMinus - w2.tolMinus) > 0.01 Then Exit Function
    WeldsAreIdentical = True
End Function

' ============================================
' ФОРМИРОВАНИЕ ДИАПАЗОНА НОМЕРОВ
' ============================================
Private Function BuildNumberRange(nums() As Long, cnt As Long) As String
    If cnt = 0 Then BuildNumberRange = "": Exit Function
    If cnt = 1 Then BuildNumberRange = CStr(nums(1)): Exit Function

    Dim i As Long
    Dim j As Long
    Dim tmp As Long
    For i = 2 To cnt
        tmp = nums(i)
        j = i - 1
        Do While j >= 1
            If nums(j) > tmp Then
                nums(j + 1) = nums(j)
                j = j - 1
            Else
                Exit Do
            End If
        Loop
        nums(j + 1) = tmp
    Next i

    Dim result As String: result = ""
    Dim rangeStart As Long: rangeStart = nums(1)
    Dim rangeEnd As Long: rangeEnd = nums(1)

    For i = 2 To cnt
        If nums(i) = rangeEnd + 1 Then
            rangeEnd = nums(i)
        Else
            If Len(result) > 0 Then result = result & ", "
            If rangeStart = rangeEnd Then
                result = result & CStr(rangeStart)
            ElseIf rangeEnd = rangeStart + 1 Then
                result = result & CStr(rangeStart) & ", " & CStr(rangeEnd)
            Else
                result = result & CStr(rangeStart) & "-" & CStr(rangeEnd)
            End If
            rangeStart = nums(i)
            rangeEnd = nums(i)
        End If
    Next i

    If Len(result) > 0 Then result = result & ", "
    If rangeStart = rangeEnd Then
        result = result & CStr(rangeStart)
    ElseIf rangeEnd = rangeStart + 1 Then
        result = result & CStr(rangeStart) & ", " & CStr(rangeEnd)
    Else
        result = result & CStr(rangeStart) & "-" & CStr(rangeEnd)
    End If

    BuildNumberRange = result
End Function

Private Function LoadKEWeldsFromExcel(ByVal filePath As String, _
    ByRef welds() As UnifiedWeld, ByRef cnt As Long) As Boolean
    LoadKEWeldsFromExcel = False
    cnt = 0

    Dim xlApp As Object, xlWB As Object, ws As Object, wsObj As Object
    On Error GoTo LoadError
    Set xlApp = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
    xlApp.Visible = False
    Set xlWB = xlApp.Workbooks.Open(filePath)

    For Each wsObj In xlWB.sheets
        If StrComp(wsObj.name, "Данные из чертежа", vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
            Set ws = wsObj
            Exit For
        End If
    Next wsObj
    If ws Is Nothing Then
        TopMsg "В выбранном Excel не найден лист 'Данные из чертежа'. Карты КЭ не созданы.", _
            "КЭ", MB_ICONEXCLAMATION
        GoTo CleanExit
    End If

    Dim lastRow As Long, r As Long, viewRefCol As Long, sketchRefCol As Long
    viewRefCol = KE_ExcelHeaderColumn(ws, "Чертёжный вид")
    sketchRefCol = KE_ExcelHeaderColumn(ws, "Ссылка эскиз/вид")
    lastRow = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
    If lastRow < 2 Then GoTo CleanExit

    ReDim welds(1 To lastRow)
    For r = 2 To lastRow
        Dim cellB As String
        cellB = Trim$(CStr(ws.cells(r, 2).value))
        If StrComp(cellB, "Сварка", vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then GoTo NextWeldRow

        Dim posVar As Variant
        posVar = ws.cells(r, 1).value
        If Not IsNumeric(posVar) Then GoTo NextWeldRow

        cnt = cnt + 1
        With welds(cnt)
            .number = CLng(posVar)
            .WeldStandard = Trim$(CStr(ws.cells(r, 12).value))
            .weldType = Trim$(CStr(ws.cells(r, 13).value))
            .weldLeg = 0
            If IsNumeric(ws.cells(r, 14).value) Then .weldLeg = CDbl(ws.cells(r, 14).value)
            .tolPlus = 0
            If IsNumeric(ws.cells(r, 15).value) Then .tolPlus = CDbl(ws.cells(r, 15).value)
            .tolMinus = 0
            If IsNumeric(ws.cells(r, 16).value) Then .tolMinus = CDbl(ws.cells(r, 16).value)
            .toolName = Trim$(CStr(ws.cells(r, 17).value))
            .rawText = Trim$(CStr(ws.cells(r, 3).value))
            .volume = Trim$(CStr(ws.cells(r, 19).value))
            .quantity = Trim$(CStr(ws.cells(r, 21).value))
            .NumberLabel = CStr(.number)
            .PicFile = ""
            .ViewReference = KE_ExcelViewReference(ws, r, viewRefCol, sketchRefCol)
        End With
        If Not KE_IsStandardWeld(welds(cnt)) Then
            If Len(welds(cnt).ViewReference) = 0 Then
                AddMacroWarning "Шов " & welds(cnt).NumberLabel & _
                    ": в Excel не указаны «Чертёжный вид» или «Ссылка эскиз/вид». Ячейка «Рис.» не заполнена автоматически."
            End If
        End If
NextWeldRow:
    Next r

    If cnt > 0 Then
        ReDim Preserve welds(1 To cnt)
        LoadKEWeldsFromExcel = True
    End If

CleanExit:
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not xlWB Is Nothing Then xlWB.Close False
    If Not xlApp Is Nothing Then xlApp.Quit
    Set xlWB = Nothing
    Set xlApp = Nothing
    On Error GoTo 0
    Exit Function

LoadError:
    TopMsg "Ошибка загрузки листа 'Данные из чертежа': " & Err.description, _
        "КЭ", MB_ICONEXCLAMATION
    Resume CleanExit
End Function

' ============================================
' СОЗДАНИЕ ЛИСТА КЭ
' ============================================
Private Function CreateKESheet(sheetName As String, _
                                templatePath As String, _
                                useTemplate As Boolean, _
                                paperW As Double, _
                                paperH As Double) As Boolean
    CreateMacroSheetChecked keSwDraw, sheetName, templatePath, useTemplate, paperW, paperH
    CreateKESheet = True
End Function


' ============================================
' ОБЪЕДИНЁННОЕ СОЗДАНИЕ КЭ И КЭ ШВОВ
' ============================================
Private Sub RunIntegratedKE(ByVal filePath As String, ByVal appObj As Object, _
    ByVal modelObj As Object, ByVal drawObj As Object, _
    ByVal detailName As String, ByVal kdNumber As String, _
    ByVal primary02190 As String, ByRef insertAfterSheet As String)

    Set keSwApp = appObj
    Set keSwModel = modelObj
    Set keSwDraw = drawObj
    Set gKePicIndex = Nothing: Set gKePicVariants = Nothing: gKePicFolderWarned = False
    gKePicLogPath = "": gKePicLogReported = False

    Dim allWelds() As UnifiedWeld, groupedWelds() As UnifiedWeld
    Dim totalWelds As Long, groupedCount As Long, comparison As Long, reason As String
    Dim forceRebuild As Boolean
    If Not LoadKEWeldsFromExcel(filePath, allWelds, totalWelds) Then
        ' Ошибка чтения или отсутствие сварок не является разрешением удалять старые листы.
        FillAllKESheetHeaders detailName, kdNumber, primary02190
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If totalWelds = 0 Then
        FillAllKESheetHeaders detailName, kdNumber, primary02190
        Exit Sub
    End If
    GroupWeldsByType allWelds, totalWelds, groupedWelds, groupedCount
    If groupedCount = 0 Then
        FillAllKESheetHeaders detailName, kdNumber, primary02190
        Exit Sub
    End If

    forceRebuild = (KC14_Setting(keSwModel.GetPathName, "ForceKEWeldRebuild", "0") = "1")
    comparison = KC22_CompareWeldSheets(keSwDraw, groupedWelds, groupedCount, reason)
    If comparison = 1 And Not forceRebuild Then
        ' Обновляем только текст нестандартных швов; ручные картинки стандартных сохраняются.
        KE_RefreshNonstandardCells keSwDraw, groupedWelds, groupedCount
        KC33_RefreshSizeNotation keSwDraw, groupedWelds, groupedCount
        FillAllKESheetHeaders detailName, kdNumber, primary02190
        Exit Sub
    End If
    If comparison = 3 And Not forceRebuild Then
        If Not TopYesNo(reason & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
            "Архивировать оставшиеся действующие листы швов и создать полный комплект заново?" & vbCrLf & _
            "Да — пересоздать. Нет — сохранить существующие листы швов без изменений." & vbCrLf & _
            "Комплект ОК уже обновлён; рамки и таблицы исполнений продолжат обновляться.", "Проверка комплекта КЭ швов") Then
            AddMacroWarning "КЭ швов не пересозданы: " & reason
            FillAllKESheetHeaders detailName, kdNumber, primary02190
            Exit Sub
        End If
    End If
    ' Шаблон требуется только для реального создания, но не для сохранения совпавшего комплекта.
    If Len(Dir$(KE_TEMPLATE_PATH)) = 0 Then
        FillAllKESheetHeaders detailName, kdNumber, primary02190
        AddMacroWarning "КЭ швов не пересозданы: не найден шаблон."
        TopMsg "Не найден шаблон КЭ2:" & vbCrLf & KE_TEMPLATE_PATH, "КЭ", MB_ICONEXCLAMATION
        Exit Sub
    End If

    Dim paperProps As Variant, paperW As Double, paperH As Double
    paperProps = keSwDraw.GetCurrentSheet.GetProperties()
    paperW = paperProps(5)
    paperH = paperProps(6)

    ' Только при изменениях, неполном комплекте с подтверждением или явном запросе.
    ArchiveExistingKEWeldSheets keSwDraw, insertAfterSheet

    Dim pages As Long, pageNum As Long, weldIdx As Long, sheetName As String
    pages = (groupedCount + KE_ROWS_PER_PAGE * KE_GROUPS_COUNT - 1) \ (KE_ROWS_PER_PAGE * KE_GROUPS_COUNT)
    weldIdx = 1
    For pageNum = 1 To pages
        sheetName = "КЭ (Швы Лист " & CStr(pageNum) & ")"
        If Not CreateKESheet(sheetName, KE_TEMPLATE_PATH, True, paperW, paperH) Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3275, "RunIntegratedKE", "Не создан лист " & sheetName & "."
        DrawWeldTable groupedWelds, groupedCount, weldIdx
    Next pageNum
    ' Старый DrawWeldTable допускает отдельные ошибки API. Не считаем результат успешным без чтения обратно.
    comparison = KC22_CompareWeldSheets(keSwDraw, groupedWelds, groupedCount, reason)
    If comparison <> 1 Or weldIdx <> groupedCount + 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3276, "RunIntegratedKE", _
        "Новый комплект КЭ швов не подтверждён. " & reason & " Прежний комплект сохранён в архиве. Проверьте чертёж."
    If forceRebuild Then KC14_SaveSetting keSwModel.GetPathName, "ForceKEWeldRebuild", "0"
    FillAllKESheetHeaders detailName, kdNumber, primary02190
End Sub

Private Sub FillAllKESheetHeaders(ByVal detailName As String, _
    ByVal kdNumber As String, ByVal primary02190 As String)
    Dim names As Variant, i As Long, n As String, totalKE As Long
    Dim code20102 As String, maxRegular As Long, weldOrdinal As Long, sheetNo As Long
    names = keSwDraw.GetSheetNames
    totalKE = CountKESheets(keSwDraw)
    maxRegular = MaxRegularKENumber(keSwDraw)
    code20102 = BuildShiftedCode(kdNumber, KC28_S("Codes.20102"))
    For i = LBound(names) To UBound(names)
        n = CStr(names(i))
        If IsKESheet(n) Then
            sheetNo = KEPrintedNumber(n, maxRegular, weldOrdinal)
            If RegularKENumber(n) = 1 Then
                FillKE1Header n, detailName, kdNumber, primary02190, code20102, totalKE, sheetNo
            Else
                FillKEOtherHeader n, kdNumber, primary02190, code20102, sheetNo
            End If
        End If
    Next i
End Sub

Private Sub FillKE1Header(ByVal sheetName As String, ByVal detailName As String, _
    ByVal kdNumber As String, ByVal primary02190 As String, ByVal code20102 As String, _
    ByVal totalKE As Long, ByVal ordinal As Long)
    If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(keSwDraw, sheetName) Then Exit Sub
    InsertBorderlessCell keSwModel, KE1_X1, KE1_Y1, KE1_W1, KE1_H1, detailName, "MF_FIELD_KE_NAME"
    InsertBorderlessCell keSwModel, KE1_X2, KE1_Y2, KE1_W2, KE1_H2, kdNumber, "MF_FIELD_KE_KD"
    InsertBorderlessCell keSwModel, KE1_X3, KE1_Y3, KE1_W3, KE1_H3, primary02190, "MF_FIELD_KE_PRIMARY"
    InsertBorderlessCell keSwModel, KE1_X4, KE1_Y4, KE1_W4, KE1_H4, FormatFrameCode(code20102), "MF_FIELD_KE_CODE"
    InsertBorderlessCell keSwModel, KE1_X5, KE1_Y5, KE1_W5, KE1_H5, CStr(totalKE), "MF_FIELD_KE_TOTAL"
    InsertBorderlessCell keSwModel, KE1_X6, KE1_Y6, KE1_W6, KE1_H6, CStr(ordinal), "MF_FIELD_KE_PAGE"
    KC23_UpdateSignHeader keSwModel, KC28_N("Sign.KE1Y")
End Sub

Private Sub FillKEOtherHeader(ByVal sheetName As String, ByVal kdNumber As String, _
    ByVal primary02190 As String, ByVal code20102 As String, ByVal ordinal As Long)
    If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(keSwDraw, sheetName) Then Exit Sub
    InsertBorderlessCell keSwModel, KEOTH_X1, KEOTH_Y1, KEOTH_W1, KEOTH_H1, kdNumber, "MF_FIELD_KE_KD"
    InsertBorderlessCell keSwModel, KEOTH_X2, KEOTH_Y2, KEOTH_W2, KEOTH_H2, primary02190, "MF_FIELD_KE_PRIMARY"
    InsertBorderlessCell keSwModel, KEOTH_X3, KEOTH_Y3, KEOTH_W3, KEOTH_H3, FormatFrameCode(code20102), "MF_FIELD_KE_CODE"
    InsertBorderlessCell keSwModel, KEOTH_X4, KEOTH_Y4, KEOTH_W4, KEOTH_H4, CStr(ordinal), "MF_FIELD_KE_PAGE"
End Sub

' ============================================
' ТАБЛИЦА ШВОВ (картинки — KE_PlaceWeldPictures)
' ============================================
Private Sub DrawWeldTable(ByRef welds() As UnifiedWeld, _
                           cnt As Long, _
                           ByRef weldIdx As Long)
    Dim weldsOnPage As Long
    weldsOnPage = KE_ROWS_PER_PAGE * KE_GROUPS_COUNT
    If weldIdx + weldsOnPage - 1 > cnt Then
        weldsOnPage = cnt - weldIdx + 1
    End If

    Dim rowsData As Long
    rowsData = (weldsOnPage + KE_GROUPS_COUNT - 1) \ KE_GROUPS_COUNT
    If rowsData < 1 Then rowsData = 1
    If rowsData > KE_ROWS_PER_PAGE Then rowsData = KE_ROWS_PER_PAGE

    Dim totalRows As Long: totalRows = 1 + rowsData
    Dim totalCols As Long: totalCols = 4 * KE_GROUPS_COUNT

    On Error Resume Next
    keSwModel.ClearSelection2 True

    Dim tbl As Object
    Set tbl = keSwModel.Extension.InsertGeneralTableAnnotation( _
        True, KE_TABLE_X, KE_TABLE_Y, 1, "", totalRows, totalCols)

    If tbl Is Nothing Then
        MsgBox "Не удалось создать таблицу!", vbCritical
        Exit Sub
    End If

    Dim tblAnn As Object
    Set tblAnn = tbl.GetAnnotation
    If Not tblAnn Is Nothing Then
        tblAnn.SetPosition2 KE_TABLE_X, KE_TABLE_Y, 0
    End If

    Dim g As Long
    Dim ci As Long
    For g = 0 To KE_GROUPS_COUNT - 1
        ci = g * 4
        tbl.SetColumnWidth ci, KE_COL_NUM_W, 0
        tbl.SetColumnWidth ci + 1, KE_COL_OBZ_W, 0
        tbl.SetColumnWidth ci + 2, KE_COL_PIC_W, 0
        tbl.SetColumnWidth ci + 3, KE_COL_SIZE_W, 0
    Next g

    tbl.SetRowHeight 0, KE_HDR_ROW_H, 0
    Dim ri As Long
    For ri = 1 To rowsData
        tbl.SetRowHeight ri, KE_ROW_DATA_H, 0
    Next ri

    ' Заголовки
    For g = 0 To KE_GROUPS_COUNT - 1
        ci = g * 4
        tbl.Text2(0, ci, False) = "№"
        tbl.Text2(0, ci + 1, False) = "Обозначение"
        tbl.Text2(0, ci + 2, False) = "Рис."
        tbl.Text2(0, ci + 3, False) = "Размеры"
        tbl.CellTextHorizontalJustification(0, ci) = 2
        tbl.CellTextHorizontalJustification(0, ci + 1) = 2
        tbl.CellTextHorizontalJustification(0, ci + 2) = 2
        tbl.CellTextHorizontalJustification(0, ci + 3) = 2
    Next g

    ' Данные
    Dim localIdx As Long, firstOnPage As Long
    localIdx = weldIdx
    firstOnPage = weldIdx

    For g = 0 To KE_GROUPS_COUNT - 1
        For ri = 1 To rowsData
            ci = g * 4
            If localIdx > cnt Then
                tbl.Text2(ri, ci, False) = ""
                tbl.Text2(ri, ci + 1, False) = ""
                tbl.Text2(ri, ci + 2, False) = ""
                tbl.Text2(ri, ci + 3, False) = ""
            Else
                Dim w As UnifiedWeld
                w = welds(localIdx)

                tbl.Text2(ri, ci, False) = w.NumberLabel
                tbl.CellTextHorizontalJustification(ri, ci) = 2

                Dim obzText As String
                obzText = BuildWeldDesignation(w)
                tbl.Text2(ri, ci + 1, False) = obzText
                tbl.CellTextHorizontalJustification(ri, ci + 1) = 2

                ' Для нестандартного шва — текст вида/разреза; для стандартного — картинка.
                tbl.Text2(ri, ci + 2, False) = KE_BuildWeldPictureText(w)
                tbl.CellTextHorizontalJustification(ri, ci + 2) = 2
                If Not KE_IsStandardWeld(w) Then
                    tbl.CellTextVerticalJustification(ri, ci + 2) = swTextAlignmentMiddle
                    tbl.CellTextVerticalJustification(ri, ci + 3) = swTextAlignmentMiddle
                End If

                Dim sizeText As String
                sizeText = BuildSizeText(w)
                tbl.Text2(ri, ci + 3, False) = sizeText
                tbl.CellTextHorizontalJustification(ri, ci + 3) = 2

                localIdx = localIdx + 1
            End If
        Next ri
    Next g

    ApplyMacroTableFont tbl
    tbl.SetRowHeight 0, KE_HDR_ROW_H, 0
    For ri = 1 To rowsData
        tbl.SetRowHeight ri, KE_ROW_DATA_H, 0
    Next ri

    ' Внешняя и внутренние границы — одинаковой толщины 0,25 мм.
    tbl.BorderLineWeight = 1
    tbl.GridLineWeight = 1
    keSwModel.ClearSelection2 True
    On Error GoTo 0

    ' v15.14: картинки швов из папки шаблонов — напротив своей ячейки "Рис.".
    KE_PlaceWeldPictures tbl, welds, firstOnPage, localIdx - 1, rowsData
    weldIdx = localIdx
End Sub

' ============================================
' v15.14: КАРТИНКИ ШВОВ В КОЛОНКЕ "Рис."
' ============================================
' Нормализация имени: без "ГОСТ", пробелов и регистра; латинские T/H/C/Y = русские Т/Н/С/У.
Private Function KE_PicNormalize(ByVal text As String) As String
    text = UCase$(Trim$(text))
    text = Replace(text, ChrW(160), "")
    text = Replace(text, " ", "")
    text = Replace(text, "GOST", "")
    text = Replace(text, ChrW(1043) & ChrW(1054) & ChrW(1057) & ChrW(1058), "")   ' ГОСТ
    text = Replace(text, "T", ChrW(1058))   ' T -> Т
    text = Replace(text, "H", ChrW(1053))   ' H -> Н
    text = Replace(text, "C", ChrW(1057))   ' C -> С
    text = Replace(text, "Y", ChrW(1059))   ' Y -> У
    KE_PicNormalize = text
End Function

' "ГОСТ 14771-76" + "Т9" -> "14771-76_Т9". Пусто, если стандарт или тип шва не определены.
Private Function KE_PicKey(w As UnifiedWeld) As String
    Dim std As String, i As Long, ch As String, token As String, started As Boolean
    If Not KE_IsStandardWeld(w) Then Exit Function
    std = Trim$(w.WeldStandard)
    For i = 1 To Len(std)
        ch = Mid$(std, i, 1)
        If ch Like "[0-9]" Or (started And ch = "-") Then
            token = token & ch
            started = True
        ElseIf started Then
            Exit For
        End If
    Next i
    ' В исходных данных стандарт может оканчиваться разделителем: ГОСТ 23518-79-.
    ' Этот дефис отделяет тип шва и не входит в имя файла изображения.
    Do While Len(token) > 0
        If Right$(token, 1) <> "-" Then Exit Do
        token = Left$(token, Len(token) - 1)
    Loop
    If Len(token) = 0 Or Len(Trim$(w.weldType)) = 0 Then Exit Function
    KE_PicKey = KE_PicNormalize(token & "_" & w.weldType)
End Function

' Только суффикс _varN в конце имени; N — положительное целое число.
Private Function KE_PicSplitVariant(ByVal stem As String, ByRef baseStem As String, _
    ByRef variantNumber As Long) As Boolean
    Dim suffixPos As Long, digits As String
    On Error GoTo NotVariant
    baseStem = "": variantNumber = 0
    suffixPos = InStrRev(UCase$(stem), "_VAR", -1, vbBinaryCompare)
    If suffixPos < 2 Then Exit Function
    digits = Mid$(stem, suffixPos + 4)
    If Len(digits) = 0 Then Exit Function
    If digits Like "*[!0-9]*" Then Exit Function
    variantNumber = CLng(digits)
    If variantNumber < 1 Then Exit Function
    baseStem = Left$(stem, suffixPos - 1)
    KE_PicSplitVariant = True
    Exit Function
NotVariant:
    baseStem = "": variantNumber = 0
End Function

Private Sub KE_PicIndexPut(ByVal target As Object, ByVal key As String, _
    ByVal path As String, ByVal extensionText As String)
    If Not target.Exists(key) Then
        target.Add key, path
    ElseIf extensionText = "bmp" Then
        target(key) = path
    End If
End Sub

' Отдельные индексы обычных картинок и вариантов. Папка читается один раз за запуск.
Private Function KE_PicIndex() As Object
    Dim fso As Object, folder As Object, f As Object, variantMap As Object
    Dim nm As String, stem As String, baseStem As String, dotPos As Long
    Dim ext As String, key As String, variantNumber As Long
    If Not gKePicIndex Is Nothing Then
        Set KE_PicIndex = gKePicIndex
        Exit Function
    End If
    Set gKePicIndex = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Set gKePicVariants = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    On Error GoTo Failed
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    If Not fso.FolderExists(KE_PIC_FOLDER) Then GoTo Failed
    Set folder = fso.GetFolder(KE_PIC_FOLDER)
    For Each f In folder.Files
        nm = CStr(f.name)
        dotPos = InStrRev(nm, ".")
        If dotPos > 1 Then
            ext = LCase$(Mid$(nm, dotPos + 1))
            Select Case ext
                Case "png", "jpg", "jpeg", "bmp", "tif", "tiff", "gif"
                    stem = Left$(nm, dotPos - 1)
                    If KE_PicSplitVariant(stem, baseStem, variantNumber) Then
                        key = KE_PicNormalize(baseStem)
                        If Not gKePicVariants.Exists(key) Then
                            Set variantMap = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
                            gKePicVariants.Add key, variantMap
                        Else
                            Set variantMap = gKePicVariants(key)
                        End If
                        KE_PicIndexPut variantMap, CStr(variantNumber), CStr(f.path), ext
                    Else
                        key = KE_PicNormalize(stem)
                        KE_PicIndexPut gKePicIndex, key, CStr(f.path), ext
                    End If
            End Select
        End If
    Next f
    Set KE_PicIndex = gKePicIndex
    Exit Function
Failed:
    If Not gKePicFolderWarned Then
        AddMacroWarning "Картинки швов: не удалось полностью прочитать папку " & KE_PIC_FOLDER & "."
        gKePicFolderWarned = True
    End If
    Set KE_PicIndex = gKePicIndex
End Function

' Возвращает пары (номер варианта, путь), отсортированные численно: 1, 2, 10.
Private Function KE_PicVariantFiles(ByVal key As String) As Collection
    Dim result As New Collection, variantMap As Object, ids As Variant
    Dim numbers() As Long, i As Long, j As Long, currentNumber As Long
    If gKePicVariants Is Nothing Then GoTo Done
    If Not gKePicVariants.Exists(key) Then GoTo Done
    Set variantMap = gKePicVariants(key)
    If variantMap.count = 0 Then GoTo Done
    ids = variantMap.Keys
    ReDim numbers(0 To variantMap.count - 1)
    For i = 0 To variantMap.count - 1
        numbers(i) = CLng(ids(i))
    Next i
    For i = 1 To UBound(numbers)
        currentNumber = numbers(i): j = i - 1
        Do While j >= 0
            If numbers(j) <= currentNumber Then Exit Do
            numbers(j + 1) = numbers(j)
            j = j - 1
        Loop
        numbers(j + 1) = currentNumber
    Next i
    For i = 0 To UBound(numbers)
        result.Add Array(numbers(i), CStr(variantMap(CStr(numbers(i)))))
    Next i
Done:
    Set KE_PicVariantFiles = result
End Function

Private Sub KE_PicAddJob(ByVal jobs As Collection, ByVal path As String, _
    ByVal x As Double, ByVal y As Double, ByVal w As Double, ByVal h As Double, _
    ByVal weldLabel As String, ByVal caption As String)
    jobs.Add Array(path, x, y, w, h, weldLabel, caption)
End Sub

' Подпись остаётся снаружи листа: в ячейку пользователь переносит только картинку.
Private Sub KE_PicAddCaption(ByVal caption As String, ByVal x As Double, ByVal y As Double)
    Dim note As Object, ann As Object, fmt As Object, ok As Boolean, reason As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    keSwModel.SetPickMode
    keSwModel.ClearSelection2 True
    Set note = keSwModel.InsertNote(caption)
    If note Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2730, , "InsertNote вернул Nothing."
    Set ann = note.GetAnnotation
    If ann Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2731, , "Не получена аннотация подписи."
    Set fmt = ann.GetTextFormat(0)
    If Not fmt Is Nothing Then
        fmt.CharHeight = 0.0025
        ok = ann.SetTextFormat(0, False, fmt)
        If Not ok Then KE_PicTrace "CAPTION font not applied: " & caption
    End If
    ok = ann.SetPosition2(x, y, 0#)
    If Not ok Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2732, , "SetPosition2 подписи вернул False."
    keSwModel.SetPickMode
    keSwModel.ClearSelection2 True
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    reason = "Подпись варианта «" & caption & "»: " & CStr(Err.number) & ": " & Err.description
    KE_PicTrace reason
    AddMacroWarning reason
    KE_PicReportLog
    On Error Resume Next
    keSwModel.SetPickMode
    keSwModel.ClearSelection2 True
    On Error GoTo 0
End Sub

Private Function KE_ColumnWidth(ByVal tbl As Object, ByVal col As Long) As Double
    Dim v As Double
    On Error Resume Next
    v = tbl.GetColumnWidth(col)
    On Error GoTo 0
    If v <= 0 Then
        Select Case col Mod 4
            Case 0: v = KE_COL_NUM_W
            Case 1: v = KE_COL_OBZ_W
            Case 2: v = KE_COL_PIC_W
            Case Else: v = KE_COL_SIZE_W
        End Select
    End If
    KE_ColumnWidth = v
End Function

Private Function KE_RowHeight(ByVal tbl As Object, ByVal row As Long) As Double
    Dim v As Double
    On Error Resume Next
    v = tbl.GetRowHeight(row)
    On Error GoTo 0
    If v <= 0 Then
        If row = 0 Then v = KE_HDR_ROW_H Else v = KE_ROW_DATA_H
    End If
    KE_RowHeight = v
End Function

' Обычная картинка — в ячейку. Файлы _varN — отдельными экземплярами за границами листа.
' Левая группа таблицы: слева от листа; правая: справа. Ячейка остаётся пустой.
Private Sub KE_PlaceWeldPictures(ByVal tbl As Object, ByRef welds() As UnifiedWeld, _
    ByVal firstIdx As Long, ByVal lastIdx As Long, ByVal rowsData As Long)
    Dim idx As Object, k As Long, g As Long, ri As Long, c As Long, r As Long, j As Long
    Dim key As String, x0 As Double, y0 As Double, pos As Variant, ann As Object
    Dim cellLeft As Double, cellTop As Double, cellW As Double, cellH As Double
    Dim jobs As New Collection, rec As Variant, variantFiles As Collection, entry As Variant
    Dim vi As Long, variantRows As Long, stageLeft As Double, stripWidth As Double
    Dim sheetWidth As Double, paperProps As Variant, caption As String, weldLabel As String
    Dim jobFeat() As Object, jobL() As Double, jobB() As Double, jobW() As Double, jobH() As Double
    Dim pic As Object, reason As String, sheetName As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    If tbl Is Nothing Or lastIdx < firstIdx Or rowsData < 1 Then Exit Sub
    sheetName = keSwDraw.GetCurrentSheet.GetName
    KE_PicTrace "SHEET " & sheetName
    Set idx = KE_PicIndex()
    If idx.count = 0 And gKePicVariants.count = 0 Then
        KE_PicTrace "NO IMAGES: " & KE_PIC_FOLDER
        If Not gKePicFolderWarned Then
            AddMacroWarning "Картинки швов не вставлены: в папке " & KE_PIC_FOLDER & " нет доступных изображений."
            gKePicFolderWarned = True
        End If
        KE_PicReportLog
        Exit Sub
    End If

    x0 = KE_TABLE_X: y0 = KE_TABLE_Y
    Set ann = tbl.GetAnnotation
    If Not ann Is Nothing Then
        pos = ann.GetPosition
        If IsArray(pos) Then
            x0 = CDbl(pos(0)): y0 = CDbl(pos(1))
        End If
    End If
    KE_PicTrace "TABLE origin(m)=" & CStr(x0) & ";" & CStr(y0)
    For k = 0 To lastIdx - firstIdx
        g = k \ rowsData
        ri = (k Mod rowsData) + 1
        If g < KE_GROUPS_COUNT Then
            key = KE_PicKey(welds(firstIdx + k))
            KE_PicTrace "LOOKUP weld=" & welds(firstIdx + k).NumberLabel & _
                "; standard=" & welds(firstIdx + k).WeldStandard & _
                "; type=" & welds(firstIdx + k).weldType & "; key=" & key
            If Len(key) > 0 Then
                cellLeft = x0
                For c = 0 To g * 4 + 1
                    cellLeft = cellLeft + KE_ColumnWidth(tbl, c)
                Next c
                cellW = KE_ColumnWidth(tbl, g * 4 + 2)
                cellTop = y0
                For r = 0 To ri - 1
                    cellTop = cellTop - KE_RowHeight(tbl, r)
                Next r
                cellH = KE_RowHeight(tbl, ri)
                weldLabel = welds(firstIdx + k).NumberLabel
                Set variantFiles = KE_PicVariantFiles(key)
                If variantFiles.count > 0 Then
                    ' Даже при наличии обычного файла не выбираем конструктивный вариант автоматически.
                    variantRows = variantRows + 1
                    If g > 0 And sheetWidth <= 0 Then
                        paperProps = keSwDraw.GetCurrentSheet.GetProperties()
                        sheetWidth = CDbl(paperProps(5))
                        If sheetWidth <= 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2733, , "Не определена ширина листа."
                    End If
                    stripWidth = variantFiles.count * cellW + (variantFiles.count - 1) * KE_PIC_VARIANT_GAP
                    If g = 0 Then
                        stageLeft = -KE_PIC_SIDE_GAP - stripWidth
                    Else
                        stageLeft = sheetWidth + KE_PIC_SIDE_GAP
                    End If
                    If cellH <= KE_PIC_CAPTION_H + 2 * KE_PIC_MARGIN Then _
                        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2734, , "Слишком малая высота строки для вариантов."
                    For vi = 1 To variantFiles.count
                        entry = variantFiles(vi)
                        caption = "Шов " & weldLabel & " | var" & CStr(entry(0))
                        KE_PicAddJob jobs, CStr(entry(1)), _
                            stageLeft + (vi - 1) * (cellW + KE_PIC_VARIANT_GAP), _
                            cellTop - cellH, cellW, cellH - KE_PIC_CAPTION_H, weldLabel, caption
                        KE_PicTrace "VARIANT weld=" & weldLabel & "; base=" & key & "; var=" & CStr(entry(0))
                    Next vi
                ElseIf idx.Exists(key) Then
                    KE_PicAddJob jobs, CStr(idx(key)), cellLeft, cellTop - cellH, _
                        cellW, cellH, weldLabel, ""
                Else
                    AddMacroWarning "Шов " & weldLabel & ": нет картинки «" & key & "» или файлов «" & key & "_varN» в папке " & KE_PIC_FOLDER & "."
                    KE_PicTrace "MISSING weld=" & weldLabel & "; key=" & key
                    KE_PicReportLog
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next k
    If jobs.count = 0 Then Exit Sub
    ReDim jobFeat(1 To jobs.count)
    ReDim jobL(1 To jobs.count): ReDim jobB(1 To jobs.count)
    ReDim jobW(1 To jobs.count): ReDim jobH(1 To jobs.count)

    ' Не меняем подтверждённую последовательность API вставки каждой картинки.
    For j = 1 To jobs.count
        rec = jobs(j)
        reason = ""
        Set jobFeat(j) = KE_InsertSheetPicture(CStr(rec(0)), CDbl(rec(1)), CDbl(rec(2)), _
            CDbl(rec(3)), CDbl(rec(4)), jobL(j), jobB(j), jobW(j), jobH(j), reason)
        If jobFeat(j) Is Nothing Then
            AddMacroWarning "Шов " & CStr(rec(5)) & ": " & reason
            KE_PicReportLog
        End If
    Next j
    ' Подписи добавляются только после всех картинок, чтобы не вмешиваться в их вставку.
    For j = 1 To jobs.count
        rec = jobs(j)
        If Len(CStr(rec(6))) > 0 Then
            If Not jobFeat(j) Is Nothing Then
                KE_PicAddCaption CStr(rec(6)), CDbl(rec(1)) + KE_PIC_MARGIN, _
                    CDbl(rec(2)) + CDbl(rec(4)) + KE_PIC_CAPTION_H - 0.0015
            End If
        End If
    Next j
    keSwModel.ClearSelection2 True
    keSwModel.EditRebuild3
    For j = 1 To jobs.count
        rec = jobs(j)
        If Not jobFeat(j) Is Nothing Then
            reason = ""
            Set pic = KE_PicFromFeature(jobFeat(j), reason)
            If Not pic Is Nothing Then
                If KE_PictureFits(pic, jobL(j), jobB(j), jobW(j), jobH(j), reason) Then
                    KE_PicTrace "FINAL OK weld=" & CStr(rec(5)) & "; file=" & CStr(rec(0))
                Else
                    KE_PicTrace "FINAL FAIL weld=" & CStr(rec(5)) & "; " & reason
                    AddMacroWarning "Шов " & CStr(rec(5)) & ": после перестроения положение картинки не подтверждено."
                    KE_PicReportLog
                End If
            Else
                AddMacroWarning "Шов " & CStr(rec(5)) & ": не удалось повторно прочитать картинку."
                KE_PicTrace "FINAL READ FAIL weld=" & CStr(rec(5)) & "; " & reason
                KE_PicReportLog
            End If
        End If
    Next j
    If variantRows > 0 Then
        AddMacroWarning sheetName & ": для " & CStr(variantRows) & _
            " строк варианты вынесены за границы листа. Перенесите нужные картинки в ячейки «Рис.» вручную."
    End If
    keSwModel.GraphicsRedraw2
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    reason = "Размещение картинок: ошибка " & CStr(Err.number) & ": " & Err.description
    KE_PicTrace reason
    AddMacroWarning reason
    KE_PicReportLog
End Sub

' Вставка на лист без создания/закрытия отдельных эскизов.
' Последовательность соответствует тесту SW21_Pictures_bmp: SetPickMode после КАЖДОЙ картинки.
Private Function KE_InsertSheetPicture(ByVal path As String, _
    ByVal cellLeft As Double, ByVal cellBottom As Double, ByVal cellW As Double, ByVal cellH As Double, _
    ByRef checkLeft As Double, ByRef checkBottom As Double, ByRef checkW As Double, _
    ByRef checkH As Double, ByRef reason As String) As Object
    Dim pic As Object, picFeat As Object, stage As String, ok As Boolean
    Dim errNum As Long, errText As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    stage = "выбор листа"
    KE_PicTrace "INSERT file=" & path
    keSwModel.SetPickMode
    keSwModel.ClearSelection2 True
    keSwDraw.EditSheet
    ok = keSwDraw.ActivateView("")
    KE_PicTrace "ActivateView(sheet)=" & CStr(ok)
    If Not ok Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2710, , "Не удалось активировать лист."

    checkLeft = cellLeft: checkBottom = cellBottom
    checkW = cellW: checkH = cellH
    KE_PicTrace "CELL sheet(m)=" & CStr(cellLeft) & ";" & CStr(cellBottom) & ";" & CStr(cellW) & ";" & CStr(cellH)
    stage = "вставка изображения"
    Set pic = keSwModel.SketchManager.InsertSketchPicture2(path, False)
    If pic Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2714, , "InsertSketchPicture2 не вернул картинку."
    Set picFeat = pic.GetFeature
    If picFeat Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2715, , "Не найден Feature картинки."
    KE_PicTrace "PICTURE " & picFeat.name

    ' До задания размера и положения не снимаем выделение и не вставляем следующую картинку.
    stage = "размер и положение"
    If Not KE_FitPictureToCell(pic, cellLeft, cellBottom, cellW, cellH, reason) Then
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2716, , reason
    End If
    stage = "возврат в режим выбора"
    keSwModel.SetPickMode
    keSwModel.ClearSelection2 True

    stage = "проверка после SetPickMode"
    Set pic = KE_PicFromFeature(picFeat, reason)
    If pic Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2718, , reason
    If Not KE_PictureFits(pic, cellLeft, cellBottom, cellW, cellH, reason) Then
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2719, , reason
    End If
    Set KE_InsertSheetPicture = picFeat
    KE_PicTrace "AFTER SetPickMode OK"
    Exit Function
Failed:
    errNum = Err.number: errText = Err.description
    reason = "этап «" & stage & "», ошибка " & CStr(errNum) & ": " & errText
    KE_PicTrace "FAIL " & reason
    ' Не удаляем вставленную картинку при ошибке — её состояние остаётся доступно для диагностики.
    On Error Resume Next
    keSwModel.SetPickMode
    keSwModel.ClearSelection2 True
    On Error GoTo 0
    Set KE_InsertSheetPicture = Nothing
End Function

Private Function KE_PicFromFeature(ByVal feat As Object, ByRef reason As String) As Object
    On Error GoTo Failed
    Set KE_PicFromFeature = feat.GetSpecificFeature2
    If KE_PicFromFeature Is Nothing Then reason = "GetSpecificFeature2 не вернул картинку."
    Exit Function
Failed:
    reason = "GetSpecificFeature2: " & CStr(Err.number) & ": " & Err.description
    Set KE_PicFromFeature = Nothing
End Function

Private Function KE_FitPictureToCell(ByVal pic As Object, ByVal cellLeft As Double, _
    ByVal cellBottom As Double, ByVal cellW As Double, ByVal cellH As Double, _
    ByRef reason As String) As Boolean
    Dim w As Double, h As Double, fitFactor As Double, maxW As Double, maxH As Double
    Dim margin As Double, x As Double, y As Double, ok As Boolean
    On Error GoTo Failed
    margin = KE_PIC_MARGIN
    maxW = cellW - 2 * margin: maxH = cellH - 2 * margin
    If maxW <= 0 Or maxH <= 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2723, , "Ячейка слишком мала."
    pic.GetSize w, h
    KE_PicTrace "ORIGINAL size(m)=" & CStr(w) & ";" & CStr(h)
    If w <= 0 Or h <= 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2724, , "GetSize вернул нулевые размеры."
    fitFactor = maxW / w
    If maxH / h < fitFactor Then fitFactor = maxH / h
    w = w * fitFactor: h = h * fitFactor
    x = cellLeft + (cellW - w) / 2
    y = cellBottom + (cellH - h) / 2
    ok = pic.SetSize(w, h, False)
    KE_PicTrace "SetSize=" & CStr(ok) & "; target(m)=" & CStr(w) & ";" & CStr(h)
    If Not ok Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2725, , "SetSize вернул False."
    ok = pic.SetOrigin(x, y)
    KE_PicTrace "SetOrigin=" & CStr(ok) & "; target(m)=" & CStr(x) & ";" & CStr(y)
    If Not ok Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2726, , "SetOrigin вернул False."
    KE_FitPictureToCell = KE_PictureFits(pic, cellLeft, cellBottom, cellW, cellH, reason)
    Exit Function
Failed:
    reason = "Изменение картинки: " & CStr(Err.number) & ": " & Err.description
    KE_PicTrace reason
End Function

' Проверка по фактическим данным API: границы, центр и заполнение хотя бы одной стороны.
Private Function KE_PictureFits(ByVal pic As Object, ByVal cellLeft As Double, _
    ByVal cellBottom As Double, ByVal cellW As Double, ByVal cellH As Double, _
    ByRef reason As String) As Boolean
    Dim x As Double, y As Double, w As Double, h As Double, tol As Double, margin As Double
    On Error GoTo Failed
    pic.GetOrigin x, y
    pic.GetSize w, h
    KE_PicTrace "READ xywh(m)=" & CStr(x) & ";" & CStr(y) & ";" & CStr(w) & ";" & CStr(h)
    tol = 0.0001
    margin = KE_PIC_MARGIN
    If w <= 0 Or h <= 0 Then
        reason = "Нулевой размер картинки.": Exit Function
    End If
    If x < cellLeft - tol Or y < cellBottom - tol Or _
       x + w > cellLeft + cellW + tol Or y + h > cellBottom + cellH + tol Then
        reason = "Фактические границы картинки вне ячейки.": Exit Function
    End If
    If Abs((x + w / 2) - (cellLeft + cellW / 2)) > tol Or _
       Abs((y + h / 2) - (cellBottom + cellH / 2)) > tol Then
        reason = "Картинка не в центре ячейки.": Exit Function
    End If
    If Abs(w - (cellW - 2 * margin)) > tol And Abs(h - (cellH - 2 * margin)) > tol Then
        reason = "Размер не соответствует вписыванию в ячейку.": Exit Function
    End If
    KE_PictureFits = True
    Exit Function
Failed:
    reason = "Чтение положения: " & CStr(Err.number) & ": " & Err.description
End Function

' Локальный Unicode-журнал; ошибки записи журнала не прерывают построение чертежа.
Private Sub KE_PicTrace(ByVal message As String)
    Dim fso As Object, stream As Object, folder As String
    If Not KC28_LogOn("Logs.Pictures") Then Exit Sub
    On Error GoTo Failed
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    If Len(gKePicLogPath) = 0 Then
        folder = Environ$("TEMP")
        If Len(folder) = 0 Then folder = Environ$("TMP")
        If Len(folder) = 0 Then Exit Sub
        gKePicLogPath = fso.BuildPath(folder, "KE_WeldPictures_" & Format$(Now, "yyyymmdd_hhnnss") & ".log")
        Set stream = fso.CreateTextFile(gKePicLogPath, True, True)
        stream.WriteLine "KE pictures v15.14.5; " & CStr(Now)
        stream.WriteLine "SW revision=" & keSwApp.RevisionNumber
    Else
        Set stream = fso.OpenTextFile(gKePicLogPath, 8, True, -1)
    End If
    stream.WriteLine Format$(Now, "hh:nn:ss") & " " & message
    stream.Close
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not stream Is Nothing Then stream.Close
    On Error GoTo 0
End Sub

Private Sub KE_PicReportLog()
    If gKePicLogReported Or Len(gKePicLogPath) = 0 Then Exit Sub
    AddMacroWarning "Диагностика картинок: " & gKePicLogPath
    gKePicLogReported = True
End Sub

' Общий формат обоих макросов: A:E — ВСЕ операции, I7:I9 — три строки ОК1.
Private Function OK1ExcelHeaders() As Variant
    OK1ExcelHeaders = Array("Р", "Контролируемые параметры", _
        "Наименование средств ТО", "Объем и ПК", "То/Тв")
End Function

Private Function OK1ExcelText(ByVal ws As Object, ByVal r As Long, ByVal c As Long) As String
    Dim v As Variant
    v = ws.cells(r, c).Value2
    If IsError(v) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2500, , _
        "Ошибка Excel в " & ws.name & "!" & ws.cells(r, c).address(False, False)
    If Not IsEmpty(v) Then OK1ExcelText = Trim$(CStr(v))
End Function

Private Function HasCardHeaders(ByVal ws As Object) As Boolean
    Dim h As Variant, c As Long
    h = OK1ExcelHeaders()
    For c = 0 To 4
        If OK1ExcelText(ws, 6, c + 1) <> CStr(h(c)) Then Exit Function
    Next c
    HasCardHeaders = True
End Function

Private Function IsUnifiedCard(ByVal ws As Object) As Boolean
    If KC23_NewLayout(ws) Then
        IsUnifiedCard = HasCardHeaders(ws)
        Exit Function
    End If
    If OK1ExcelText(ws, 5, 8) <> UNIFIED_CARD_MARKER And _
        OK1ExcelText(ws, 5, 8) <> "ОК: единый список v9" Then Exit Function
    If Not HasCardHeaders(ws) Then Exit Function
    If OK1ExcelText(ws, 6, 9) <> "Первые три строки ОК1" Then Exit Function
    IsUnifiedCard = True
End Function

Private Function IsLegacyOK1Block(ByVal ws As Object) As Boolean
    Dim h As Variant, c As Long
    If OK1ExcelText(ws, 5, 8) <> "Данные ОК1 (11 строк)" Then Exit Function
    h = OK1ExcelHeaders()
    For c = 0 To 4
        If OK1ExcelText(ws, 6, c + 8) <> CStr(h(c)) Then Exit Function
    Next c
    IsLegacyOK1Block = True
End Function

Private Function IsIntroOperationKey(ByVal text As String) As Boolean
    IsIntroOperationKey = StrComp(Left$(Trim$(text), 4), "ОК1-", vbTextCompare) = 0
End Function

Private Function LastOKContinuationRow(ByVal ws As Object) As Long
    Dim c As Long, r As Long, result As Long
    result = 6
    For c = 1 To 5
        r = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, c).End(-4162).row
        If r > result Then result = r
    Next c
    LastOKContinuationRow = result
End Function

Private Sub DefaultFixedCardText(ByRef fixed() As String)
    ReDim fixed(0 To 2)
    fixed(0) = "Контролер ОТК, мастер, исполнитель."
    fixed(1) = "Костюм для защиты от общих производственных загрязнений и механических воздействий, ботинки кожаные с защитным подноском"
    fixed(2) = "перчатки трикотажные с точечным полимерным покрытием, каска защитная, подшлемник под каску."
End Sub

Private Sub DefaultIntroOperations(ByRef intro() As Variant, ByRef introCount As Long)
    ' ДВА таких же четырёхстрочных блока, как у параметров чертежа.
    introCount = 8
    ReDim intro(1 To introCount, 1 To 5)
    intro(1, 1) = "ОК1-1"
    intro(1, 2) = "1. Клеймо сварщика, мастера, заводской номер. Эскиз 2"
    intro(1, 3) = "Визуально"
    intro(1, 4) = "100%"
    intro(1, 5) = 0.5
    intro(5, 1) = "ОК1-2"
    intro(5, 2) = "2. Отсутствие дефектов сварных швов: прожогов, подрезов, раковин, трещин. Отсутствие сварочных брызг, окалин и побежалости."
    intro(5, 3) = "Визуально"
    intro(5, 4) = "100%"
    intro(5, 5) = 7
    intro(6, 3) = "Лупа ГОСТ 25706-83"
End Sub

Private Sub GetIntroSource(ByVal source As Object, ByRef fixed() As String, _
    ByRef mass As Variant, ByRef intro() As Variant, ByRef introCount As Long)
    Dim r As Long, c As Long, lastRow As Long, key As String
    Dim firstGroup As Boolean, groupNo As Long, text As String, anyValue As Boolean
    Dim rx As Object
    DefaultFixedCardText fixed
    mass = Empty
    If source Is Nothing Then
        DefaultIntroOperations intro, introCount
        Exit Sub
    End If
    key = OK1ExcelText(source, 5, 8)
    If key = UNIFIED_CARD_MARKER Or key = "ОК: единый список v9" Then
        If Not IsUnifiedCard(source) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2527, , _
            "В прежнем Excel есть подпись единого списка, но изменена шапка. Перенос отменён без подстановки образца."
    ElseIf key = "Данные ОК1 (11 строк)" Then
        If Not IsLegacyOK1Block(source) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2528, , _
            "В прежнем Excel есть подпись v8, но изменена шапка ОК1. Перенос отменён."
    End If
    If IsUnifiedCard(source) Or IsLegacyOK1Block(source) Then
        For r = 0 To 2
            fixed(r) = KC23_CellText(KC23_FixedCell(source, r))
        Next r
        mass = KC23_Field(source, "OK_Mass", "I3").Value2
    End If
    If IsUnifiedCard(source) Then
        ' Только префиксные операции, не параметры чертежа и не клеймение в конце.
        introCount = 0
        lastRow = KC15_IntroLast(source)
        If lastRow >= 7 Then introCount = lastRow - 6
        If introCount > 0 Then
            ReDim intro(1 To introCount, 1 To 5)
            For r = 1 To introCount
                For c = 1 To 5
                    intro(r, c) = source.cells(r + 6, c).Value2
                Next c
                If Len(Trim$(CStr(intro(r, 1)))) > 0 Then
                    groupNo = groupNo + 1
                    intro(r, 1) = "ОК1-" & CStr(groupNo)
                End If
                intro(r, 4) = KC15_PercentText(intro(r, 4))
            Next r
        Else
            ReDim intro(1 To 1, 1 To 5)
        End If
    ElseIf IsLegacyOK1Block(source) Then
        ' Миграция v8: физические строки 04–11 становятся исходными строками операций.
        introCount = 8
        ReDim intro(1 To introCount, 1 To 5)
        Set rx = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")
        rx.Pattern = "^\s*[0-9]+[.)]\s*"
        firstGroup = True
        For r = 1 To introCount
            anyValue = False
            For c = 2 To 5
                intro(r, c) = source.cells(9 + r, 7 + c).Value2
                If Len(OK1ExcelText(source, 9 + r, 7 + c)) > 0 Then anyValue = True
            Next c
            If anyValue Then
                text = OK1ExcelText(source, 9 + r, 9)
                If firstGroup Or rx.Test(text) Then
                    groupNo = groupNo + 1
                    intro(r, 1) = "ОК1-" & CStr(groupNo)
                    firstGroup = False
                End If
            End If
        Next r
    Else
        DefaultIntroOperations intro, introCount
    End If
End Sub

Private Sub WriteFixedCardBlock(ByVal ws As Object, ByRef fixed() As String, ByVal mass As Variant)
    Dim r As Long
    KC23_EnsureLayout ws
    KC23_Field(ws, "OK_Mass", "I3").Value2 = mass
    For r = 0 To 2: KC23_FixedCell(ws, r).Value2 = fixed(r): Next r
    ws.Range("H20:I22").rows.AutoFit
End Sub

Private Sub WriteIntroOperations(ByVal ws As Object, ByRef intro() As Variant, ByVal introCount As Long)
    Dim r As Long, c As Long
    For r = 1 To introCount
        For c = 1 To 5
            ws.cells(6 + r, c).Value2 = intro(r, c)
        Next c
    Next r
    If introCount > 0 Then
        ws.Range("A7:E" & CStr(6 + introCount)).Font.Bold = False
        ws.Range("A7:E" & CStr(6 + introCount)).Interior.Pattern = -4142
        ws.Range("A7:E" & CStr(6 + introCount)).VerticalAlignment = -4160
        ws.Range("A7:E" & CStr(6 + introCount)).Borders.LineStyle = 1
        ws.Range("A7:A" & CStr(6 + introCount)).NumberFormat = "@"
        ws.Range("B7:C" & CStr(6 + introCount)).WrapText = True
        ws.Range("A7:E" & CStr(6 + introCount)).rows.AutoFit
    End If
End Sub

Private Function InitializeUnifiedExport(ByVal target As Object, ByVal xl As Object, _
    ByVal sourcePath As String) As Long
    Dim sourceBook As Object, source As Object, ws As Object
    Dim fixed() As String, mass As Variant, intro() As Variant, introCount As Long
    Dim errorNumber As Long, errorText As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    If Len(Trim$(sourcePath)) > 0 Then
        Set sourceBook = xl.Workbooks.Open(sourcePath, 0, True)
        For Each ws In sourceBook.Worksheets
            If StrComp(ws.name, "Карта контроля", vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
                Set source = ws
                Exit For
            End If
        Next ws
        If Not source Is Nothing Then source.Calculate
    End If
    GetIntroSource source, fixed, mass, intro, introCount
    NormalizeIntroBlocks intro, introCount
    WriteFixedCardBlock target, fixed, mass
    KC23_CopyPanel source, target
    WriteIntroOperations target, intro, introCount
    InitializeUnifiedExport = 7 + introCount
    If Not sourceBook Is Nothing Then sourceBook.Close False
    Exit Function
Failed:
    errorNumber = Err.number
    errorText = Err.description
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not sourceBook Is Nothing Then sourceBook.Close False
    On Error GoTo 0
    Err.Raise errorNumber, "InitializeUnifiedExport", errorText
End Function
' Единственная публичная точка входа модуля — Main.
Private Function EnsureUnifiedExcel(ByVal xl As Object, ByVal book As Object, ByVal ws As Object, ByRef filePath As String, ByRef primaryNumber As String, ByRef kdNumber As String, ByRef detailName As String) As Boolean
    EnsureUnifiedExcel = KC31_ValidateCard(xl, book, ws, primaryNumber, kdNumber, detailName)
End Function

Private Sub ReadOK1ExcelData(ByVal ws As Object, ByRef data() As String, _
    ByRef massText As String, ByRef total As Double)
    Dim r As Long, n As Double
    If Not IsUnifiedCard(ws) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2505, , "Не распознан единый формат Excel v9."
    If Not KC23_NewLayout(ws) Then
        If OK1ExcelText(ws, 3, 8) <> "Масса детали, кг" Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2506, , "Не найдена подпись массы H3."
    End If
    massText = KC23_CellText(KC23_Field(ws, "OK_Mass", "I3"))
    If Not NumericCellValue(massText, n) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2507, , "Масса в панели реквизитов должна быть числом."
    If n <= 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2508, , "Масса в панели реквизитов должна быть больше нуля."
    ReDim data(0 To OK1_DATA_ROWS - 1, 0 To NUM_COLS - 1)
    For r = 0 To 2
        If KC23_CellText(KC23_FixedCell(ws, r).Offset(0, -1)) <> Format$(r + 1, "00") Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2509, , "Не изменяйте номера фиксированных строк ОК1: 01, 02, 03."
        data(r, 0) = KC23_CellText(KC23_FixedCell(ws, r))
    Next r
    total = 0
End Sub

Private Function OKPageCapacity(ByVal pageNumber As Long) As Long
    If pageNumber = 1 Then
        OKPageCapacity = OK1_DATA_ROWS - 4
    Else
        OKPageCapacity = MAX_ROWS_PER_PAGE
    End If
End Function

Private Sub PaginateUnifiedRows(ByRef rows() As VisRow, ByVal rowCount As Long, _
    ByRef pages() As Long, ByRef pageCount As Long, ByRef splitCount As Long)
    Dim first As Long, last As Long, i As Long, groupID As Long, dataSize As Long
    Dim currentPage As Long, used As Long, gap As Long, priorSeparator As Long
    If rowCount < 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2510, , "Нет строк операций для размещения."
    ReDim pages(1 To rowCount)
    currentPage = 1
    first = 1
    splitCount = 0
    Do While first <= rowCount
        groupID = rows(first).GrpIdx
        last = first
        Do While last < rowCount
            If rows(last + 1).GrpIdx <> groupID Then Exit Do
            last = last + 1
        Loop
        dataSize = 0
        For i = first To last
            If Not rows(i).IsSep Then dataSize = dataSize + 1
        Next i
        If dataSize > 0 Then
            If dataSize <= MAX_ROWS_PER_PAGE Then
                gap = 0
                If used > 0 And priorSeparator > 0 Then gap = 1
                If used + gap + dataSize > OKPageCapacity(currentPage) Then
                    currentPage = currentPage + 1
                    used = 0
                    gap = 0
                End If
                If gap = 1 Then
                    pages(priorSeparator) = currentPage
                    used = used + 1
                End If
                For i = first To last
                    If Not rows(i).IsSep Then
                        pages(i) = currentPage
                        used = used + 1
                    End If
                Next i
            Else
                ' Пункт больше 16 строк нельзя поместить целиком ни на одном листе.
                ' Начинаем его с нового листа продолжения и разбиваем БЕЗ потери строк.
                If currentPage = 1 Or used > 0 Then currentPage = currentPage + 1
                used = 0
                splitCount = splitCount + 1
                For i = first To last
                    If Not rows(i).IsSep Then
                        If used >= OKPageCapacity(currentPage) Then
                            currentPage = currentPage + 1
                            used = 0
                        End If
                        pages(i) = currentPage
                        used = used + 1
                    End If
                Next i
            End If
        End If
        priorSeparator = 0
        If rows(last).IsSep Then priorSeparator = last
        first = last + 1
    Loop
    pageCount = currentPage
    For i = 1 To rowCount
        If Not rows(i).IsSep And pages(i) = 0 Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2511, , "Потеря строки при распределении по листам."
    Next i
End Sub

Private Sub PutFirstPageRows(ByRef data() As String, ByRef rows() As VisRow, _
    ByRef pages() As Long, ByVal rowCount As Long)
    Dim i As Long, targetRow As Long
    targetRow = 4
    For i = 1 To rowCount
        If pages(i) = 1 Then
            If targetRow >= OK1_DATA_ROWS Then _
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2512, , "Переполнение ОК1 при заполнении."
            data(targetRow, 0) = rows(i).col0
            data(targetRow, 1) = rows(i).col1
            data(targetRow, 2) = rows(i).Col2
            data(targetRow, 3) = rows(i).Col3
            targetRow = targetRow + 1
        End If
    Next i
End Sub

Private Sub UpdateSeamPhraseInRaw(ByRef col0() As String, ByVal rowCount As Long, ByVal seamRange As String)
    Const TARGET_PHRASE As String = "Отсутствие дефектов сварных швов"
    Dim r As Long, p As Long, tail As String, found As Boolean
    ' ДО WrapTextByWords: диапазон швов тоже участвует в переносе и пагинации.
    For r = 1 To rowCount
        p = InStr(1, col0(r), TARGET_PHRASE, vbTextCompare)
        If p > 0 Then
            found = True
            tail = Mid$(col0(r), p + Len(TARGET_PHRASE))
            col0(r) = Left$(col0(r), p + Len(TARGET_PHRASE) - 1) & ReplaceSeamRangeTail(tail, seamRange)
        End If
    Next r
    If Not found Then AddMacroWarning "Не найдена фраза '" & TARGET_PHRASE & "' в списке операций Excel."
End Sub

Private Function IsOKCardFooter(ByVal paramText As String, ByVal toolText As String, _
    Optional ByVal rowLabel As String = "") As Boolean
    Dim label As Variant, text As String
    If InStr(1, paramText, "Операционная карта контроля", vbTextCompare) > 0 Or _
        InStr(1, toolText, "Операционная карта контроля", vbTextCompare) > 0 Then
        IsOKCardFooter = True
        Exit Function
    End If
    For Each label In Array(paramText, toolText, rowLabel)
        text = LCase$(Trim$(CStr(label)))
        If Right$(text, 1) = ":" Then text = Trim$(Left$(text, Len(text) - 1))
        If text = "итого" Or text = "всего" Or text = "общее время" Then
            IsOKCardFooter = True
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next label
End Function

Private Function ExcelTimeValue(ByVal ws As Object, ByVal r As Long, ByVal c As Long) As Double
    Dim text As String, n As Double
    text = OK1ExcelText(ws, r, c)
    If Len(text) = 0 Or text = "-" Or text = "—" Then Exit Function
    If Not NumericCellValue(text, n) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2405, , _
        "Нечисловое время в " & ws.name & "!" & ws.cells(r, c).address(False, False) & _
        ": '" & text & "'. Укажите число без единиц измерения."
    If n < 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2406, , _
        "Отрицательное время в " & ws.name & "!" & ws.cells(r, c).address(False, False)
    ExcelTimeValue = n
End Function

Private Sub EnsureOK1SheetFromTemplate(ByVal swDraw As Object, ByVal swModel As Object, _
    ByRef insertAfterSheet As String)
    Dim sheet As Object, refProps As Variant
    Dim w As Double, h As Double
    Dim created As Boolean
    Dim anchor As String, newNames(0 To 0) As String
    If Len(Dir$(OK1_TEMPLATE_PATH)) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2414, , _
        "Не найден шаблон ОК1:" & vbCrLf & OK1_TEMPLATE_PATH
    anchor = insertAfterSheet
    Set sheet = swDraw.GetCurrentSheet
    refProps = sheet.GetProperties()
    w = refProps(5)
    h = refProps(6)
    If Not SheetExistsExact(swDraw, "ОК1") Then
        anchor = insertAfterSheet
        If Len(anchor) = 0 Or StrComp(anchor, "ОК1", vbTextCompare) = 0 Then anchor = sheet.GetName
        ' Только версии API, принимающие путь формата, без отката к пустому листу.
        created = TryCreateSheetVersion(swDraw, "ОК1", OK1_TEMPLATE_PATH, w, h, 4)
        If Not created Then created = TryCreateSheetVersion(swDraw, "ОК1", OK1_TEMPLATE_PATH, w, h, 3)
        If Not created Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2431, , "Не создан лист ОК1."
    End If
    If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(swDraw, "ОК1") Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2415, , "Не активирован ОК1."
    Set sheet = swDraw.GetCurrentSheet
    ' Новый лист уже получает формат через NewSheet. Существующий с тем же
    ' форматом также не перезагружаем; при другом пути — одна загрузка с проверкой.
    EnsureMacroSheetFormat sheet, OK1_TEMPLATE_PATH
    ' Перемещаем и существующий ОК1, иначе ОК2 оказались бы сразу после титульного, перед ОК1.
    If Len(anchor) > 0 And StrComp(anchor, "ОК1", vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then
        newNames(0) = "ОК1"
        If Not MoveSheetsAfterTarget(swDraw, anchor, newNames) Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2435, , "Не удалось разместить ОК1 после листа " & anchor
    End If
    insertAfterSheet = "ОК1"
    Set frameAttributeNames = Nothing
    swModel.ClearSelection2 True
End Sub

Private Sub BuildOK1DataTable(ByVal swDraw As Object, ByVal swModel As Object, _
    ByRef data() As String)
    Dim oldTable As Object, oldFeature As Object, tbl As Object, ann As Object, feat As Object
    Dim r As Long, c As Long, merged As Boolean, complete As Boolean, oldRenamed As Boolean
    Dim errorNumber As Long, errorText As String, oldName As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(swDraw, "ОК1") Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2417, , "Не активирован ОК1 перед созданием таблицы."
    Set oldTable = GetTableByName(swDraw, "Таблица данных1")
    swModel.ClearSelection2 True
    Set tbl = swModel.Extension.InsertGeneralTableAnnotation( _
        False, OK1_TABLE_X, OK1_TABLE_Y, 1, "", OK1_DATA_ROWS, NUM_COLS)
    If tbl Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 2418, , "Не создана новая таблица ОК1."
    tbl.Anchored = False
    tbl.SetColumnWidth 0, COL2_W, 0
