    If Len(gKC25TemplatePath) = 0 Then
        candidate = BrowseForFile(app, "Шаблоны таблиц SOLIDWORKS (*.sldtbt),*.sldtbt", _
            "Выберите шаблон шапки с нулевым вертикальным отступом", ParentFolderOfFile(macroPath))
        If Len(candidate) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3360, , "Шаблон шапки не выбран."
        If Not fso.FileExists(candidate) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3361, , "Шаблон не найден: " & candidate
        If LCase$(fso.GetExtensionName(candidate)) <> "sldtbt" Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3362, , "Выберите файл .sldtbt."
        gKC25TemplatePath = candidate
    End If
    KC25_HeaderTemplatePath = gKC25TemplatePath
End Function

Private Function KC25_NewSignTable(ByVal model As Object, ByVal x As Double, ByVal y As Double, _
    ByVal width As Double, ByRef values() As String, ByVal forSurnames As Boolean) As Object
    Dim tbl As Object, ann As Object, fmt As Object, i As Long, ok As Boolean
    Dim errorNumber As Long, detail As String
    Dim actualHeight As Double, actualGap As Double
    Dim templatePath As String, columnToDelete As Long
    Dim rowHeightM As Double
    rowHeightM = KC28_N("Sign.RowHeight")
    Const sizeCanChange As Long = 0 ' swTableRowColChange_TableSizeCanChange
    On Error GoTo Failed
    templatePath = KC25_HeaderTemplatePath()
    ' При непустом пути SW игнорирует Rows/Columns и берёт структуру из шаблона.
    Set tbl = model.Extension.InsertGeneralTableAnnotation(False, x, y, 1, templatePath, 5, 1)
    If tbl Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3320, , "Не создана таблица шапки 1 x 5."
    If tbl.rowCount <> 5 Or (tbl.ColumnCount <> 1 And tbl.ColumnCount <> 2) Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3363, , "В шаблоне нужны 5 строк и 1 либо 2 колонки. Получено: " & _
            CStr(tbl.rowCount) & " строк, " & CStr(tbl.ColumnCount) & " колонок."
    ' Из 5 x 2 оставляем нужную колонку, сохраняя именно её оформление.
    If tbl.ColumnCount = 2 Then
        If forSurnames Then columnToDelete = 0 Else columnToDelete = 1
        If Not tbl.DeleteColumn2(columnToDelete, True) Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3364, , "Не выделена нужная колонка шаблона."
    End If
    If tbl.ColumnCount <> 1 Or tbl.rowCount <> 5 Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3365, , "Не получена таблица 1 x 5."
    tbl.Anchored = False
    tbl.anchorType = 1 ' swBOMConfigurationAnchor_TopLeft
    tbl.SetColumnWidth 0, width, 0
    For i = 0 To 4: tbl.Text2(i, 0, False) = values(i + 1): Next i
    ' Не переустанавливаем шрифт и внутренние отступы: используем оформление .sldtbt.
    For i = 0 To 4
        tbl.CellTextHorizontalJustification(i, 0) = 1
        tbl.CellTextVerticalJustification(i, 0) = swTextAlignmentMiddle
    Next i
    For i = 0 To 4
        actualHeight = tbl.SetRowHeight(i, rowHeightM, sizeCanChange)
    Next i
    ' Разлиновка уже есть в формате листа; вторую сетку поверх неё не рисуем.
    tbl.BorderLineWeight = -1
    tbl.GridLineWeight = -1
    Set ann = tbl.GetAnnotation
    If ann Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3324, , "Нет аннотации шапки."
    If Not ann.SetPosition2(x, y, 0#) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3325, , "Не задана позиция шапки."
    If Abs(tbl.GetColumnWidth(0) - width) > 0.00005 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3326, , "Ширина таблицы шапки отличается от заданной."
    For i = 0 To 4
        If FrameTextComparable(CStr(tbl.Text2(i, 0, False))) <> FrameTextComparable(values(i + 1)) Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3327, , "Не подтверждён текст строки шапки."
        actualHeight = tbl.GetRowHeight(i)
        actualGap = tbl.GetRowVerticalGap(i)
        Set fmt = tbl.GetCellTextFormat(i, 0)
        If Abs(actualHeight - rowHeightM) > 0.00005 Then
            detail = "SW не установил заданную высоту строки шаблона шапки: " & CStr(rowHeightM * 1000#) & " мм." & vbCrLf & _
                "Строка: " & CStr(i + 1) & "; текст: " & values(i + 1) & vbCrLf & _
                "Ширина: " & Format$(tbl.GetColumnWidth(0) * 1000#, "0.000") & " мм" & vbCrLf & _
                "Фактическая высота: " & Format$(actualHeight * 1000#, "0.000") & " мм" & vbCrLf & _
                "Вертикальный отступ: " & Format$(actualGap * 1000#, "0.000") & " мм" & vbCrLf & _
                "Шрифт: " & CStr(fmt.TypeFaceName) & "; размер: " & CStr(fmt.CharHeightInPts) & " пт." & vbCrLf & _
                "Шрифт и отступы не перезаписывались. Шаблон: " & templatePath
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3328, , detail
        End If
        If CLng(fmt.CharHeightInPts) <> CLng(KC28_N("Sign.FontPoints")) Or Not fmt.Italic Or fmt.Bold Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3329, , "Не подтверждён шрифт шапки из Sign.FontPoints: " & KC28_S("Sign.FontPoints") & " пт; требуется наклонный, не полужирный."
        If StrComp(CStr(fmt.TypeFaceName), KC28_S("Sign.FontName"), vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3330, , "Не подтверждён шрифт " & KC28_S("Sign.FontName") & ". Проверьте шаблон и установку шрифта."
    Next i
    Set KC25_NewSignTable = tbl
    Exit Function
Failed:
    errorNumber = Err.number: detail = Err.description
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not tbl Is Nothing Then DeleteFrameDuplicate model, tbl
    On Error GoTo 0
    Err.Raise errorNumber, "KC25_NewSignTable", detail
End Function

Private Sub KC23_PreflightHeader(ByVal model As Object)
    Dim tbl As Object
    KC24_SheetContext model
    Set tbl = KC25_NewSignTable(model, -0.4, -0.4, KC28_N("Sign.RolesWidth"), gKC23Roles, False)
    DeleteFrameDuplicate model, tbl
    Set tbl = KC25_NewSignTable(model, -0.4, -0.4, KC28_N("Sign.NamesWidth"), gKC23Surnames, True)
    DeleteFrameDuplicate model, tbl
End Sub

Private Sub KC23_UpdateSignHeader(ByVal model As Object, ByVal topY As Double)
    Dim previousTables As Collection, previousNotes As Collection, item As Variant
    Dim rolesTable As Object, namesTable As Object, ready As Boolean
    Dim errorNumber As Long, detail As String
    If Not gKC23HeaderReady Then Exit Sub
    On Error GoTo Failed
    KC24_SheetContext model
    Set previousTables = KC23_PreviousSignTables(model, topY)
    Set previousNotes = KC24_OwnedSignNotes(model)
    Set rolesTable = KC25_NewSignTable(model, KC28_N("Sign.RolesX"), topY, KC28_N("Sign.RolesWidth"), gKC23Roles, False)
    MarkFrameTable model, rolesTable, "MF_SIGN_ROLES_V18"
    Set namesTable = KC25_NewSignTable(model, KC28_N("Sign.NamesX"), topY, KC28_N("Sign.NamesWidth"), gKC23Surnames, True)
    MarkFrameTable model, namesTable, "MF_SIGN_NAMES_V18"
    ready = True
    ' Старую шапку удаляем только после проверки обеих новых таблиц.
    For Each item In previousNotes: KC24_DeleteNote model, item: Next item
    For Each item In previousTables: DeleteFrameDuplicate model, item: Next item
    model.SetSaveFlag
    model.ClearSelection2 True
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    errorNumber = Err.number: detail = Err.description
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not ready Then
        If Not rolesTable Is Nothing Then DeleteFrameDuplicate model, rolesTable
        If Not namesTable Is Nothing Then DeleteFrameDuplicate model, namesTable
    End If
    model.ClearSelection2 True
    On Error GoTo 0
    Err.Raise errorNumber, "KC23_UpdateSignHeader", detail
End Sub


' Шаблоны, а не рассчитанные размеры. Числа и допуски заполняются по КД.
' Индексы записаны обычными символами: e1, g1, K1.
' Источники: присланные ГОСТ 23518-79, табл. 2–20;
' ГОСТ 14771-76, табл. 2–47. Параметры подготовки кромок сюда не включаются.
Private Function KC26_WeldTypeKey(ByVal value As String) As String
    Dim text As String
    text = UCase$(Trim$(value))
    text = Replace(text, " ", "")
    text = Replace(text, ChrW(160), "")
    text = Replace(text, vbTab, "")
    ' Латинские визуально похожие буквы приводим к обозначениям ГОСТ.
    text = Replace(text, "C", "С")
    text = Replace(text, "T", "Т")
    text = Replace(text, "H", "Н")
    text = Replace(text, "Y", "У")
    KC26_WeldTypeKey = text
End Function

Private Function KC26_SizeTemplate(w As UnifiedWeld) As String
    Dim joint As String, key As String
    Dim standard14771 As Boolean, standard23518 As Boolean
    joint = KC26_WeldTypeKey(w.weldType)
    standard14771 = (InStr(1, w.WeldStandard, "14771", vbTextCompare) > 0)
    standard23518 = (InStr(1, w.WeldStandard, "23518", vbTextCompare) > 0)
    If standard14771 = standard23518 Then Exit Function
    If standard23518 Then key = "23518|" & joint Else key = "14771|" & joint
    If gKC28Sizes Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3489, , "Шаблоны размеров не загружены."
    If gKC28Sizes.Exists(key) Then KC26_SizeTemplate = CStr(gKC28Sizes(key))
End Function


' v17: первичное применение хранится только в панели Excel, не в имени файла.







' v18: реквизиты изделия в Excel; имя файла больше не источник шапки.







' Единый снимок настроек на запуск. Неизвестные ключи и пропуски — ошибка.
Private Function KC28_S(ByVal key As String) As String
    If gKC28 Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3450, , "Настройки не загружены."
    If Not gKC28.Exists(key) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3451, , "Нет настройки " & key
    KC28_S = CStr(gKC28(key))
End Function

Private Function KC28_N(ByVal key As String) As Double
    KC28_N = CDbl(gKC28(key))
End Function

Private Function KC28_B(ByVal key As String) As Boolean
    KC28_B = (KC28_N(key) <> 0)
End Function

Private Function KC28_LogOn(ByVal category As String) As Boolean
    KC28_LogOn = True
    If gKC28 Is Nothing Then KC28_LogOn = False: Exit Function
    If gKC28.Exists("Service.LogEnabled") Then
        If Not CBool(gKC28("Service.LogEnabled")) Then KC28_LogOn = False: Exit Function
    End If
    If gKC28.Exists(category) Then KC28_LogOn = CBool(gKC28(category))
End Function

Private Function KC28_Number(ByVal text As String) As Double
    Dim re As Object, value As String
    value = Replace(Trim$(text), ",", ".")
    Set re = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")
    re.Pattern = "^[+-]?[0-9]+(\.[0-9]+)?([Ee][+-]?[0-9]+)?$"
    If Not re.Test(value) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3452, , "Ожидается число: " & text
    KC28_Number = val(value)
End Function

Private Sub KC28_Def(ByVal defs As Object, ByVal key As String, ByVal kind As String, ByVal minimum As Double, ByVal maximum As Double, Optional ByVal displayScale As Double = 1, Optional ByVal defaultValue As Variant)
    ' v16.2: defaultValue задан -> строку можно не заводить в Excel (или оставить значение пустым).
    Dim d As Variant
    If IsMissing(defaultValue) Then d = Empty Else d = defaultValue
    defs.Add key, Array(kind, minimum, maximum, displayScale, d)
End Sub

    KC28_Def defs, "Header.EquipmentY", "Double", 0.001, 1000
    KC28_Def defs, "Header.EquipmentW", "Double", 0.001, 1000
    KC28_Def defs, "Header.EquipmentH", "Double", 0.001, 1000
    KC28_Def defs, "Variant.FontPoints", "Long", 1, 72
    KC28_Def defs, "Operations.ShortDimensionLimit", "Double", 0, 100000
    KC28_Def defs, "Operations.DimensionShortTime", "Double", 0, 100000
    KC28_Def defs, "Operations.DimensionLongTime", "Double", 0, 100000
    KC28_Def defs, "Operations.GtolTime", "Double", 0, 100000
    KC28_Def defs, "Operations.OtherTime", "Double", 0, 100000
    KC28_Def defs, "Operations.DefaultVolume", "String", 0, 0
    ' v16.2: необязательные параметры (есть значение по умолчанию).
    KC28_Def defs, "Tools.MaxExtraTools", "Long", 0, 3, 1, 3
    KC28_Def defs, "Tools.AddToSavedBlocks", "Boolean", 0, 1, 1, 1
    KC28_Def defs, "Refs.Enabled", "Boolean", 0, 1, 1, 1
    KC28_Def defs, "Refs.BorderStyle", "Long", -1, 50, 1, 2
    ' v16.5.3: optional settings; old settings books retain safe defaults.
    KC28_Def defs, "Backup.Subfolder", "String", 0, 0, 1, "Резерв КК"
    KC28_Def defs, "Backup.KeepCompleted", "Long", 0, 1000, 1, 5
    KC28_Def defs, "Archive.HideCompleted", "Boolean", 0, 1, 1, 1
    On Error Resume Next
    Set xl = GetObject(, "Excel.Application")
    If Not xl Is Nothing Then
        For Each item In xl.Workbooks
            If StrComp(item.FullName, candidate, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then Set book = item: Exit For
        Next item
    End If
    On Error GoTo Failed
    If book Is Nothing Then
        Set xl = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
        xl.AutomationSecurity = 3: xl.EnableEvents = False
        owned = True
        Set book = xl.Workbooks.Open(candidate, 0, True)
    ElseIf Not book.Saved Then
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3454, , "Сначала сохраните файл настроек."
    End If
    schema = CStr(book.Worksheets("Версия").Range("B1").Value2)
    If schema <> "OK_SETTINGS_V1" And schema <> "OK_SETTINGS_V2" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3455, , "Неподдерживаемая версия настроек."
    Set seen = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): seen.CompareMode = 1
    If schema = "OK_SETTINGS_V1" Then
        pageList = Array("Параметры")
    Else
        pageList = Array("Файлы и папки", "Баллоны", "Нормы контроля", "Шифры", "Подписи", "Сервис и логи", "Таблицы ОК", "Таблицы КЭ", "Рамки ОК", "Рамки КЭ", "?Свои параметры")
    End If
    For Each pageName In pageList
        If Left$(CStr(pageName), 1) = "?" Then
            ' Необязательный лист: нет его или он пуст — не ошибка.
            If Not KC28_HasSheet(book, Mid$(CStr(pageName), 2)) Then GoTo NextPage
            Set ws = book.Worksheets(Mid$(CStr(pageName), 2))
        Else
            Set ws = book.Worksheets(CStr(pageName))
        End If
        last = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, IIf(schema = "OK_SETTINGS_V1", 1, 6)).End(-4162).row
        If last < 2 And Left$(CStr(pageName), 1) = "?" Then GoTo NextPage
        If last < 2 Or last > 5000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3456, , "Некорректный лист настроек: " & ws.name
        values = ws.Range("A2:F" & CStr(last)).Value2
        For r = 1 To UBound(values, 1)
            keyColumn = IIf(schema = "OK_SETTINGS_V1", 1, 6)
            If IsError(values(r, keyColumn)) Or IsError(values(r, 2)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3457, , "Ошибка Excel: " & ws.name & ", строка " & CStr(r + 1)
            key = Trim$(CStr(values(r, keyColumn))): value = Trim$(CStr(values(r, 2)))
            location = ws.name & "!B" & CStr(r + 1)
            If Len(key) = 0 And Len(value) = 0 Then GoTo NextRow   ' v16.2: пустая строка-разделитель
            If Len(key) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3458, , "Пустой служебный ключ: " & location
            If seen.Exists(key) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3460, , "Параметр повторяется: " & location
            If Not defs.Exists(key) Then
                ' v16.2: свой параметр — не ошибка. Хранится как текст, читается KC28_S("ключ").
                seen.Add key, value
                If Left$(key, 5) <> "Свои." And Left$(key, 5) <> "User." Then _
                    gKC28Warn = gKC28Warn & "• " & key & " (" & location & ")" & vbCrLf
                GoTo NextRow
            End If
            spec = defs(key): kind = CStr(spec(0))
            If Len(value) = 0 And Not IsEmpty(spec(4)) Then GoTo NextRow   ' возьмётся значение по умолчанию
            If Len(value) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3461, , "Заполните значение: " & location
            If kind = "String" Then
                If Left$(key, 6) = "Codes." Then
                    If Len(value) <> 5 Or value Like "*[!0-9]*" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3462, , "Шифр должен содержать пять цифр: " & location
                End If
                seen.Add key, value
            Else
                If kind = "Boolean" Then n = KC32_Flag(value) Else n = KC28_Number(value)
                If schema = "OK_SETTINGS_V2" Then n = n * CDbl(spec(3))
                If n < CDbl(spec(1)) Or n > CDbl(spec(2)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3463, , "Значение вне допустимого диапазона: " & location
                If kind = "Long" Or kind = "Boolean" Then
                    If n <> Fix(n) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3464, , "Введите целое значение: " & location
                End If
                seen.Add key, n
            End If
NextRow:
        Next r
NextPage:
    Next pageName
    ' Старый файл сохраняет пользовательские значения; новый параметр имеет совместимый default.
    If schema = "OK_SETTINGS_V1" And Not seen.Exists("Codes.CompactPrefixLength") Then seen.Add "Codes.CompactPrefixLength", 4
    For Each defKey In defs.Keys
        If Not seen.Exists(CStr(defKey)) Then
            spec = defs(CStr(defKey))
            If IsEmpty(spec(4)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3465, , "Пропущен параметр " & CStr(defKey) & ". Восстановите строку из нового файла настроек."
            If CStr(spec(0)) = "String" Then seen.Add CStr(defKey), CStr(spec(4)) Else seen.Add CStr(defKey), CDbl(spec(4))
        End If
    Next defKey
    Set gKC28 = seen
    If schema = "OK_SETTINGS_V1" Then
    Set ws = book.Worksheets("Средства ТО")
    last = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
    If last < 2 Or last > 1000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3466, , "Некорректное число правил средств ТО."
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 1).Value2) <> "Enabled" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Изменён заголовок правил средств ТО: Enabled"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 2).Value2) <> "Type" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Изменён заголовок правил средств ТО: Type"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 3).Value2) <> "StandardContains" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Изменён заголовок правил средств ТО: StandardContains"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 4).Value2) <> "WeldPrefixes" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Изменён заголовок правил средств ТО: WeldPrefixes"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 5).Value2) <> "GdtEquals" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Изменён заголовок правил средств ТО: GdtEquals"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 6).Value2) <> "LegMin" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Изменён заголовок правил средств ТО: LegMin"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 7).Value2) <> "RoundMin" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Изменён заголовок правил средств ТО: RoundMin"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 8).Value2) <> "RoundMax" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Изменён заголовок правил средств ТО: RoundMax"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 9).Value2) <> "RoundedLegs" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Изменён заголовок правил средств ТО: RoundedLegs"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 10).Value2) <> "Parity" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Изменён заголовок правил средств ТО: Parity"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 11).Value2) <> "NominalMin" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Изменён заголовок правил средств ТО: NominalMin"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 12).Value2) <> "NominalMax" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Изменён заголовок правил средств ТО: NominalMax"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 13).Value2) <> "MainTool" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Изменён заголовок правил средств ТО: MainTool"
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 14).Value2) <> "ExtraTool" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Изменён заголовок правил средств ТО: ExtraTool"
    gKC28Rules = KC28_Widen(ws.Range("A2:N" & CStr(last)).Value2)
    Else
        ' v16.2: столбцы ищутся по заголовкам; порядок свободный, лишние столбцы (комментарии) игнорируются,
        ' «Прерывистый шов» и «Текст содержит» — необязательные столбцы.
        Set ws = book.Worksheets("Выбор инструментов")
        gKC28Rules = KC28_ReadRuleTable(ws, "Основное средство", "Дополнительное средство")
        If IsEmpty(gKC28Rules) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3466, , "Лист «Выбор инструментов» пуст."
    End If
    KC28_NormalizeRules gKC28Rules
    KC28_ValidateRules
    ' v16.2: необязательный лист — КАЖДАЯ подходящая строка добавляет своё средство.
    If KC28_HasSheet(book, "Добавочные средства") Then
        gKC28Extra = KC28_ReadRuleTable(book.Worksheets("Добавочные средства"), "Добавить средство", "")
        If Not IsEmpty(gKC28Extra) Then
            KC28_NormalizeRules gKC28Extra
            KC28_ValidateRuleTable gKC28Extra, "Добавочные средства", False
        End If
    End If
    Set ws = book.Worksheets("Шаблоны размеров")
    If (CStr(ws.cells(1, 1).Value2) <> "Стандарт" And CStr(ws.cells(1, 1).Value2) <> "Standard") Or _
       (CStr(ws.cells(1, 2).Value2) <> "Тип шва" And CStr(ws.cells(1, 2).Value2) <> "WeldType") Or _
       (CStr(ws.cells(1, 3).Value2) <> "Надпись в графе Размеры" And CStr(ws.cells(1, 3).Value2) <> "Template") Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3483, , "Изменены заголовки шаблонов размеров."
    last = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
    If last < 2 Or last > 1000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3484, , "Некорректная таблица шаблонов размеров."
    values = ws.Range("A2:C" & CStr(last)).Value2
    Set gKC28Sizes = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): gKC28Sizes.CompareMode = 1
    For r = 1 To UBound(values, 1)
        For c = 1 To 3
            If IsError(values(r, c)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3485, , "Ошибка Excel в шаблоне размеров."
        Next c
        value = Trim$(CStr(values(r, 1)))
        If value <> "14771" And value <> "23518" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3486, , "Standard шаблона: 14771 или 23518."
        key = value & "|" & KC28_WeldKey(CStr(values(r, 2)))
        If Len(KC28_WeldKey(CStr(values(r, 2)))) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3487, , "Пустой тип шва в шаблоне размеров."
        If gKC28Sizes.Exists(key) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3488, , "Повтор шаблона размеров " & key
        value = Replace(CStr(values(r, 3)), Chr(13), "")
        value = Replace(value, "|", Chr(10))
        value = Replace(value, "… ", "")
        value = Replace(value, "…", "")
        gKC28Sizes.Add key, value
    Next r
    If owned Then book.Close False: xl.Quit
    Set book = Nothing: Set xl = Nothing
    OK1_TEMPLATE_PATH = KC28_S("M2.OK1_TEMPLATE_PATH")
    OK1_TABLE_X = KC28_N("M2.OK1_TABLE_X")
    OK1_TABLE_Y = KC28_N("M2.OK1_TABLE_Y")
    OK1_MASS_X = KC28_N("M2.OK1_MASS_X")
    OK1_MASS_Y = KC28_N("M2.OK1_MASS_Y")
    OK1_MASS_W = KC28_N("M2.OK1_MASS_W")
    OK1_MASS_H = KC28_N("M2.OK1_MASS_H")
    MACRO_TEXT_HEIGHT_MM = KC28_N("M2.MACRO_TEXT_HEIGHT_MM")
    FRAME_MATCH_TOL_M = KC28_N("M2.FRAME_MATCH_TOL_M")
    COL2_W = KC28_N("M2.COL2_W")
    COL3_W = KC28_N("M2.COL3_W")
    COL4_W = KC28_N("M2.COL4_W")
    COL5_W = KC28_N("M2.COL5_W")
    ROW_H = KC28_N("M2.ROW_H")
    MAX_ROWS_PER_PAGE = CLng(KC28_N("M2.MAX_ROWS_PER_PAGE"))
    MAX_CHARS_COL0 = CLng(KC28_N("M2.MAX_CHARS_COL0"))
    MAX_CHARS_COL1 = CLng(KC28_N("M2.MAX_CHARS_COL1"))
    DEFAULT_X_MM = KC28_N("M2.DEFAULT_X_MM")
    DEFAULT_Y_MM = KC28_N("M2.DEFAULT_Y_MM")
    HDR1_X = KC28_N("M2.HDR1_X")
    HDR1_Y = KC28_N("M2.HDR1_Y")
    HDR1_COL1_W = KC28_N("M2.HDR1_COL1_W")
    HDR1_COL2_W = KC28_N("M2.HDR1_COL2_W")
    HDR1_ROW_H = KC28_N("M2.HDR1_ROW_H")
    HDR2_X = KC28_N("M2.HDR2_X")
    HDR2_Y = KC28_N("M2.HDR2_Y")
    HDR2_COL1_W = KC28_N("M2.HDR2_COL1_W")
    HDR2_COL2_W = KC28_N("M2.HDR2_COL2_W")
    HDR2_ROW_H = KC28_N("M2.HDR2_ROW_H")
    KE_TEMPLATE_PATH = KC28_S("M2.KE_TEMPLATE_PATH")
    KE1_X1 = KC28_N("M2.KE1_X1")
    KE1_Y1 = KC28_N("M2.KE1_Y1")
    KE1_W1 = KC28_N("M2.KE1_W1")
    KE1_H1 = KC28_N("M2.KE1_H1")
    KE1_X2 = KC28_N("M2.KE1_X2")
    KE1_Y2 = KC28_N("M2.KE1_Y2")
    KE1_W2 = KC28_N("M2.KE1_W2")
    KE1_H2 = KC28_N("M2.KE1_H2")
    KE1_X3 = KC28_N("M2.KE1_X3")
    KE1_Y3 = KC28_N("M2.KE1_Y3")
    KE1_W3 = KC28_N("M2.KE1_W3")
    KE1_H3 = KC28_N("M2.KE1_H3")
    KE1_X4 = KC28_N("M2.KE1_X4")
    KE1_Y4 = KC28_N("M2.KE1_Y4")
    KE1_W4 = KC28_N("M2.KE1_W4")
    KE1_H4 = KC28_N("M2.KE1_H4")
    KE1_X5 = KC28_N("M2.KE1_X5")
    KE1_Y5 = KC28_N("M2.KE1_Y5")
    KE1_W5 = KC28_N("M2.KE1_W5")
    KE1_H5 = KC28_N("M2.KE1_H5")
    KE1_X6 = KC28_N("M2.KE1_X6")
    KE1_Y6 = KC28_N("M2.KE1_Y6")
    KE1_W6 = KC28_N("M2.KE1_W6")
    KE1_H6 = KC28_N("M2.KE1_H6")
    KEOTH_X1 = KC28_N("M2.KEOTH_X1")
    KEOTH_Y1 = KC28_N("M2.KEOTH_Y1")
    KEOTH_W1 = KC28_N("M2.KEOTH_W1")
    KEOTH_H1 = KC28_N("M2.KEOTH_H1")
    KEOTH_X2 = KC28_N("M2.KEOTH_X2")
    KEOTH_Y2 = KC28_N("M2.KEOTH_Y2")
    KEOTH_W2 = KC28_N("M2.KEOTH_W2")
    KEOTH_H2 = KC28_N("M2.KEOTH_H2")
    KEOTH_X3 = KC28_N("M2.KEOTH_X3")
    KEOTH_Y3 = KC28_N("M2.KEOTH_Y3")
    KEOTH_W3 = KC28_N("M2.KEOTH_W3")
    KEOTH_H3 = KC28_N("M2.KEOTH_H3")
    KEOTH_X4 = KC28_N("M2.KEOTH_X4")
    KEOTH_Y4 = KC28_N("M2.KEOTH_Y4")
    KEOTH_W4 = KC28_N("M2.KEOTH_W4")
    KEOTH_H4 = KC28_N("M2.KEOTH_H4")
    DEFAULT_TEMPLATE_PATH = KC28_S("M2.DEFAULT_TEMPLATE_PATH")
    TL_X1 = KC28_N("M2.TL_X1")
    TL_Y1 = KC28_N("M2.TL_Y1")
    TL_W1 = KC28_N("M2.TL_W1")
    TL_H1 = KC28_N("M2.TL_H1")
    TL_X2 = KC28_N("M2.TL_X2")
    TL_Y2 = KC28_N("M2.TL_Y2")
    TL_W2 = KC28_N("M2.TL_W2")
    TL_H2 = KC28_N("M2.TL_H2")
    TL_X3 = KC28_N("M2.TL_X3")
    TL_Y3 = KC28_N("M2.TL_Y3")
    TL_W3 = KC28_N("M2.TL_W3")
    TL_H3 = KC28_N("M2.TL_H3")
    TL_X5 = KC28_N("M2.TL_X5")
    TL_Y5 = KC28_N("M2.TL_Y5")
    TL_W5 = KC28_N("M2.TL_W5")
    TL_H5 = KC28_N("M2.TL_H5")
    TL_X4 = KC28_N("M2.TL_X4")
    TL_Y4 = KC28_N("M2.TL_Y4")
    TL_W4 = KC28_N("M2.TL_W4")
    TL_H4 = KC28_N("M2.TL_H4")
    OK1_X1 = KC28_N("M2.OK1_X1")
    OK1_Y1 = KC28_N("M2.OK1_Y1")
    OK1_W1 = KC28_N("M2.OK1_W1")
    OK1_H1 = KC28_N("M2.OK1_H1")
    OK1_X2 = KC28_N("M2.OK1_X2")
    OK1_Y2 = KC28_N("M2.OK1_Y2")
    OK1_W2 = KC28_N("M2.OK1_W2")
    OK1_H2 = KC28_N("M2.OK1_H2")
    OK1_X3 = KC28_N("M2.OK1_X3")
    OK1_Y3 = KC28_N("M2.OK1_Y3")
    OK1_W3 = KC28_N("M2.OK1_W3")
    OK1_H3 = KC28_N("M2.OK1_H3")
    OK1_X4 = KC28_N("M2.OK1_X4")
    OK1_Y4 = KC28_N("M2.OK1_Y4")
    OK1_W4 = KC28_N("M2.OK1_W4")
    OK1_H4 = KC28_N("M2.OK1_H4")
    OK1_X5 = KC28_N("M2.OK1_X5")
    OK1_Y5 = KC28_N("M2.OK1_Y5")
    OK1_W5 = KC28_N("M2.OK1_W5")
    OK1_H5 = KC28_N("M2.OK1_H5")
    OK1_X6 = KC28_N("M2.OK1_X6")
    OK1_Y6 = KC28_N("M2.OK1_Y6")
    OK1_W6 = KC28_N("M2.OK1_W6")
    OK1_H6 = KC28_N("M2.OK1_H6")
    KE_TABLE_X = KC28_N("M2.KE_TABLE_X")
    KE_TABLE_Y = KC28_N("M2.KE_TABLE_Y")
    KE_ROWS_PER_PAGE = CLng(KC28_N("M2.KE_ROWS_PER_PAGE"))
    KE_HDR_ROW_H = KC28_N("M2.KE_HDR_ROW_H")
    KE_ROW_DATA_H = KC28_N("M2.KE_ROW_DATA_H")
    KE_COL_NUM_W = KC28_N("M2.KE_COL_NUM_W")
    KE_COL_OBZ_W = KC28_N("M2.KE_COL_OBZ_W")
    KE_COL_PIC_W = KC28_N("M2.KE_COL_PIC_W")
    KE_COL_SIZE_W = KC28_N("M2.KE_COL_SIZE_W")
    KE_PIC_FOLDER = KC28_S("M2.KE_PIC_FOLDER")
    KE_PIC_MARGIN = KC28_N("M2.KE_PIC_MARGIN")
    KE_PIC_SIDE_GAP = KC28_N("M2.KE_PIC_SIDE_GAP")
    KE_PIC_VARIANT_GAP = KC28_N("M2.KE_PIC_VARIANT_GAP")
    KE_PIC_CAPTION_H = KC28_N("M2.KE_PIC_CAPTION_H")
    If Len(gKC28Warn) > 0 And gKC28WarnShown <> gKC28Path Then
        gKC28WarnShown = gKC28Path
        TopMsg "В настройках есть неизвестные ключи (проверьте опечатки):" & vbCrLf & Left$(gKC28Warn, 900), "Настройки", MB_ICONINFORMATION
    End If
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    errorText = "Настройки: " & Err.description
    On Error Resume Next
    If owned Then
        If Not book Is Nothing Then book.Close False
        If Not xl Is Nothing Then xl.Quit
    End If
    Set gKC28 = Nothing
    On Error GoTo 0
    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3469, "KC28_Load", errorText
End Sub

Private Sub KC28_ValidateRules()
    KC28_ValidateRuleTable gKC28Rules, "Выбор инструментов", True
End Sub

Private Sub KC28_ValidateRuleTable(ByRef rules As Variant, ByVal label As String, ByVal requireFallback As Boolean)
    Dim re As Object, at As String
    Dim r As Long, c As Long, n As Double, text As String, parts As Variant, p As Variant
    Dim haveFallback As Boolean
    For r = 1 To UBound(rules, 1)
        at = " (" & label & ", строка " & CStr(rules(r, 17)) & ")"
        For c = 1 To 16
            If IsError(rules(r, c)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3470, , "Ошибка Excel в правиле" & at
            If VarType(rules(r, c)) = vbString Then rules(r, c) = Trim$(CStr(rules(r, c)))
        Next c
        n = KC28_Number(CStr(rules(r, 1)))
        If n <> 0 And n <> 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3471, , "В колонке «Использовать» выберите Да или Нет."
        text = CStr(rules(r, 2))
        If InStr("|DIMENSION|GTOL|WELD|NOTE|*|", "|" & text & "|") = 0 Or Len(text) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3472, , "Неверное значение «Что контролируем»" & at
        If Len(CStr(rules(r, 13))) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3473, , "Не задано средство ТО" & at
        For c = 6 To 8
            If Len(CStr(rules(r, c))) > 0 Then
                n = KC28_Number(CStr(rules(r, c)))
                If n < 0 Or n > 100000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3474, , "Неверная граница катета в правиле."
                If c > 6 And n <> Fix(n) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3474, , "Границы округлённого катета должны быть целыми."
            End If
        Next c
        For c = 11 To 12
            If Len(CStr(rules(r, c))) > 0 Then
                n = KC28_Number(CStr(rules(r, c)))
                If n < 0 Or n > 1000000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3474, , "Неверная граница номинала в правиле."
            End If
        Next c
        If Len(CStr(rules(r, 7))) > 0 And Len(CStr(rules(r, 8))) > 0 Then
            If KC28_Number(CStr(rules(r, 7))) > KC28_Number(CStr(rules(r, 8))) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3474, , "Нижняя граница катета больше верхней."
        End If
        If Len(CStr(rules(r, 11))) > 0 And Len(CStr(rules(r, 12))) > 0 Then
            If KC28_Number(CStr(rules(r, 11))) > KC28_Number(CStr(rules(r, 12))) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3474, , "Нижняя граница номинала больше верхней."
        End If
        text = CStr(rules(r, 10))
        If text <> "" And text <> "ODD" And text <> "EVEN" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3475, , "Чётность катета: пусто, Нечётный или Чётный."
        If Len(CStr(rules(r, 9))) > 0 Then
            parts = Split(CStr(rules(r, 9)), ",")
            For Each p In parts
                n = KC28_Number(CStr(p))
                If n < 0 Or n > 100000 Or n <> Fix(n) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3476, , "В списке катетов нужны целые значения через запятую."
            Next p
        End If
        If Len(CStr(rules(r, 16))) > 0 Then
            Set re = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")
            re.Pattern = CStr(rules(r, 16))
            On Error Resume Next
            re.Test "x"
            If Err.number <> 0 Then
                Err.Clear
                On Error GoTo 0
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3479, , "Неверное выражение в «Текст содержит»" & at
            End If
            On Error GoTo 0
        End If
        If CStr(rules(r, 2)) = "*" And CStr(rules(r, 1)) = "1" Then
            text = ""
            For c = 3 To 12: text = text & CStr(rules(r, c)): Next c
            text = text & CStr(rules(r, 15)) & CStr(rules(r, 16))
            If Len(text) = 0 Then haveFallback = True
        End If
    Next r
    If requireFallback And Not haveFallback Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3477, , "Нужно включённое запасное правило «Любой» без условий."
End Sub


' Перепривязка вызывается только если сохранённый/ожидаемый файл отсутствует.
' Никаких операций над аннотациями, баллонами или геометрией в этих процедурах нет.
Private Sub KC30_WriteBinding(ByVal drawing As String, ByVal path As String)
    Dim fso As Object, stream As Object, target As String, temp As String, detail As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    target = drawing & ".okintro-v12.txt": temp = target & ".tmp"
    Set stream = fso.CreateTextFile(temp, True, True)
    stream.WriteLine "OK_INTRO_BINDING_V12"
    stream.WriteLine drawing
    stream.WriteLine path
    stream.Close
    Set stream = fso.OpenTextFile(temp, 1, False, -1)
    If stream.ReadLine <> "OK_INTRO_BINDING_V12" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3500, , "Ошибка записи привязки."
    If StrComp(stream.ReadLine, drawing, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3500, , "Ошибка владельца привязки."
    If StrComp(stream.ReadLine, path, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3500, , "Ошибка пути привязки."
    stream.Close
    fso.CopyFile temp, target, True
    fso.DeleteFile temp
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    detail = Err.description
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not stream Is Nothing Then stream.Close
    If Not fso Is Nothing Then
        If Len(temp) > 0 Then
            If fso.FileExists(temp) Then fso.DeleteFile temp
        End If
    End If
    On Error GoTo 0
    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3501, "KC30_WriteBinding", "Не записана привязка: " & detail & ". Баллоны не изменены."
End Sub

Private Sub KC30_ValidateSource(ByVal book As Object, ByVal drawing As String)
    Dim card As Object, raw As Object, meta As Object, app As Object, model As Object
    Dim sheets As Variant, known As Object, data As Variant, last As Long, r As Long, i As Long
    Set card = book.Worksheets("Карта контроля")
    If Not IsUnifiedCard(card) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3502, , "Не распознана полная карта контроля."
    Set meta = book.Worksheets("ОК_Служебное")
    If Len(Trim$(CStr(meta.Range("B2").Value2))) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3503, , "Выбран чистый шаблон, а не рабочая карта."
    Set raw = book.Worksheets("Данные из чертежа")
    If CStr(raw.cells(1, 1).Value2) <> "№ поз." Or CStr(raw.cells(1, 3).Value2) <> "Текст аннотации" Or _
       CStr(raw.cells(1, 23).Value2) <> "Лист" Or CStr(raw.cells(1, 24).Value2) <> "Чертёжный вид" Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3504, , "Не распознана структура исходных данных карты."
    last = raw.cells(raw.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
    If last < 2 Or last > 100000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3505, , "В карте нет корректного списка параметров."
    Set app = Application.SldWorks: Set model = app.ActiveDoc
    If StrComp(CStr(model.GetPathName), drawing, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3506, , "Активный чертёж сменился."
    sheets = model.GetSheetNames
    Set known = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): known.CompareMode = 1
    For i = LBound(sheets) To UBound(sheets): known(CStr(sheets(i))) = True: Next i
    data = raw.Range("W2:X" & CStr(last)).Value2
    For r = 1 To UBound(data, 1)
        If IsError(data(r, 1)) Or IsError(data(r, 2)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3507, , "Ошибка Excel в исходных данных."
        If Len(Trim$(CStr(data(r, 1)))) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3508, , "Пустое имя исходного листа."
        If Not known.Exists(CStr(data(r, 1))) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3509, , "В текущем чертеже нет исходного листа: " & CStr(data(r, 1)) & ". Требуется отдельный разбор соответствий, привязка не изменена."
    Next r
End Sub

Private Function KC30_RebindMissing(ByVal drawing As String, ByVal expected As String, ByVal required As Boolean) As String
    Dim answer As VbMsgBoxResult, xl As Object, book As Object, sourceBook As Object, picked As Variant, dest As Variant
    Dim fso As Object, oldOwner As String, target As String, meta As Object, item As Variant, text As String
    Dim existingApp As Object, openBook As Object, detail As String, sourcePath As String
    answer = MsgBox("Связанная рабочая карта не найдена:" & vbCrLf & expected & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
        "Да — выбрать существующую .xlsm. Для другого чертежа будет создана отдельная копия; исходная карта не изменится." & vbCrLf & _
        "Нет — новая карта первым макросом." & vbCrLf & "Отмена — остановиться без изменения баллонов.", vbYesNoCancel + vbQuestion, "Карта контроля не найдена")
    If answer = vbCancel Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3510, , "Выбор рабочей карты отменён. Баллоны не изменены."
    If answer = vbNo Then
        If required Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3511, , "Создайте новую карту первым макросом."
        target = KC14_DefaultPath(drawing)
        If CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject").FileExists(target) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3514, , "Новая книга по умолчанию уже существует. Выберите Да и укажите её явно. Перезаписи нет."
        KC30_RebindMissing = target
        Exit Function
    End If
    On Error GoTo Failed
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    Set xl = CreateObject("Excel.Application"): xl.Visible = True
    xl.AutomationSecurity = 3: xl.EnableEvents = False
    picked = KC32_PickCard(xl, drawing, "Выберите существующую полную карту", "*.xlsm")
    If VarType(picked) = vbBoolean Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3510, , "Выбор карты отменён."
    sourcePath = CStr(picked)
    If LCase$(fso.GetExtensionName(sourcePath)) <> "xlsm" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3512, , "Нужна рабочая .xlsm."
    On Error Resume Next
    Set existingApp = GetObject(, "Excel.Application")
    On Error GoTo Failed
    If Not existingApp Is Nothing Then
        For Each openBook In existingApp.Workbooks
            If StrComp(openBook.FullName, sourcePath, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
                If Not openBook.Saved Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3513, , "Сначала сохраните выбранную карту Excel."
            End If
        Next openBook
    End If
    Set sourceBook = xl.Workbooks.Open(sourcePath, 0, True)
    KC30_ValidateSource sourceBook, drawing
    Set meta = sourceBook.Worksheets("ОК_Служебное")
    oldOwner = CStr(meta.Range("B2").Value2)
    If StrComp(oldOwner, drawing, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
        target = sourcePath
        sourceBook.Close False: Set sourceBook = Nothing
    Else
        dest = xl.GetSaveAsFilename(KC14_DefaultPath(drawing), "Excel с макросами (*.xlsm),*.xlsm", , "Независимая копия карты для текущего чертежа")
        If VarType(dest) = vbBoolean Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3510, , "Создание копии отменено."
        target = CStr(dest)
        If LCase$(Right$(target, 5)) <> ".xlsm" Then target = target & ".xlsm"
        If fso.FileExists(target) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3514, , "Файл уже существует. Выберите новое имя, перезапись запрещена."
        sourceBook.SaveCopyAs target
        sourceBook.Close False: Set sourceBook = Nothing
        Set book = xl.Workbooks.Open(target, 0, False)
        If book.ReadOnly Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3515, , "Копия доступна только для чтения."
        Set meta = book.Worksheets("ОК_Служебное")
        meta.Range("A12").Value2 = "CardCopyId"
        meta.Range("B12").Value2 = Format$(Now, "yyyymmddhhnnss") & "_" & fso.GetTempName
        meta.Range("A13").Value2 = "CopiedFrom"
        meta.Range("B13").Value2 = sourcePath
        KC37_InvalidateCopiedLayout book, oldOwner, drawing
        meta.Range("B2").Value2 = drawing
        meta.Range("B10").Value2 = "PENDING"
        book.Save
        book.Close False: Set book = Nothing
        For Each item In Array("Sheets", "SheetsKnown", "Types", "Anchor")
            text = KC14_Setting(oldOwner, CStr(item), "")
            If Len(text) > 0 Then KC14_SaveSetting drawing, CStr(item), text
        Next item
    End If
    xl.Quit: Set xl = Nothing
    KC30_WriteBinding drawing, target
    KC14_Log drawing, "Перепривязка: " & sourcePath & " -> " & target
    If StrComp(oldOwner, drawing, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then _
        MsgBox "Независимая копия карты создана и привязана. Исходная книга и баллоны не изменены." & vbCrLf & _
        "Перед заполнением чертежа выполните режим 1 первого макроса: он сверит параметры, используя существующие баллоны.", vbInformation
    KC30_RebindMissing = target
    Exit Function
Failed:
    detail = Err.description
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not sourceBook Is Nothing Then sourceBook.Close False
    If Not book Is Nothing Then book.Close False
    If Not xl Is Nothing Then xl.Quit
    On Error GoTo 0
    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3516, "KC30_RebindMissing", detail & vbCrLf & "Привязка не подтверждена; баллоны не изменены. Созданная копия, если есть, оставлена на диске."
End Function


Private Function KC28_WeldKey(ByVal value As String) As String
    Dim text As String
    text = UCase$(Trim$(value))
    text = Replace(text, " ", "")
    text = Replace(text, ChrW(160), "")
    text = Replace(text, vbTab, "")
    text = Replace(text, "C", "С")
    text = Replace(text, "T", "Т")
    text = Replace(text, "H", "Н")
    text = Replace(text, "Y", "У")
    KC28_WeldKey = text
End Function


' v19: семантическое чтение старой и новой панели без подмены значений местами.
Private Function KC29_NewFields(ByVal ws As Object) As Boolean
    KC29_NewFields = (KC23_CellText(ws.Range("K6")) = "Первичное применение" And KC23_CellText(ws.Range("H18")) = "Оборудование ОК1")
End Function

Private Function KC29_TextField(ByVal ws As Object, ByVal name As String, ByVal address As String) As String
    Dim nm As Object, area As Object
    On Error Resume Next
    Set nm = ws.Parent.names(name)
    On Error GoTo 0
    If nm Is Nothing Then
        Set area = ws.Range(address)
    Else
        Set area = nm.RefersToRange
        If Not area.Parent Is ws Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3400, , "Диапазон " & name & " относится к другому листу."
        If area.address <> ws.Range(address).address Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3401, , "Диапазон " & name & " должен указывать на " & address & ". Выполните OK_UpdateEditor."
    End If
    If area.MergeCells Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3402, , "Не объединяйте ячейку " & address & "."
    KC29_TextField = KC23_CellText(area)
End Function

Private Function KC27_ReadPrimary(ByVal ws As Object, ByRef value As String) As Boolean
    value = ""
    If Not KC23_NewLayout(ws) Then Exit Function
    If KC29_NewFields(ws) Then
        value = KC29_TextField(ws, "OK_PrimaryApplication", "L6")
    ElseIf KC23_CellText(ws.Range("H18")) = "Первичное применение" Then
        value = KC29_TextField(ws, "OK_PrimaryApplication", "I18")
    Else
        Exit Function
    End If
    KC27_ReadPrimary = True
End Function

Private Function KC29_ReadHeaderData(ByVal ws As Object, ByRef kd As String, ByRef itemName As String, ByRef equipment As String) As Boolean
    kd = "": itemName = "": equipment = ""
    If KC23_CellText(ws.Range("K3")) <> "Реквизиты изделия" Then Exit Function
    kd = KC29_TextField(ws, "OK_KDNumber", "L4")
    itemName = KC29_TextField(ws, "OK_ProductName", "L5")
    If KC29_NewFields(ws) Then
        equipment = KC29_TextField(ws, "OK_Equipment", "I18")
    ElseIf KC23_CellText(ws.Range("K6")) = "Оборудование ОК1" Then
        equipment = KC29_TextField(ws, "OK_Equipment", "L6")
    Else
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3403, , "Не распознана панель реквизитов. Выполните OK_UpdateEditor."
    End If
    KC29_ReadHeaderData = True
End Function

Private Sub KC29_EnsureHeaderData(ByVal ws As Object)
    Dim kd As String, itemName As String, equipment As String, primary As String
    Dim haveProduct As Boolean, havePrimary As Boolean, cell As Object, fresh As Boolean
    ' До первой записи читаем оба старых поля. Повторное обновление не меняет их местами.
    haveProduct = KC29_ReadHeaderData(ws, kd, itemName, equipment)
    havePrimary = KC27_ReadPrimary(ws, primary)
    For Each cell In ws.Range("K3:L6").cells
        If cell.MergeCells Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3404, , "Освободите K3:L6 от объединений перед обновлением панели."
    Next cell
    If ws.Range("H18").MergeCells Or ws.Range("I18").MergeCells Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3404, , "Освободите H18:I18 от объединений."
    If Not haveProduct Then
        If ws.Application.WorksheetFunction.CountA(ws.Range("K3:L6")) > 0 Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3405, , "В K3:L6 есть посторонние данные. Перенесите их до обновления."
    End If
    If Not havePrimary And Not KC29_NewFields(ws) Then
        If ws.Application.WorksheetFunction.CountA(ws.Range("H18:I18")) > 0 Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3406, , "В H18:I18 есть посторонние данные. Перенесите их до обновления."
    End If
    ws.Range("K3").Value2 = "Реквизиты изделия"
    ws.Range("K4").Value2 = "Номер КД"
    ws.Range("K5").Value2 = "Наименование изделия"
    ws.Range("K6").Value2 = "Первичное применение"
    ws.Range("H18").Value2 = "Оборудование ОК1"
    ws.Range("L4:L6").NumberFormat = "@"
    ws.Range("I18").NumberFormat = "@"
    ws.Range("L4").Value2 = kd
    ws.Range("L5").Value2 = itemName
    ws.Range("L6").Value2 = primary
    ws.Range("I18").Value2 = equipment
    KC23_Name ws, "OK_ProductData", "L4:L6"
    KC23_Name ws, "OK_KDNumber", "L4"
    KC23_Name ws, "OK_ProductName", "L5"
    KC23_Name ws, "OK_PrimaryApplication", "L6"
    KC23_Name ws, "OK_Equipment", "I18"
    ws.Columns("K").ColumnWidth = 26
    ws.Columns("L").ColumnWidth = 60
    ws.Range("K3:L3").Interior.Color = RGB(217, 229, 241)
    ws.Range("K3:L3").Font.Bold = True
    ws.Range("K4:L6").Borders.LineStyle = 1
    ws.Range("K4:L6").WrapText = True
    ws.Range("L4:L6").Interior.Color = RGB(255, 242, 204)
    ws.Range("H18").WrapText = True
    With ws.Range("I18")
        .Interior.Color = RGB(255, 242, 204)
        .Font.Bold = False
        .BorderAround LineStyle:=1, Weight:=2
    End With
    If ws.rows(5).RowHeight < 40 Then ws.rows(5).RowHeight = 40
    If ws.rows(18).RowHeight < 30 Then ws.rows(18).RowHeight = 30
End Sub


Private Sub KC31_AddIssue(ByRef issues As String, ByVal text As String, ByRef firstAddress As String, ByVal address As String)
    issues = issues & ChrW(&H2022) & " " & text & vbCrLf
    If Len(firstAddress) = 0 And Len(address) > 0 Then firstAddress = address
End Sub

Private Function KC31_ReadCell(ByVal ws As Object, ByVal address As String, ByVal label As String, ByRef issues As String, ByRef firstAddress As String, ByRef valid As Boolean) As String
    Dim text As String, detail As String
    valid = False
    On Error GoTo Failed
    text = KC23_CellText(ws.Range(address))
    valid = True
    KC31_ReadCell = Trim$(Replace(text, ChrW(160), " "))
    Exit Function
Failed:
    detail = Err.description
    KC31_AddIssue issues, label & " (" & address & "): " & detail, firstAddress, address
End Function

Private Function KC31_ValidateCard(ByVal xl As Object, ByVal book As Object, ByVal ws As Object, ByRef primary As String, ByRef kd As String, ByRef itemName As String) As Boolean
    Dim issues As String, focus As String, raw As String, n As Double, valid As Boolean
    Dim role As String, surname As String, foundDeveloper As Boolean, r As Long, readOnlyText As String
    primary = "": kd = "": itemName = "": gKC29Equipment = ""
    If Not IsUnifiedCard(ws) Then KC31_AddIssue issues, "Не распознана структура карты; обновите её первым макросом.", focus, "L4"
    If Not KC29_NewFields(ws) Then KC31_AddIssue issues, "Обновите Excel-модуль до v19 и выполните OK_UpdateEditor для переноса полей.", focus, "L6"
    raw = KC31_ReadCell(ws, "L4", "Номер КД", issues, focus, valid)
    If valid Then
        kd = NormalizePartDesignation(raw)
        If Len(raw) = 0 Then
            KC31_AddIssue issues, "Не заполнен номер КД (L4).", focus, "L4"
        ElseIf Len(kd) = 0 Then
            KC31_AddIssue issues, "Неверный номер КД (L4). Примеры: D10M.1133.000 или 1211002020.", focus, "L4"
        ElseIf Len(BuildShiftedCode(kd, KC28_S("Codes.20102"))) = 0 Then
            KC31_AddIssue issues, "В номере КД (L4) недостаточно цифр для формирования обозначения.", focus, "L4"
        End If
    End If
    itemName = KC31_ReadCell(ws, "L5", "Наименование изделия", issues, focus, valid)
    If valid And Len(itemName) = 0 Then KC31_AddIssue issues, "Не заполнено наименование изделия (L5).", focus, "L5"
    ' Старую панель не читаем как новую: L6 раньше содержала оборудование.
    If KC29_NewFields(ws) Then
        raw = KC31_ReadCell(ws, "L6", "Первичное применение", issues, focus, valid)
        If valid Then
            primary = NormalizePartDesignation(raw)
            If Len(raw) = 0 Then
                KC31_AddIssue issues, "Не заполнено первичное применение (L6).", focus, "L6"
            ElseIf Len(primary) = 0 Then
                KC31_AddIssue issues, "Неверное первичное применение (L6). Примеры: D10M.00.000-02 или 1211002000.", focus, "L6"
            ElseIf Len(BuildShiftedCode(primary, KC28_S("Codes.02190"))) = 0 Then
                KC31_AddIssue issues, "В первичном применении (L6) недостаточно цифр.", focus, "L6"
            End If
        End If
        gKC29Equipment = KC31_ReadCell(ws, "I18", "Оборудование ОК1", issues, focus, valid)
        ' Пустое оборудование допустимо. Ошибка Excel в нём — не пустое значение.
    End If
    raw = KC31_ReadCell(ws, "I17", "Масса", issues, focus, valid)
    If valid Then
        If Not NumericCellValue(raw, n) Then
            KC31_AddIssue issues, "Масса (I17) должна быть заполнена положительным числом.", focus, "I17"
        ElseIf n <= 0 Then
            KC31_AddIssue issues, "Масса (I17) должна быть больше нуля.", focus, "I17"
        End If
    End If
    For r = 4 To 8
        role = KC31_ReadCell(ws, "H" & CStr(r), "Должность", issues, focus, valid)
        If valid Then
            role = LCase$(Replace(Replace(role, ".", ""), " ", ""))
            If role = "разраб" Then
                foundDeveloper = True
                surname = KC31_ReadCell(ws, "I" & CStr(r), "Фамилия Разраб.", issues, focus, valid)
                If valid And Len(surname) = 0 Then KC31_AddIssue issues, "Не заполнена фамилия напротив «Разраб.» (I" & CStr(r) & ").", focus, "I" & CStr(r)
            End If
        End If
    Next r
    If Not foundDeveloper Then KC31_AddIssue issues, "В H4:H8 не найдена строка «Разраб.» для проверки фамилии.", focus, "H4"
    If Len(issues) = 0 Then KC31_ValidateCard = True: Exit Function
    On Error Resume Next
    xl.Visible = True
    If book.ReadOnly Then book.ChangeFileAccess 2, , False
    If book.ReadOnly Then readOnlyText = vbCrLf & "Книга только для чтения: откройте исходную .xlsm обычным способом для сохранения."
    book.Activate: ws.Activate
    If Len(focus) > 0 Then ws.Range(focus).Select
    On Error GoTo 0
    TopMsg "Исправьте все перечисленные поля:" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & issues & readOnlyText & vbCrLf & _
        "Сохраните книгу и повторите запуск. Чертёж не изменён.", "Проверка карты контроля", MB_ICONEXCLAMATION
End Function


Private Function KC32_PickCard(ByVal xl As Object, ByVal drawing As String, ByVal title As String, ByVal filter As String) As Variant
    Dim dialog As Object, fso As Object, folder As String
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    folder = fso.GetParentFolderName(drawing)
    If Not fso.FolderExists(folder) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3550, , "Папка активного документа недоступна: " & folder
    Set dialog = xl.FileDialog(3)
    With dialog
        .title = title
        .AllowMultiSelect = False
        .filters.Clear
        .filters.Add "Карты контроля Excel", filter
        .InitialFileName = folder & "\"
        KC32_PickCard = False
        If .Show = -1 Then KC32_PickCard = CStr(.SelectedItems(1))
    End With
End Function


Private Function KC32_Flag(ByVal value As String) As Double
    Select Case UCase$(Trim$(value))
        Case "ДА": KC32_Flag = 1
        Case "НЕТ": KC32_Flag = 0
        Case Else: KC32_Flag = KC28_Number(value)
    End Select
End Function

' Убираем только символ-заглушку в e=… не более / e1=… не менее.
' Числовые размеры, ручной текст других видов и изображения остаются прежними.
Private Function KC33_CompactSizeNotation(ByVal text As String) As String
    Dim re As Object
    Set re = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")
    re.Global = True
    re.Pattern = "([eе][0-9]*=)[ ]*(…|\.{3})[ ]*(не[ ]+(более|менее))"
    KC33_CompactSizeNotation = re.Replace(text, "$1$3")
End Function

Private Sub KC33_RefreshSizeNotation(ByVal drawing As Object, ByRef welds() As UnifiedWeld, ByVal count As Long)
    Dim originalSheet As String, table As Object, page As Long, pages As Long, perPage As Long
    Dim firstIdx As Long, lastIdx As Long, rowsData As Long, i As Long, localIdx As Long, row As Long, col As Long
    Dim oldText As String, newText As String, detail As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    If count <= 0 Then Exit Sub
    originalSheet = drawing.GetCurrentSheet.GetName
    perPage = KE_ROWS_PER_PAGE * KE_GROUPS_COUNT
    pages = (count + perPage - 1) \ perPage
    For page = 1 To pages
        firstIdx = (page - 1) * perPage + 1
        lastIdx = firstIdx + perPage - 1
        If lastIdx > count Then lastIdx = count
        If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(drawing, "КЭ (Швы Лист " & CStr(page) & ")") Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3551, , "Не активирован лист для исправления размерной надписи."
        Set table = KE_OneWeldTableOnSheet(drawing)
        rowsData = (lastIdx - firstIdx + 1 + KE_GROUPS_COUNT - 1) \ KE_GROUPS_COUNT
        If table.rowCount <> rowsData + 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3551, , "Изменилась структура таблицы швов."
        For i = firstIdx To lastIdx
            If KE_IsStandardWeld(welds(i)) Then
                localIdx = i - firstIdx
                row = (localIdx Mod rowsData) + 1
                col = (localIdx \ rowsData) * 4
                If KC22_WeldText(CStr(table.Text2(row, col, False))) <> KC22_WeldText(welds(i).NumberLabel) Or _
                   KC22_WeldText(CStr(table.Text2(row, col + 1, False))) <> KC22_WeldText(BuildWeldDesignation(welds(i))) Or _
                   KC22_WeldText(CStr(table.Text2(0, col + 3, False))) <> "Размеры" Then _
                    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3551, , "Не подтверждена ячейка размерной надписи."
                oldText = CStr(table.Text2(row, col + 3, False))
                newText = KC33_CompactSizeNotation(oldText)
                If newText <> oldText Then
                    table.Text2(row, col + 3, False) = newText
                    If CStr(table.Text2(row, col + 3, False)) <> newText Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3551, , "Не подтверждено исправление размерной надписи."
                End If
            End If
        Next i
    Next page
    If Len(originalSheet) > 0 Then drawing.ActivateSheet originalSheet
    Exit Sub
Failed:
    detail = Err.description
    On Error Resume Next
    If Len(originalSheet) > 0 Then drawing.ActivateSheet originalSheet
    On Error GoTo 0
    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3551, "KC33_RefreshSizeNotation", detail
End Sub




Private Function KC28_HasSheet(ByVal book As Object, ByVal sheetName As String) As Boolean
    Dim ws As Object
    For Each ws In book.Worksheets
        If StrComp(Trim$(CStr(ws.name)), sheetName, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then KC28_HasSheet = True: Exit Function
    Next ws
End Function


Private Function KC28_HeaderKey(ByVal value As Variant) As String
    Dim t As String
    If IsError(value) Then Exit Function
    t = LCase$(Trim$(CStr(value)))
    t = Replace(t, "ё", "е")
    t = Replace(t, ChrW(160), " ")
    t = Replace(t, vbCr, " ")
    t = Replace(t, vbLf, " ")
    Do While InStr(t, "  ") > 0: t = Replace(t, "  ", " "): Loop
    KC28_HeaderKey = t
End Function


Private Function KC28_ReadRuleTable(ByVal ws As Object, ByVal toolHeader As String, ByVal extraHeader As String) As Variant
    Dim names As Variant, cols(1 To 16) As Long, lastCol As Long, lastRow As Long, rowLast As Long
    Dim c As Long, k As Long, r As Long, n As Long, h As String, v As Variant
    Dim values As Variant, result As Variant, blank As Boolean
    names = Array("Использовать", "Что контролируем", "ГОСТ содержит", "Типы швов", "Геометрический допуск", _
        "Катет от, мм", "Округлённый катет от", "Округлённый катет до", "Катеты из списка", "Чётность катета", _
        "Номинал от, мм", "Номинал до, мм", toolHeader, extraHeader, "Прерывистый шов", "Текст содержит")
    lastCol = ws.cells(1, ws.Columns.count).End(-4159).Column
    For c = 1 To lastCol
        h = KC28_HeaderKey(ws.cells(1, c).Value2)
        If Len(h) > 0 Then
            For k = 1 To 16
                If Len(CStr(names(k - 1))) > 0 Then
                    If h = KC28_HeaderKey(names(k - 1)) Then
                        If cols(k) > 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Столбец «" & names(k - 1) & "» повторяется на листе " & ws.name
                        cols(k) = c
                    End If
                End If
            Next k
        End If
    Next c
    If cols(1) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Лист «" & ws.name & "»: нет столбца «Использовать»."
    If cols(2) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Лист «" & ws.name & "»: нет столбца «Что контролируем»."
    If cols(13) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3467, , "Лист «" & ws.name & "»: нет столбца «" & toolHeader & "»."
    lastRow = 1
    For k = 1 To 16
        If cols(k) > 0 Then
            rowLast = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, cols(k)).End(-4162).row
            If rowLast > lastRow Then lastRow = rowLast
        End If
    Next k
    If lastRow > 1001 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3466, , "Слишком много правил на листе " & ws.name
    If lastRow < 2 Then KC28_ReadRuleTable = Empty: Exit Function
    values = ws.Range(ws.cells(2, 1), ws.cells(lastRow, lastCol)).Value2
    ReDim result(1 To lastRow - 1, 1 To 17)
    For r = 1 To lastRow - 1
        blank = True
        For k = 1 To 16
            If cols(k) > 0 Then
                v = values(r, cols(k))
                If IsError(v) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3457, , "Ошибка Excel: " & ws.name & ", строка " & CStr(r + 1)
                If Len(Trim$(CStr(v))) > 0 Then blank = False
            End If
        Next k
        If Not blank Then          ' пустые строки-разделители разрешены
            n = n + 1
            For k = 1 To 16
                If cols(k) > 0 Then result(n, k) = Trim$(CStr(values(r, cols(k)))) Else result(n, k) = ""
            Next k
            result(n, 17) = r + 1
        End If
    Next r
    If n = 0 Then KC28_ReadRuleTable = Empty: Exit Function
    KC28_ReadRuleTable = KC28_Shrink(result, n, 17)
End Function


Private Function KC28_Shrink(ByRef src As Variant, ByVal n As Long, ByVal width As Long) As Variant
    Dim result As Variant, r As Long, c As Long
    ReDim result(1 To n, 1 To width)
    For r = 1 To n
        For c = 1 To width: result(r, c) = src(r, c): Next c
    Next r
    KC28_Shrink = result
End Function


Private Function KC28_Widen(ByVal v As Variant) As Variant
    Dim result As Variant, r As Long, c As Long
    ReDim result(1 To UBound(v, 1), 1 To 17)
    For r = 1 To UBound(v, 1)
        For c = 1 To 14: result(r, c) = v(r, c): Next c
        result(r, 15) = "": result(r, 16) = "": result(r, 17) = r + 1
    Next r
    KC28_Widen = result
End Function


Private Sub KC28_NormalizeRules(ByRef rules As Variant)
    Dim r As Long
    For r = 1 To UBound(rules, 1)
        If IsError(rules(r, 1)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3470, , "Ошибка Excel в правиле, строка " & CStr(rules(r, 17))
        If Len(Trim$(CStr(rules(r, 1)))) = 0 Then rules(r, 1) = 0 Else rules(r, 1) = KC32_Flag(CStr(rules(r, 1)))
        Select Case UCase$(Trim$(CStr(rules(r, 2))))
            Case "РАЗМЕР": rules(r, 2) = "DIMENSION"
            Case "ГЕОМЕТРИЧЕСКИЙ ДОПУСК": rules(r, 2) = "GTOL"
            Case "СВАРНОЙ ШОВ": rules(r, 2) = "WELD"
            Case "ЗАМЕТКА": rules(r, 2) = "NOTE"
            Case "ЛЮБОЙ": rules(r, 2) = "*"
        End Select
        Select Case UCase$(Trim$(CStr(rules(r, 10))))
            Case "НЕЧЁТНЫЙ", "НЕЧЕТНЫЙ": rules(r, 10) = "ODD"
            Case "ЧЁТНЫЙ", "ЧЕТНЫЙ": rules(r, 10) = "EVEN"
        End Select
        Select Case UCase$(Trim$(CStr(rules(r, 15))))
            Case "": rules(r, 15) = ""
            Case "ДА", "1": rules(r, 15) = "1"
            Case "НЕТ", "0": rules(r, 15) = "0"
            Case Else: Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3479, , "«Прерывистый шов»: пусто, Да или Нет (строка " & CStr(rules(r, 17)) & ")."
        End Select
    Next r
End Sub


' Ключи текущей и прежней геометрии одного и того же поля.
Private Function KC34_IsKEAlias(ByVal marker As String, ByVal wanted As String) As Boolean
    Dim old1 As String, old2 As String
    Select Case wanted
        Case "Field_KE_NAME": old1 = FrameFieldKey(KE1_X1, KE1_Y1)
        Case "Field_KE_KD": old1 = FrameFieldKey(KE1_X2, KE1_Y2): old2 = FrameFieldKey(KEOTH_X1, KEOTH_Y1)
        Case "Field_KE_PRIMARY": old1 = FrameFieldKey(KE1_X3, KE1_Y3): old2 = FrameFieldKey(KEOTH_X2, KEOTH_Y2)
        Case "Field_KE_CODE": old1 = FrameFieldKey(KE1_X4, KE1_Y4): old2 = FrameFieldKey(KEOTH_X3, KEOTH_Y3)
        Case "Field_KE_TOTAL": old1 = FrameFieldKey(KE1_X5, KE1_Y5)
        Case "Field_KE_PAGE": old1 = FrameFieldKey(KE1_X6, KE1_Y6): old2 = FrameFieldKey(KEOTH_X4, KEOTH_Y4)
        Case Else: Exit Function
    End Select
    KC34_IsKEAlias = (Len(marker) > 0 And (marker = old1 Or marker = old2))
End Function

Private Function KC34_FrameKeyMatches(ByVal marker As String, ByVal wanted As String, _
                                     ByVal xMm As Double, ByVal yMm As Double) As Boolean
    KC34_FrameKeyMatches = (StrComp(marker, wanted, vbBinaryCompare) = 0)
    If KC34_FrameKeyMatches Then Exit Function
    If Left$(wanted, 6) = "Field_" Then KC34_FrameKeyMatches = KC34_IsKEAlias(marker, wanted)
End Function

' Имя MF_<старое имя листа>_<X>_<Y>[_N] переживает переименование листа.
' Не используем только текст ячейки: совпадающий текст не доказывает принадлежность.
Private Function KC34_LegacyFrameName(ByVal name As String, ByVal xMm As Double, ByVal yMm As Double) As Boolean
    Dim re As Object
    Set re = CreateObject("VBScript.RegExp")
    re.IgnoreCase = True
    re.Pattern = "^MF_.+_" & CStr(CLng(xMm * 1000#)) & "_" & CStr(CLng(yMm * 1000#)) & "(_[0-9]+)?$"
    KC34_LegacyFrameName = re.Test(name)
End Function
'

Private Function KC34_StableFrameName(ByVal name As String, ByVal requested As String) As Boolean
    Dim suffix As String
    If Left$(requested, 9) <> "MF_FIELD_" Then Exit Function
    If StrComp(name, requested, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
        KC34_StableFrameName = True
        Exit Function
    End If
    If StrComp(Left$(name, Len(requested) + 1), requested & "_", vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then Exit Function
    suffix = Mid$(name, Len(requested) + 2)
    KC34_StableFrameName = (Len(suffix) > 0 And Not (suffix Like "*[!0-9]*"))
End Function


' v16.5.3-review: вспомогательные процедуры согласованной миграции КЭ.
Private Sub KC35_OpenBook(ByVal path As String, ByRef xl As Object, ByRef book As Object, ByRef owns As Boolean)
    Dim item As Object
    On Error Resume Next
    Set xl = GetObject(, "Excel.Application")
    If Not xl Is Nothing Then
        For Each item In xl.Workbooks
            If StrComp(CStr(item.FullName), path, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then Set book = item: Exit For
        Next item
    End If
    On Error GoTo 0
    If book Is Nothing Then
        Set xl = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
        owns = True
        xl.AutomationSecurity = 3: xl.EnableEvents = False: xl.Visible = False
        Set book = xl.Workbooks.Open(path, 0, False)
    End If
    If book.ReadOnly Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3580, , "Книга открыта только для чтения. Для миграции нужна запись: " & path
    If Not book.Saved Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3580, , "Сначала сохраните пользовательские изменения рабочей книги."
End Sub

Private Function KC35_BackupBook(ByVal book As Object) As String
    Dim path As String, candidate As String, suffix As Long, fso As Object
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    path = CStr(book.FullName)
    candidate = fso.BuildPath(fso.GetParentFolderName(path), fso.GetBaseName(path) & _
        ".before-ke-rename_" & Format$(Now, "yyyymmdd_hhnnss") & ".xlsm")
    Do While fso.FileExists(candidate)
        suffix = suffix + 1
        candidate = fso.BuildPath(fso.GetParentFolderName(path), fso.GetBaseName(path) & _
            ".before-ke-rename_" & Format$(Now, "yyyymmdd_hhnnss") & "_" & CStr(suffix) & ".xlsm")
    Loop
    book.SaveCopyAs candidate
    If Not fso.FileExists(candidate) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3581, , "Не подтверждена резервная копия карты."
    KC35_BackupBook = candidate
End Function

Private Sub KC35_CheckLegacyMarkerFiles(ByVal book As Object, ByVal drawingPath As String)
    Dim ws As Object, fso As Object, suffix As Variant
    Set ws = KC35_Sheet(book, "Маркеры КК")
    If Not ws Is Nothing Then Exit Sub
    Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    For Each suffix In Array("_markers.xlsx", "_hashes.xlsx", "_at_notes.xlsx")
        If fso.FileExists(drawingPath & CStr(suffix)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3582, , _
            "Сначала перенесите прежние маркеры в лист «Маркеры КК» первым макросом. Старые внешние файлы не переписываются."
    Next suffix
End Sub

Private Function KC35_Sheet(ByVal book As Object, ByVal name As String) As Object
    On Error Resume Next
    Set KC35_Sheet = book.Worksheets(name)
End Function

Private Function KC35_GetRef(ByVal model As Object, ByVal ann As Object) As String
    Dim text As String, restored As Object, specific As Object
    On Error GoTo Unsupported
    If ann Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    text = KC40_RawRef(model, ann)
    If Len(text) > 0 Then
        Set restored = KC35_ResolveRef(model, text)
        If KC39_SameObject(restored, ann) Then KC35_GetRef = text: Exit Function
    End If
    Set specific = ann.GetSpecificAnnotation
    text = KC40_RawRef(model, specific)
    If Len(text) = 0 Then Exit Function
    Set restored = KC35_ResolveRef(model, text)
    If KC39_SameObject(restored, ann) Then KC35_GetRef = text
Unsupported:
End Function

Private Function KC35_ResolveRef(ByVal model As Object, ByVal text As String) As Object
    Dim bytes() As Byte, i As Long, state As Long, obj As Object
    On Error GoTo Unresolved
    If Len(text) = 0 Or Len(text) Mod 2 <> 0 Then Exit Function
    ReDim bytes(0 To Len(text) \ 2 - 1)
    For i = 0 To UBound(bytes)
        bytes(i) = CByte(CLng("&H" & Mid$(text, i * 2 + 1, 2)))
    Next i
    Set obj = model.Extension.GetObjectByPersistReference3(bytes, state)
    If state = 0 And Not obj Is Nothing Then Set KC35_ResolveRef = KC40_AsAnnotation(obj)
Unresolved:
End Function

Private Sub KC35_AddSource(ByVal result As Collection, ByVal ann As Object, ByVal kind As Long, _
                          ByVal sheet As Object, ByVal view As Object, ByVal note As Object)
    Dim rec As Object, have As Object
    If ann Is Nothing Then Exit Sub
    For Each rec In result
        Set have = rec("Ann")
        If KC39_SameObject(have, ann) Then
            If StrComp(CStr(rec("OldSheet")), CStr(sheet.GetName), vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then _
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3901, , "Одна аннотация обнаружена на разных листах; перенос заблокирован."
            If Not view Is Nothing Then
                If Len(CStr(rec("OldView"))) > 0 Then
                    If StrComp(CStr(rec("OldView")), CStr(view.GetName2), vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then _
                        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3901, , "Одна аннотация обнаружена в разных видах; владелец не определён."
                End If
                Set rec("View") = view
                rec("OldView") = CStr(view.GetName2)
            End If
            If Not note Is Nothing Then Set rec("Note") = note
            If CLng(rec("Type")) <> kind Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3901, , "Не совпал тип повторной аннотации."
            Exit Sub
        End If
    Next rec
    Set rec = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    rec.Add "Ann", ann: rec.Add "Sheet", sheet: rec.Add "View", view: rec.Add "Note", note
    rec.Add "Type", kind: rec.Add "OldSheet", CStr(sheet.GetName): rec.Add "OldName", Trim$(CStr(ann.GetName))
    rec.Add "OldView", ""
    If Not view Is Nothing Then rec("OldView") = CStr(view.GetName2)
    result.Add rec
End Sub

Private Function KC35_CollectSources(ByVal drawing As Object, ByVal model As Object, ByVal sheets As Collection) As Collection
    Dim result As New Collection, sheet As Variant, sel As Object, obj As Object, ann As Object
    Dim view As Object, dimObj As Object, note As Object, nothingObj As Object, i As Long, kind As Long
    For Each sheet In sheets
        If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(drawing, CStr(sheet.GetName)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3583, , "Не активирован исходный лист."
        model.ClearSelection2 True
        model.Extension.SelectAll
        Set sel = model.SelectionManager
        For i = 1 To sel.GetSelectedObjectCount2(-1)
            kind = sel.GetSelectedObjectType3(i, -1)
            ' Размеры (14) ниже собираются только из видов. Здесь только GDT/сварка.
            If kind = 13 Or kind = 38 Then
                Set obj = sel.GetSelectedObject6(i, -1)
                Set ann = obj.GetAnnotation
                KC35_AddSource result, ann, kind, sheet, nothingObj, nothingObj
            End If
        Next i
        model.ClearSelection2 True
        Set view = drawing.GetFirstView
        Do While Not view Is Nothing
            Set dimObj = view.GetFirstDisplayDimension5
            Do While Not dimObj Is Nothing
                Set ann = dimObj.GetAnnotation
                KC35_AddSource result, ann, 14, sheet, view, nothingObj
                Set dimObj = dimObj.GetNext5
            Loop
            Set note = view.GetFirstNote
            Do While Not note Is Nothing
                Set ann = note.GetAnnotation
                KC35_AddSource result, ann, 15, sheet, view, note
                Set note = note.GetNext
            Loop
            Set view = view.GetNextView
        Loop
    Next sheet
    Set KC35_CollectSources = result
End Function

' Нормализация и хеш совпадают с KC_IdentityRaw/StableHash первого макроса.
Private Function KC35_Normalize(ByVal text As String) As String
    text = LCase$(Trim$(text))
    text = Replace(text, ChrW(216), "d"): text = Replace(text, ChrW(248), "d")
    text = Replace(text, Chr(177), "+-"): text = Replace(text, ",", ".")
    Do While InStr(text, "  ") > 0: text = Replace(text, "  ", " "): Loop
    KC35_Normalize = text
End Function

Private Function KC35_Hash(ByVal text As String) As String
    Dim a As Double, b As Double, code As Long, i As Long
    Const modulus As Double = 2147483647#
    a = 5381#: b = 7#
    For i = 1 To Len(text)
        code = AscW(Mid$(text, i, 1))
        If code < 0 Then code = code + 65536
        a = a * 33# + code: a = a - Int(a / modulus) * modulus
        b = b * 131# + code: b = b - Int(b / modulus) * modulus
    Next i
    KC35_Hash = Right$("00000000" & Hex$(CLng(a)), 8) & Right$("00000000" & Hex$(CLng(b)), 8)
End Function

Private Function KC35_SourceKey(ByVal model As Object, ByVal rec As Object, ByVal old As Boolean, ByVal extended As Boolean) As String
    Dim sheetName As String, sourceName As String, raw As String, ref As String, ann As Object, sheet As Object
    Set ann = rec("Ann"): Set sheet = rec("Sheet")
    If old Then
        sheetName = CStr(rec("OldSheet")): sourceName = CStr(rec("OldName"))
    Else
        sheetName = CStr(sheet.GetName): sourceName = Trim$(CStr(ann.GetName))
    End If
    If Len(sourceName) = 0 Then Exit Function
    raw = "KC_ID2|" & CStr(rec("Type")) & "|" & KC35_Normalize(sheetName) & "|" & LCase$(Trim$(sourceName))
    If extended Then
        ref = KC35_GetRef(model, ann)
        If Len(ref) = 0 Then Exit Function
        raw = raw & "|" & ref
    End If
    KC35_SourceKey = "KC_B2_" & KC35_Hash(raw)
End Function

Private Sub KC35_AddKeyCandidate(ByVal index As Object, ByVal key As String, ByVal rec As Object, ByVal extended As Boolean)
    Dim list As Collection, item As Object
    If Len(key) = 0 Then Exit Sub
    If Not index.Exists(key) Then
        Set list = New Collection
        index.Add key, list
    End If
    Set list = index(key)
    Set item = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    item.Add "Source", rec: item.Add "Extended", extended
    list.Add item
End Sub

Private Function KC35_PlanBalloons(ByVal model As Object, ByVal sources As Collection) As Collection
    Dim result As New Collection, index As Object, used As Object, rec As Object, candidate As Object, plan As Object
    Dim name As String, key As String, note As Object, list As Collection, source As Object
    Set index = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Set used = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    For Each rec In sources
        name = CStr(rec("OldName"))
        If Left$(name, 5) <> "KC_B_" And Left$(name, 6) <> "KC_B2_" Then
            KC35_AddKeyCandidate index, KC35_SourceKey(model, rec, True, False), rec, False
            KC35_AddKeyCandidate index, KC35_SourceKey(model, rec, True, True), rec, True
        End If
    Next rec
    For Each rec In sources
        Set note = rec("Note")
        If Not note Is Nothing Then
            name = CStr(rec("OldName"))
            If Left$(name, 5) = "KC_B_" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3584, , _
                "На листе " & rec("OldSheet") & " остался координатный ключ v15.12. Сначала обновите баллоны режимом 1 первого макроса."
            If Left$(name, 6) = "KC_B2_" Then
                If Not index.Exists(name) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3584, , _
                    "Не найден исходный объект ключа " & name & " на листе " & rec("OldSheet") & ". Переименование заблокировано."
                Set list = index(name)
                If list.count <> 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3584, , "Неоднозначный ключ баллона " & name
                If used.Exists(name) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3584, , "Несколько баллонов используют ключ " & name
                used.Add name, True
                Set candidate = list(1): Set source = candidate("Source")
                If StrComp(CStr(source("OldSheet")), CStr(rec("OldSheet")), vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then _
                    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3584, , "Ключ баллона указывает на исходный объект другого листа."
                Set plan = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
                plan.Add "Ann", rec("Ann"): plan.Add "Source", source
                plan.Add "OldName", name: plan.Add "Extended", CBool(candidate("Extended"))
                plan.Add "NewName", "": plan.Add "TempName", ""
                result.Add plan
            End If
        End If
    Next rec
    Set KC35_PlanBalloons = result
End Function

Private Function KC35_PlanMarkers(ByVal book As Object, ByVal model As Object, ByVal sources As Collection, _
    ByRef oldNames() As String, ByRef newNames() As String, ByVal total As Long) As Collection
    Dim result As New Collection, ws As Object, rec As Object, target As Object, ann As Object, owner As Object
    Dim row As Long, lastRow As Long, savedSheet As String, ref As String, hits As Long, plan As Object
    Dim affected As Boolean, expectedKind As String
    Set ws = KC35_Sheet(book, "Маркеры КК")
    If ws Is Nothing Then Set KC35_PlanMarkers = result: Exit Function
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 1).Value2) <> "KC_MARKERS_1" Or _
       StrComp(CStr(ws.cells(2, 2).Value2), CStr(model.GetPathName), vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3585, , "Неверный формат или владелец листа «Маркеры КК»."
    If CStr(ws.cells(5, 3).Value2) <> "Лист" Or CStr(ws.cells(5, 4).Value2) <> "Вид" Or _
       CStr(ws.cells(5, 5).Value2) <> "Имя" Or CStr(ws.cells(5, 9).Value2) <> "PersistentReferenceHex" Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3585, , "Изменены заголовки листа маркеров."
    lastRow = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
    For row = 6 To lastRow
        savedSheet = CStr(ws.cells(row, 3).Value2)
        affected = (KC21_MappedName(savedSheet, oldNames, newNames, total) <> savedSheet)
        ref = CStr(ws.cells(row, 9).Value2)
        Set target = Nothing: Set owner = Nothing: hits = 0
        If Len(ref) > 0 Then Set target = KC35_ResolveRef(model, ref)
        If Not target Is Nothing Then
            For Each rec In sources
                Set ann = rec("Ann")
                If KC39_SameObject(ann, target) Then hits = hits + 1: Set owner = rec
            Next rec
        End If
        If hits > 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3585, , "Неоднозначный владелец маркера в строке " & row
        If hits = 0 And affected Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3585, , _
            "Маркер, строка " & row & ": нет подтверждённой persistent reference. Переименование не начато; проверьте/пересохраните список на исходных листах."
        If hits = 1 Then
            expectedKind = ""
            If CLng(owner("Type")) = 14 Then expectedKind = "DIM"
            If CLng(owner("Type")) = 15 Then expectedKind = "NOTE"
            If CStr(ws.cells(row, 2).Value2) <> expectedKind Or Len(expectedKind) = 0 Then _
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3585, , "Не совпал тип объекта маркера, строка " & row
            Set ann = owner("View")
            If ann Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3585, , "Не найден вид владельца маркера, строка " & row
            Set plan = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
            plan.Add "Sheet", ws: plan.Add "Row", row: plan.Add "Owner", owner
            result.Add plan
        End If
    Next row
    Set KC35_PlanMarkers = result
End Function

Private Sub KC35_SetAnnName(ByVal ann As Object, ByVal name As String)
    On Error Resume Next
    ann.SetName2 name
    If StrComp(CStr(ann.GetName), name, vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then
        Err.Clear
        ann.SetName name
    End If
    Err.Clear
    On Error GoTo 0
    If StrComp(CStr(ann.GetName), name, vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3586, , "Не подтверждено имя аннотации " & name
End Sub

Private Sub KC35_RekeyBalloons(ByVal model As Object, ByVal plans As Collection)
    Dim plan As Object, source As Object, ann As Object, seen As Object, i As Long, key As String
    Set seen = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    For Each plan In plans
        Set source = plan("Source")
        key = KC35_SourceKey(model, source, False, CBool(plan("Extended")))
        If Len(key) = 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3586, , "Не вычислен новый ключ баллона."
        If seen.Exists(key) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3586, , "Совпали новые ключи разных баллонов."
        seen.Add key, True: plan("NewName") = key
    Next plan
    For Each plan In plans
        i = i + 1: Set ann = plan("Ann")
        plan("TempName") = "KC_RN_" & Format$(Now, "hhnnss") & "_" & CStr(i)
        KC35_SetAnnName ann, CStr(plan("TempName"))
    Next plan
    For Each plan In plans
        Set ann = plan("Ann")
        KC35_SetAnnName ann, CStr(plan("NewName"))
    Next plan
End Sub

Private Function KC35_RestoreBalloonNames(ByVal plans As Collection) As Long
    Dim plan As Object, ann As Object, i As Long, errors As Long
    On Error Resume Next
    For Each plan In plans
        i = i + 1: Set ann = plan("Ann"): Err.Clear
