        KC35_SetAnnName ann, "KC_RB_" & Format$(Now, "hhnnss") & "_" & CStr(i)
        If Err.number <> 0 Then errors = errors + 1
    Next plan
    For Each plan In plans
        Set ann = plan("Ann"): Err.Clear
        KC35_SetAnnName ann, CStr(plan("OldName"))
        If Err.number <> 0 Then errors = errors + 1
    Next plan
    KC35_RestoreBalloonNames = errors
End Function

Private Sub KC35_WriteCell(ByVal cell As Object, ByVal text As String, ByVal changes As Collection)
    Dim rec As Object
    If cell.HasFormula Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3587, , "Формула в служебной ячейке " & cell.address & "; замена запрещена."
    If CStr(cell.Value2) = text Then Exit Sub
    Set rec = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    rec.Add "Cell", cell: rec.Add "Formula", cell.formula
    changes.Add rec
    cell.Value2 = text
    If CStr(cell.Value2) <> text Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3587, , "Не подтверждена запись " & cell.address
End Sub

Private Function KC35_RestoreCells(ByVal changes As Collection) As Long
    Dim i As Long, rec As Object, cell As Object, errors As Long
    On Error Resume Next
    For i = changes.count To 1 Step -1
        Set rec = changes(i): Set cell = rec("Cell"): Err.Clear
        cell.formula = rec("Formula")
        If Err.number <> 0 Then
            errors = errors + 1
        ElseIf CStr(cell.formula) <> CStr(rec("Formula")) Then
            errors = errors + 1
        End If
    Next i
    KC35_RestoreCells = errors
End Function

Private Sub KC35_WriteMarkerOwners(ByVal model As Object, ByVal plans As Collection, ByVal changes As Collection)
    Dim plan As Object, owner As Object, ws As Object, ann As Object, sheet As Object, view As Object
    Dim row As Long, ref As String, restored As Object
    For Each plan In plans
        Set owner = plan("Owner"): Set ws = plan("Sheet"): row = CLng(plan("Row"))
        Set ann = owner("Ann"): Set sheet = owner("Sheet"): Set view = owner("View")
        ref = KC35_GetRef(model, ann)
        If Len(ref) = 0 Then
            ref = CStr(ws.cells(row, 9).Value2)
            Set restored = KC35_ResolveRef(model, ref)
            If restored Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3588, , "Потеряна persistent reference маркера."
            If Not (restored Is ann) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3588, , "Маркер разрешился в другой объект."
        End If
        KC35_WriteCell ws.cells(row, 3), CStr(sheet.GetName), changes
        KC35_WriteCell ws.cells(row, 4), CStr(view.GetName2), changes
        KC35_WriteCell ws.cells(row, 5), CStr(ann.GetName), changes
        KC35_WriteCell ws.cells(row, 9), ref, changes
    Next plan
End Sub

Private Sub KC35_ValidateRaw(ByVal book As Object)
    Dim ws As Object
    Set ws = KC35_Sheet(book, "Данные из чертежа")
    If ws Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3589, , "Нет листа «Данные из чертежа»."
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 23).Value2) <> "Лист" Or CStr(ws.cells(1, 24).Value2) <> "Чертёжный вид" Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3589, , "Изменена схема исходных данных W:X."
End Sub

Private Sub KC35_MapRaw(ByVal book As Object, ByRef oldNames() As String, ByRef newNames() As String, _
                       ByVal total As Long, ByVal changes As Collection)
    Dim ws As Object, row As Long, old As String, mapped As String, oldView As String
    Set ws = book.Worksheets("Данные из чертежа")
    For row = 2 To ws.cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
        old = CStr(ws.cells(row, 23).Value2)
        mapped = KC21_MappedName(old, oldNames, newNames, total)
        If mapped <> old Then
            oldView = CStr(ws.cells(row, 24).Value2)
            KC35_WriteCell ws.cells(row, 23), mapped, changes
            ' Меняем только имя служебного вида листа, а не произвольную подпись вида.
            If oldView = old Then KC35_WriteCell ws.cells(row, 24), mapped, changes
        End If
    Next row
End Sub

Private Sub KC35_Setting(ByVal drawing As String, ByVal key As String, ByVal value As String)
    KC14_SaveSetting drawing, key, value
    If KC14_Setting(drawing, key, ChrW(1)) <> value Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3590, , "Не сохранён параметр " & key
End Sub


' v16.5.3-review: отсутствующие листы и потерянные привязки проверяются ДО SetName.
Private Function KC36_IsRegular(ByVal name As String) As Boolean
    KC36_IsRegular = (RegularKENumber(name) > 0 Or KC21_IsNewKE(name))
End Function

Private Function KC36_CurrentNames(ByVal drawing As Object) As Object
    Dim result As Object, names As Variant, i As Long, first As Long, last As Long
    Set result = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): result.CompareMode = vbTextCompare
    names = drawing.GetSheetNames
    If Not KC15_TryArrayBounds(names, first, last) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3600, , "Нет списка текущих листов."
    For i = first To last: result.Add CStr(names(i)), True: Next i
    Set KC36_CurrentNames = result
End Function

Private Sub KC36_CheckReview(ByVal book As Object)
    Dim ws As Object
    For Each ws In book.Worksheets
        If Left$(CStr(ws.name), 9) = "КЭ_Архив_" Then
            If CStr(ws.Range("A1").Value2) <> "KC_KE_ARCHIVE_1" Then _
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3601, , "Неизвестный формат архива КЭ: " & ws.name
            If CStr(ws.Range("B2").Value2) <> CStr(book.Worksheets("ОК_Служебное").Range("B2").Value2) Then _
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3601, , "Архив относится к другому чертежу: " & ws.name
            If Not KD_CompletedArchive(ws) And UCase$(Trim$(CStr(ws.Range("B4").Value2))) <> "ПРОВЕРЕНО" Then
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3601, , "Удаление КЭ не завершено. Сначала выполните режим 1 первого макроса. Архив: " & ws.name
            End If
        End If
    Next ws
End Sub

Private Sub KC36_AddMissing(ByVal missing As Object, ByVal current As Object, ByVal name As String)
    If KC36_IsRegular(name) And Not current.Exists(name) Then missing(name) = True
End Sub

Private Function KC36_Missing(ByVal book As Object, ByVal current As Object, ByVal known As String) As Object
    Dim result As Object, ws As Object, row As Long, last As Long, count As Long, value As Variant, owner As String
    Set result = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): result.CompareMode = vbTextCompare
    ' Registry SheetsKnown is only advisory. It may belong to a deleted earlier workbook.
    ' Actual historical ownership comes from the CURRENT validated workbook, not the registry.
    Set ws = KC35_Sheet(book, "Данные из чертежа")
    If Not ws Is Nothing Then
        last = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
        If last > 100000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4010, , "Слишком большой список исходных данных."
        For row = 2 To last: KC36_AddMissing result, current, CStr(ws.cells(row, 23).Value2): Next row
    End If
    Set ws = KC35_Sheet(book, "Маркеры КК")
    If Not ws Is Nothing Then
        last = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
        If last > 100000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4010, , "Слишком большой список маркеров."
        For row = 6 To last: KC36_AddMissing result, current, CStr(ws.cells(row, 3).Value2): Next row
    End If
    Set ws = KC35_Sheet(book, "КЭ_Состав")
    If Not ws Is Nothing Then
        owner = CStr(book.Worksheets("ОК_Служебное").Range("B2").Value2)
        If CStr(ws.Range("A1").Value2) <> "KC_KE_LAYOUT_1" Or StrComp(CStr(ws.Range("B2").Value2), owner, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4010, , "Не подтверждён владелец/формат истории состава КЭ."
        If CStr(ws.Range("B3").Value2) <> "NEEDS_MODE1" Then
            value = ws.Range("B4").Value2
            If Not IsNumeric(value) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4010, , "Повреждён счётчик истории КЭ."
            If CDbl(value) < 1 Or CDbl(value) > 100000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4010, , "Некорректный счётчик истории КЭ."
            count = CLng(value)
            If CDbl(value) <> CDbl(count) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4010, , "Счётчик истории КЭ должен быть целым."
            For row = 5 To count + 4
                If RegularKENumber(CStr(ws.cells(row, 1).Value2)) < 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4010, , "Повреждено имя в истории КЭ."
                KC36_AddMissing result, current, CStr(ws.cells(row, 1).Value2)
            Next row
        Else
            If CStr(ws.Range("B4").Value2) <> "0" Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4010, , "Некорректно сброшена история копии карты."
        End If
    End If
    Set KC36_Missing = result
End Function

Private Sub KC36_AddOrphan(ByVal result As Collection, ByVal ws As Object, ByVal row As Long, _
                           ByVal width As Long, ByVal reason As String, ByVal marker As Boolean)
    Dim rec As Object, c As Long
    ' Формулы в очищаемых ячейках нельзя заменять константами даже при откате.
    If marker Then
        For c = 1 To width
            If ws.cells(row, c).HasFormula Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3602, , "Формула в строке маркера " & row
        Next c
    Else
        For c = 23 To 24
            If ws.cells(row, c).HasFormula Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3602, , "Формула W:X в исходной строке " & row
        Next c
    End If
    Set rec = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    rec.Add "Sheet", ws: rec.Add "Row", row: rec.Add "Width", width
    rec.Add "Reason", reason: rec.Add "Marker", marker
    result.Add rec
End Sub

Private Function KC36_PlanMarkers(ByVal book As Object, ByVal model As Object, ByVal sources As Collection, _
    ByVal current As Object, ByVal missing As Object, ByRef oldNames() As String, ByRef newNames() As String, _
    ByVal total As Long, ByVal orphans As Collection, ByVal uncertain As Object) As Collection
    Dim result As New Collection, ws As Object, row As Long, last As Long, savedSheet As String, ref As String
    Dim target As Object, owner As Object, rec As Object, ann As Object, view As Object, plan As Object
    Dim hits As Long, expectedKind As String, affected As Boolean
    Set ws = KC35_Sheet(book, "Маркеры КК")
    If ws Is Nothing Then Set KC36_PlanMarkers = result: Exit Function
    If CStr(ws.cells(1, 1).Value2) <> "KC_MARKERS_1" Or _
       StrComp(CStr(ws.cells(2, 2).Value2), CStr(model.GetPathName), vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3603, , "Не совпал формат/владелец маркеров."
    If CStr(ws.cells(5, 3).Value2) <> "Лист" Or CStr(ws.cells(5, 4).Value2) <> "Вид" Or _
       CStr(ws.cells(5, 5).Value2) <> "Имя" Or CStr(ws.cells(5, 9).Value2) <> "PersistentReferenceHex" Or _
       CStr(ws.cells(5, 10).Value2) <> "#" Or CStr(ws.cells(5, 11).Value2) <> "@" Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3603, , "Повреждены заголовки маркеров."
    last = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
    If last > 100000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3603, , "Слишком много строк маркеров."
    For row = 6 To last
        ' Пусты только полностью исключённые ранее строки A:K; неполные строки не скрываем.
        If book.Application.WorksheetFunction.CountA(ws.Range(ws.cells(row, 1), ws.cells(row, 11))) = 0 Then GoTo NextMarker
        savedSheet = CStr(ws.cells(row, 3).Value2): ref = CStr(ws.cells(row, 9).Value2)
        affected = (KC21_MappedName(savedSheet, oldNames, newNames, total) <> savedSheet)
        Set target = Nothing: Set owner = Nothing: hits = 0
        If Len(ref) > 0 Then Set target = KC35_ResolveRef(model, ref)
        If Not target Is Nothing Then
            For Each rec In sources
                Set ann = rec("Ann")
                If KC39_SameObject(ann, target) Then hits = hits + 1: Set owner = rec
            Next rec
        End If
        If hits > 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3603, , "Неоднозначный владелец строки маркера " & row
        If hits = 1 Then
            expectedKind = ""
            If CLng(owner("Type")) = 14 Then expectedKind = "DIM"
            If CLng(owner("Type")) = 15 Then expectedKind = "NOTE"
            If expectedKind = "" Or CStr(ws.cells(row, 2).Value2) <> expectedKind Then _
                Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3603, , "Не совпал тип владельца маркера " & row
            Set view = owner("View")
            If view Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3603, , "Не определён вид владельца маркера " & row
            Set plan = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
            plan.Add "Sheet", ws: plan.Add "Row", row: plan.Add "Owner", owner
            result.Add plan
        ElseIf Not target Is Nothing Then
            ' Разрешившийся, но не собранный объект НЕ считается удалённым.
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3603, , "Объект маркера существует, но владелец не подтверждён: строка " & row
        ElseIf missing.Exists(savedSheet) Then
            KC36_AddOrphan orphans, ws, row, 11, "Исходное имя листа отсутствует; ссылка не подтверждена: " & savedSheet, True
        ElseIf Len(ref) > 0 And KC36_IsRegular(savedSheet) Then
            ' Это может быть удалённый объект или отказ API. Не утверждаем, что лист удалён.
            KC36_AddOrphan orphans, ws, row, 11, "Persistent reference не разрешается; владелец неизвестен: " & savedSheet, True
            uncertain(savedSheet) = True
        ElseIf affected Or (missing.count > 0 And KC36_IsRegular(savedSheet)) Then
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3603, , "Нет persistent reference у маркера " & row & _
                "; без подтверждённой истории перенос запрещён. Сначала пересохраните список на исходных листах."
        End If
NextMarker:
    Next row
    Set KC36_PlanMarkers = result
End Function

Private Sub KC36_PlanRaw(ByVal book As Object, ByVal missing As Object, ByVal uncertain As Object, ByVal orphans As Collection)
    Dim ws As Object, row As Long, last As Long, name As String
    Set ws = book.Worksheets("Данные из чертежа")
    last = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
    If last > 100000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3604, , "Слишком много исходных строк."
    For row = 2 To last
        name = CStr(ws.cells(row, 23).Value2)
        If missing.Exists(name) Then
            KC36_AddOrphan orphans, ws, row, 26, "Исходное имя листа отсутствует: " & name, False
        ElseIf uncertain.Exists(name) Then
            KC36_AddOrphan orphans, ws, row, 26, "Неоднозначная история владельца; W:X не переносятся по имени: " & name, False
        End If
    Next row
End Sub

Private Function KC36_Selection(ByVal value As String, ByVal current As Object, _
    ByRef oldNames() As String, ByRef newNames() As String, ByVal total As Long) As String
    Dim name As Variant, result As String, used As Object, mapped As String
    If value = "*" Then KC36_Selection = "*": Exit Function
    Set used = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary"): used.CompareMode = vbTextCompare
    For Each name In Split(value, vbLf)
        If current.Exists(CStr(name)) Then
            mapped = KC21_MappedName(CStr(name), oldNames, newNames, total)
            If Not used.Exists(mapped) Then
                If Len(result) > 0 Then result = result & vbLf
                result = result & mapped: used.Add mapped, True
            End If
        End If
    Next name
    KC36_Selection = result
End Function

Private Function KC36_AllKnown(ByVal names As Variant, ByRef oldNames() As String, _
    ByRef newNames() As String, ByVal total As Long) As String
    Dim i As Long, first As Long, last As Long, result As String
    If Not KC15_TryArrayBounds(names, first, last) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3605, , "Нет списка для SheetsKnown."
    For i = first To last
        If i > first Then result = result & vbLf
        result = result & KC21_MappedName(CStr(names(i)), oldNames, newNames, total)
    Next i
    KC36_AllKnown = result
End Function

Private Function KC36_Anchor(ByVal drawing As Object, ByVal current As Object, ByVal old As String) As String
    Dim name As String
    If current.Exists(old) Then KC36_Anchor = old: Exit Function
    If current.Exists("Титульный лист") Then
        name = "Титульный лист"
    ElseIf current.Exists("ОК1") Then
        name = "ОК1"
    Else
        name = CStr(drawing.GetCurrentSheet.GetName)
    End If
    If Not TopYesNo("Прежний ориентир вставки отсутствует: " & old & "." & vbCrLf & _
        "Использовать существующий лист «" & name & "»?", "Ориентир после удаления КЭ") Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3605, , "Новый ориентир не подтверждён. Ничего не изменено."
    KC36_Anchor = name
End Function

Private Sub KC36_ArchiveText(ByVal cell As Object, ByVal text As String)
    ' Даже строка =... сохраняется как текст, а не выполняемая формула.
    cell.NumberFormat = "@": cell.Value2 = "'" & text
    If cell.HasFormula Or CStr(cell.Value2) <> text Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3606, , "Не подтверждён текст архива."
End Sub

Private Sub KC36_ArchiveRow(ByVal archive As Object, ByRef destRow As Long, ByVal source As Object, _
    ByVal sourceRow As Long, ByVal width As Long, ByVal reason As String)
    Dim c As Long, cell As Object, text As String
    For c = 1 To width
        Set cell = source.cells(sourceRow, c)
        KC36_ArchiveText archive.cells(destRow, 1), CStr(source.name)
        KC36_ArchiveText archive.cells(destRow, 2), CStr(sourceRow)
        KC36_ArchiveText archive.cells(destRow, 3), reason
        KC36_ArchiveText archive.cells(destRow, 4), CStr(cell.address(False, False))
        If IsError(cell.Value2) Then text = CStr(cell.text) Else text = CStr(cell.Value2)
        KC36_ArchiveText archive.cells(destRow, 5), text
        If cell.HasFormula Then KC36_ArchiveText archive.cells(destRow, 6), CStr(cell.formula)
        destRow = destRow + 1
    Next c
End Sub

Private Sub KC36_CreateArchive(ByVal book As Object, ByVal model As Object, ByVal missing As Object, _
    ByVal orphans As Collection, ByRef archive As Object)
    Dim name As String, suffix As Long, probe As Object, rec As Object, source As Object
    Dim destRow As Long, row As Long, last As Long, item As Variant, headers As Variant, c As Long
    Dim estimated As Double
    Set source = book.Worksheets("Карта контроля")
    last = LastOKContinuationRow(source)
    estimated = 8# + missing.count + CDbl(last) * 5#
    For Each rec In orphans: estimated = estimated + CDbl(rec("Width")): Next rec
    If last > 100000 Or estimated > source.rows.count Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3607, , "Архив слишком велик."
    name = "КЭ_Архив_" & Format$(Now, "yymmdd_hhnnss")
    Do
        Set probe = KC35_Sheet(book, name)
        If probe Is Nothing Then Exit Do
        suffix = suffix + 1: name = "КЭ_Архив_" & Format$(Now, "yymmdd_hhnnss") & "_" & suffix
        If Len(name) > 31 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3607, , "Не найдено свободное имя архива."
    Loop
    Set archive = book.Worksheets.Add(After:=book.Worksheets(book.Worksheets.count))
    archive.name = name
    KC36_ArchiveText archive.Range("A1"), "KC_KE_ARCHIVE_1"
    KC36_ArchiveText archive.Range("A2"), "Чертёж": KC36_ArchiveText archive.Range("B2"), CStr(model.GetPathName)
    KC36_ArchiveText archive.Range("A3"), "Создан": KC36_ArchiveText archive.Range("B3"), Format$(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd hh:nn:ss")
    KC36_ArchiveText archive.Range("A4"), "Проверка": KC36_ArchiveText archive.Range("B4"), "ТРЕБУЕТ ПРОВЕРКИ"
    KC36_ArchiveText archive.Range("A5"), "После проверки/исправления карты введите ПРОВЕРЕНО в B4 и сохраните книгу."
    KC36_ArchiveText archive.Range("A6"), "Карточные строки НЕ удалены. Значения и формулы ниже — текстовый снимок до изменений."
    headers = Array("Лист книги", "Строка", "Причина", "Ячейка", "Значение", "Формула (текст)")
    For c = 0 To 5: KC36_ArchiveText archive.cells(7, c + 1), CStr(headers(c)): Next c
    destRow = 8
    For Each item In missing.Keys
        KC36_ArchiveText archive.cells(destRow, 1), "Отсутствующее имя листа"
        KC36_ArchiveText archive.cells(destRow, 3), CStr(item): destRow = destRow + 1
    Next item
    For Each rec In orphans
        Set source = rec("Sheet")
        KC36_ArchiveRow archive, destRow, source, CLng(rec("Row")), CLng(rec("Width")), CStr(rec("Reason"))
    Next rec
    Set source = book.Worksheets("Карта контроля")
    last = LastOKContinuationRow(source)
    If last > 100000 Or destRow + last * 5 > archive.rows.count Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3607, , "Архив слишком велик."
    For row = 1 To last
        KC36_ArchiveRow archive, destRow, source, row, 5, "Карта ДО согласования; снимок для ручной сверки, не активные маркеры"
    Next row
    archive.Columns("A:F").ColumnWidth = 24
    archive.Columns("C").ColumnWidth = 55: archive.Columns("E:F").ColumnWidth = 55
    archive.Range("A1:F7").Font.Bold = True
    archive.Range("B4").Interior.Color = RGB(255, 230, 140)
    archive.Range("A7:F" & CStr(destRow - 1)).WrapText = True
    archive.Range("A7:F" & CStr(destRow - 1)).AutoFilter
End Sub

Private Sub KC36_Quarantine(ByVal orphans As Collection, ByVal changes As Collection)
    Dim rec As Object, ws As Object, row As Long, c As Long
    For Each rec In orphans
        Set ws = rec("Sheet"): row = CLng(rec("Row"))
        If CBool(rec("Marker")) Then
            ' Не удаляем Rows: номера прочих строк и формулы карты остаются на месте.
            For c = 1 To 11: KC35_WriteCell ws.cells(row, c), "", changes: Next c
        Else
            ' A:V,Y:Z остаются для сопоставления прежних ручных блоков; только связь W:X исключается.
            KC35_WriteCell ws.cells(row, 23), "", changes
            KC35_WriteCell ws.cells(row, 24), "", changes
        End If
    Next rec
End Sub

Private Function KC36_RemoveArchive(ByVal book As Object, ByVal archive As Object) As Long
    Dim alerts As Boolean
    If archive Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    On Error GoTo Failed
    alerts = book.Application.DisplayAlerts
    book.Application.DisplayAlerts = False
    archive.Delete
    book.Application.DisplayAlerts = alerts
    Exit Function
Failed:
    KC36_RemoveArchive = 1
    On Error Resume Next
    book.Application.DisplayAlerts = alerts
End Function




' v16.5.3-review: КЭ1 — фиксированный обычный лист, новые КЭ допускаются только после него.
Private Sub KC37_AssertKE1(ByVal drawing As Object)
    Dim names As Variant, first As Long, last As Long, i As Long, name As String, found As Boolean, seenRegular As Boolean
    names = drawing.GetSheetNames
    If Not KC15_TryArrayBounds(names, first, last) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3700, , "Нет списка листов."
    For i = first To last
        name = CStr(names(i))
        If RegularKENumber(name) > 0 Or KC21_IsNewKE(name) Then
            If Not seenRegular Then
                If StrComp(name, "КЭ1", vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3700, , _
                    "Первым обычным КЭ должен быть КЭ1. Восстановите его или переместите новый лист после КЭ1. Автозамены КЭ1 нет."
                seenRegular = True
            End If
            If RegularKENumber(name) = 1 Then
                If StrComp(name, "КЭ1", vbBinaryCompare) <> 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3700, , "Неоднозначное имя КЭ1: " & name
                found = True
            End If
        End If
    Next i
    If Not found Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3700, , "Нет обязательного КЭ1. Восстановите лист из копии; другой КЭ не назначается вместо него."
End Sub

Private Function KC37_RegularSnapshot(ByVal drawing As Object) As String
    Dim names As Variant, first As Long, last As Long, i As Long, name As String, result As String
    KC37_AssertKE1 drawing
    names = drawing.GetSheetNames
    If Not KC15_TryArrayBounds(names, first, last) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3701, , "Не прочитан состав КЭ."
    For i = first To last
        name = CStr(names(i))
        If RegularKENumber(name) > 0 Or KC21_IsNewKE(name) Then
            If Len(result) > 0 Then result = result & vbLf
            result = result & name
        End If
    Next i
    KC37_RegularSnapshot = result
End Function

Private Sub KC37_CheckLayoutSlot(ByVal book As Object, ByVal owner As String)
    Dim ws As Object
    Set ws = KC35_Sheet(book, "КЭ_Состав")
    If ws Is Nothing Then Exit Sub
    If CStr(ws.Range("A1").Value2) <> "KC_KE_LAYOUT_1" Or _
       StrComp(CStr(ws.Range("B2").Value2), owner, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3702, , "Лист КЭ_Состав занят другим форматом/владельцем. Автоматическая перезапись запрещена."
End Sub

' Только новая независимая копия книги: прежний состав НЕ считается подтверждённым для другого чертежа.
Private Sub KC37_InvalidateCopiedLayout(ByVal book As Object, ByVal oldOwner As String, ByVal newOwner As String)
    Dim ws As Object
    Set ws = KC35_Sheet(book, "КЭ_Состав")
    If ws Is Nothing Then Exit Sub
    If CStr(ws.Range("A1").Value2) <> "KC_KE_LAYOUT_1" Or _
       StrComp(CStr(ws.Range("B2").Value2), oldOwner, vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3702, , "Не подтверждён владелец состава в копируемой книге."
    ws.Range("B2").Value2 = newOwner
    ws.Range("B3").Value2 = "NEEDS_MODE1"
    ws.Range("B4").Value2 = 0
End Sub


' Только чтение книги и проверка существующих объектов. SetName/Save/архивирование здесь нет.
Private Function KC37_ValidateForFill(ByVal drawing As Object, ByVal model As Object, ByVal book As Object) As String
    Dim oldNames() As String, newNames() As String, total As Long, changed As Long
    Dim ws As Object, count As Long, row As Long, names As Variant, snapshot As String, savedSnapshot As String
    Dim current As Object, sheets As New Collection, sheet As Object, item As Variant, sources As Collection
    Dim rec As Object, ann As Object, target As Object, owner As Object, view As Object, hits As Long
    Dim originalCurrent As String, ref As String, savedSheet As String, kind As String, failure As String
    On Error GoTo Failed
    originalCurrent = CStr(drawing.GetCurrentSheet.GetName)
    KC21_PlanRegularKE drawing, oldNames, newNames, total, changed
    If changed > 0 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3710, , "Есть новые/неупорядоченные КЭ. Сначала выполните режим 1 первого макроса."
    KC36_CheckReview book
    KC37_CheckLayoutSlot book, CStr(model.GetPathName)
    Set ws = KC35_Sheet(book, "КЭ_Состав")
    If ws Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3710, , "Нет подтверждённого состава КЭ. Выполните режим 1 первого макроса v16.5.1."
    If Not IsNumeric(ws.Range("B4").Value2) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3710, , "Повреждён счётчик состава КЭ."
    If CDbl(ws.Range("B4").Value2) < 1 Or CDbl(ws.Range("B4").Value2) > 100000 Then _
        Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3710, , "Некорректное число КЭ в служебном составе."
    count = CLng(ws.Range("B4").Value2)
    If CDbl(ws.Range("B4").Value2) <> CDbl(count) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3710, , "Число КЭ должно быть целым."
    For row = 5 To count + 4
        If row > 5 Then savedSnapshot = savedSnapshot & vbLf
        savedSnapshot = savedSnapshot & CStr(ws.cells(row, 1).Value2)
    Next row
    snapshot = KC37_RegularSnapshot(drawing)
    If savedSnapshot <> snapshot Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3710, , _
        "Состав/порядок КЭ отличается от последнего экспорта. Выполните режим 1 первого макроса. Второй макрос листы не переименовывает."
    Set current = KC36_CurrentNames(drawing)
    KC35_ValidateRaw book
    Set ws = book.Worksheets("Данные из чертежа")
    count = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
    If count > 100000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3710, , "Слишком много исходных строк."
    For row = 2 To count
        savedSheet = CStr(ws.cells(row, 23).Value2)
        If Len(savedSheet) = 0 Or Not current.Exists(savedSheet) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3710, , _
            "Потеряна исходная связь W:X, строка " & row & ". Требуется режим 1 первого макроса и проверка карты."
    Next row
    ' Reference — доказательство владельца; текстовые имена сами по себе им не являются.
    For Each item In current.Keys
        If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(drawing, CStr(item)) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3710, , "Не прочитан лист для проверки владельцев."
        Set sheet = drawing.GetCurrentSheet: sheets.Add sheet
    Next item
    Set sources = KC35_CollectSources(drawing, model, sheets)
    Set ws = KC35_Sheet(book, "Маркеры КК")
    If Not ws Is Nothing Then
        If CStr(ws.cells(1, 1).Value2) <> "KC_MARKERS_1" Or _
           StrComp(CStr(ws.cells(2, 2).Value2), CStr(model.GetPathName), vbTextCompare) <> 0 Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3710, , "Не совпал формат/владелец маркеров."
        If CStr(ws.cells(5, 3).Value2) <> "Лист" Or CStr(ws.cells(5, 9).Value2) <> "PersistentReferenceHex" Then _
            Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3710, , "Повреждены заголовки маркеров."
        count = ws.cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(-4162).row
        If count > 100000 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3710, , "Слишком много строк маркеров."
        For row = 6 To count
            If book.Application.WorksheetFunction.CountA(ws.Range(ws.cells(row, 1), ws.cells(row, 11))) = 0 Then GoTo NextCheckedMarker
            ref = CStr(ws.cells(row, 9).Value2): savedSheet = CStr(ws.cells(row, 3).Value2)
            If Not current.Exists(savedSheet) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3710, , "Нет листа владельца маркера " & row
            If Len(ref) > 0 Then
                Set target = KC35_ResolveRef(model, ref): Set owner = Nothing: hits = 0
                If target Is Nothing Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3710, , "Не разрешилась постоянная ссылка маркера " & row & ". Запустите первый макрос."
                For Each rec In sources
                    Set ann = rec("Ann")
                    If KC39_SameObject(ann, target) Then hits = hits + 1: Set owner = rec
                Next rec
                If hits <> 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3710, , "Не подтверждён единственный владелец маркера " & row
                kind = ""
                If CLng(owner("Type")) = 14 Then kind = "DIM"
                If CLng(owner("Type")) = 15 Then kind = "NOTE"
                If Len(kind) = 0 Or CStr(ws.cells(row, 2).Value2) <> kind Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3710, , "Не совпал тип маркера " & row
                If CStr(owner("OldSheet")) <> savedSheet Or CStr(owner("OldName")) <> CStr(ws.cells(row, 5).Value2) Or _
                   CStr(owner("OldView")) <> CStr(ws.cells(row, 4).Value2) Then _
                    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3710, , "Устарели поля владельца маркера " & row & ". Выполните режим 1 первого макроса KC41.1; для потерянной связи используйте его пункт 4."
            Else
                Set owner = KR_ExactOwner(sources, savedSheet, CStr(ws.cells(row, 4).Value2), _
                    CStr(ws.cells(row, 5).Value2), CStr(ws.cells(row, 2).Value2), hits)
                If hits <> 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3710, , _
                    "Не подтверждён единственный владелец маркера " & row & ". В первом макросе KC41.1 выберите пункт 4 для явной перепривязки, затем выполните режим 1."
            End If
NextCheckedMarker:
        Next row
    End If
    If Not ActivateSheetForMacro(drawing, originalCurrent) Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3710, , "Не восстановлен активный лист после проверки."
    KC37_ValidateForFill = snapshot
    Exit Function
Failed:
    failure = Err.description
    On Error Resume Next
    If Len(originalCurrent) > 0 Then drawing.ActivateSheet originalCurrent
    On Error GoTo 0
    Err.Raise vbObjectError + 3710, "KC37_ValidateForFill", failure & " ОК не обновлены."
End Function


Private Function KC39_SameObject(ByVal leftObj As Object, ByVal rightObj As Object) As Boolean
    Dim app As Object, state As Long, leftSpecific As Object, rightSpecific As Object
    On Error Resume Next
    If leftObj Is Nothing Or rightObj Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    If leftObj Is rightObj Then KC39_SameObject = True: Exit Function
    Err.Clear: Set app = Application.SldWorks: state = 2
    state = CLng(app.IsSame(leftObj, rightObj))
    If Err.number = 0 Then
        If state = 1 Then KC39_SameObject = True: Exit Function
        If state = 0 Then Exit Function
    End If
    Err.Clear
    Set leftSpecific = leftObj.GetSpecificAnnotation
    Set rightSpecific = rightObj.GetSpecificAnnotation
    If Err.number <> 0 Then Exit Function
    If leftSpecific Is Nothing Or rightSpecific Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    If leftSpecific Is rightSpecific Then KC39_SameObject = True: Exit Function
    Err.Clear: state = 2
    state = CLng(app.IsSame(leftSpecific, rightSpecific))
    KC39_SameObject = (Err.number = 0 And state = 1)
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

Private Function KC39_CandidateDetails(ByVal candidates As Collection) As String
    Dim item As Object, source As Object, text As String, i As Long
    For Each item In candidates
        i = i + 1: If i > 8 Then Exit For
        Set source = item("Source")
        text = text & vbCrLf & CStr(i) & ") " & CStr(source("OldSheet")) & " | " & _
            CStr(source("OldView")) & " | " & CStr(source("OldName")) & " | тип " & CStr(source("Type"))
    Next item
    If candidates.count > 8 Then text = text & vbCrLf & "..."
    KC39_CandidateDetails = text
End Function

Private Function KC40_AsAnnotation(ByVal obj As Object) As Object
    Dim ann As Object
    On Error Resume Next
    If obj Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    Err.Clear: Set ann = obj.GetSpecificAnnotation
    If Err.number = 0 Then Set KC40_AsAnnotation = obj: Exit Function
    Err.Clear: Set ann = obj.GetAnnotation
    If Err.number = 0 And Not ann Is Nothing Then Set KC40_AsAnnotation = ann
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

Private Function KC40_RawRef(ByVal model As Object, ByVal obj As Object) As String
    Dim bytes As Variant, i As Long, text As String
    On Error GoTo Unsupported
    If obj Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    bytes = model.Extension.GetPersistReference3(obj)
    If Not IsArray(bytes) Then Exit Function
    For i = LBound(bytes) To UBound(bytes)
        text = text & Right$("0" & Hex$(CLng(bytes(i))), 2)
    Next i
    KC40_RawRef = text
Unsupported:
End Function


' v16.5.1: names are matched only in the OLD namespace, before any sheet rename.
Private Function KR_ExactOwner(ByVal sources As Collection, ByVal sheetName As String, ByVal viewName As String, _
    ByVal objectName As String, ByVal kind As String, ByRef hits As Long) As Object
    Dim rec As Object, found As Object, expected As Long
    hits = 0
    If kind = "DIM" Then expected = 14
    If kind = "NOTE" Then expected = 15
    If expected = 0 Or Len(objectName) = 0 Or Len(sheetName) = 0 Or Len(viewName) = 0 Then Exit Function
    For Each rec In sources
        If CLng(rec("Type")) = expected And CStr(rec("OldSheet")) = sheetName And _
           CStr(rec("OldView")) = viewName And CStr(rec("OldName")) = objectName Then
            hits = hits + 1: Set found = rec
        End If
    Next rec
    If hits = 1 Then Set KR_ExactOwner = found
End Function

Private Function KR_PinnedKey(ByVal plans As Collection, ByVal ann As Object) As String
    Dim plan As Object, source As Object, sourceAnn As Object, hits As Long, value As String
    If plans Is Nothing Or ann Is Nothing Then Exit Function
    For Each plan In plans
        Set source = plan("Source"): Set sourceAnn = source("Ann")
        If KC39_SameObject(sourceAnn, ann) Then
            hits = hits + 1: value = CStr(plan("NewName"))
        End If
    Next plan
    If hits > 1 Then Err.Raise vbObjectError + 4101, , "У источника несколько баллонов; выбор по расстоянию запрещён."
    If hits = 1 Then KR_PinnedKey = value
End Function


Private Function KD_CompletedArchive(ByVal ws As Object) As Boolean
    If CStr(ws.Range("B4").Value2) <> "AUTO_REBUILT" Then Exit Function
    If CStr(ws.Range("H1").Value2) <> "KC_DELETE_REBUILD_1" Then Exit Function
    If Not IsNumeric(ws.Range("H2").Value2) Then Exit Function
    If CDbl(ws.Range("H2").Value2) < 1 Then Exit Function
    If Left$(CStr(ws.Range("I2").Value2), 4) <> "TS: " Then Exit Function
    If Len(CStr(ws.Range("H3").Value2)) = 0 Then Exit Function
    KD_CompletedArchive = True
End Function
